## The audit names a form that posts to this computer · v0.21.0 (2026-11-15)

The audit row for the code `forms.local-address` (added by the backend, see the backend log) is the one finding whose sentence carries a value, the page name, so `auditSite()` in `main.ts` writes it with `label(node, LOCAL_FORM_KEY, [pageName])` instead of the engine's English `message`: the page translator matches whole English strings and cannot match a sentence with a name in it, and `label()` records the English source so a live language switch re-translates the row. Every other finding still goes through `text()`, which replaces the text node and so drops any earlier record when `keyed()` reuses a row. The key is one whole sentence with `{0}`, appended to `i18n/keys.ts` and to the 23 locale arrays by hand; each translation names the Motion tab, the Script section, its Trigger field, the Submit trigger and Publish with that language's own words for them, read from the same arrays. Select on the row focuses the form, because the finding carries the form's node id. The wording names only what the editor offers: a script with the Submit trigger, or Publish, which serves the site from this computer; the Content tab has no field for a form's own `action`, so the sentence does not send people there. Checked: a sample site with Insert > Form shows the row in English and in German at 1440 x 900, CPU 4x, console clean, and Select lands on "Contact form"; the starter and the three demos opened from Home show no such row; `./formweb i18n check` 2564 keys in 23 languages; `npx tsc --noEmit`. Known: the status line after Export static site and Send to FormPublisher still mentions only the 404 base URL warning, by the owner's decision to leave export as it is.

## Demos on Home and in Help, with a Preview of the finished sites · v0.21.0 (2026-11-15)

Commit 1c69024, records in 4346423. `ui/demos.ts` holds the list (`DEMOS`: Lowlight, Looking Back, Harbour Ferries), one command per demo (`demo.<id>`) plus `demo.preview` and `demo.preview-close`, and `openDemo()`, which asks Home's question when the open site has unsaved work, fetches `demos/<id>/site.fweb` and loads it like File > Import, so a demo is always a new, unsaved site and no saved project changes. The files are `frontend/public/demos/<id>/site.fweb` and `cover.webp` (640 x 360); the one-line descriptions are language keys, the titles are proper names and stay as they are. Help > Demos is a real submenu: a command may name a `submenu` (`commands/registry.ts`), `ui/menus.ts` groups those rows, and the window bar, the folded More menu and the macOS menu bar all draw the same nested menu. `ui/demo-preview.ts` shows `demos/preview.html`, one self-contained file packed from the three exported sites, in a full-window overlay; "Open this demo" opens the site on show for editing. Why the frame has `allow-same-origin` in its sandbox: the packed shell writes each page into a child frame with `document.write`, and in an opaque-origin sandbox every frame gets its own origin, so the shell stops with "Blocked a frame with origin null from accessing a cross-origin frame"; serving the file from the other loopback name instead is refused by the engine's cross-site rule. The content is our own file, there is no `allow-top-navigation` so the frame cannot move the app window, and Chrome's one console warning per open (a frame with both `allow-scripts` and `allow-same-origin` can escape its sandbox) is true and accepted. While the overlay is open every other child of `body` is `inert`, `commands.gate` lets only `demo.*` commands run, the menubar is blocked and a capture listener keeps keys from the editor; Escape is heard inside the frame's window and each page frame (hooked again on every page load, because `document.open()` erases listeners) and does nothing while the page has an open `dialog`, so the first Escape closes a lightbox picture and the second the preview. Why the demos' forms are scripted: a form that posts gets the local engine's address as its action on a plain export, which is dead on any other host and would put `127.0.0.1` into `preview.html`; the Lowlight booking form and the Harbour Ferries contact form answer on the page through a submit script instead, so the three `.fweb` files, their exports and the packed preview hold no local address and need no backend. Checked (the bundle task's `verify.py`): 44 of 44 in English and 45 of 45 in German and Japanese at 1440 x 900 and 1000 x 700 with CPU 4x and a slow network; layout shift 0 at load, on Home, in the menus and in every preview step; no demo line clipped in 26 languages at three sizes; the native menu tree through a stand-in for the Tauri bridge. Known: with a slow network a demo takes 60 to 80 s to open (the 3.6 MB project is fetched, then posted to `/api/load`); the native menu was not run on macOS.

## Review fixes in the editor and the desktop shell · v0.21.0 (2026-11-15)

Commit 8aabcb4, editor and shell part. `ui/canvas.ts`: the six SVG shape tags the model allows (`rect`, `circle`, `ellipse`, `line`, `polyline`, `polygon`) and the attributes `points`, `rx`, `ry` were missing from the canvas lists, so a circle became a `div` and was not drawn. `features/inspector/fonts.ts`: the canvas wrote each face with the site's own `font-display`; with `optional` a font that arrives after the first hundred milliseconds is never used in that document, so site fonts did not show; the canvas now always writes `swap` (the exported site keeps the author's value). `ui/shell.ts` `place()`: a bar menu taller than the window was moved up over the bar, so a second click on its button could not close it; a bar menu is now capped to the room below its button and scrolls, a submenu beside the More menu keeps the old placement. `main.ts`: Reset All Preferences removed only keys starting `formweb.`; it now also removes the theme (`nodemode_ui_settings`, `formweb_theme`), language, shortcuts, workspace layout, terminal state and the pathfinder options, and keeps saved width profiles because they are things the person made. The New site dialog, Open project (the dialog and Home's cards) and Import close their dialog and then ask Home's question (`mayReplace`, `ui/unsaved.ts`) before the open site is replaced. `styles/app.css`: the stage header row is clipped (`min-width: 0; overflow: hidden`), because the scene scrubber does not shrink and painted over the inspector's head at 1000 px. `ui/opened-file.ts` (new) with `src-tauri/src/main.rs`: the shell keeps a project file from the launch argument (Windows, Linux) or `RunEvent::Opened` (macOS) in a list, the command `take_opened_file` hands the page its bytes as an ArrayBuffer, and the page loads them through the same question and loader as Import; the page asks once after it connects and again on the window event `formweb-open-file`. `main.rs` also sets a download handler on macOS and Linux (their web views cancel a download without one, so Export and Download project did nothing; with one the file goes to Downloads) and sends new-window links to the system browser. `build_app`: a signed build stops when `codesign --verify --deep --strict` fails, and the frozen engine's `--selfcheck` runs inside the finished app before the disk image is made. Checked: canvas circles 0 to 19 and Oswald drawn on the Harbour Ferries site; the Vector menu from 38 to 896 of 900 px and closed by a second click, also at 640 px height; the question in the three places with Cancel and Replace; reset from a German light setup back to dark English; a session at 1440 x 900, 1000 x 700 (German, light) and 760 x 640 with CPU 4x, no console error and no failed request; `cargo check` of the shell for Linux and for macOS, both clean. Known: file opening, downloads and links in the shell are type-checked only, never run on macOS or Windows; a file opened while the app runs starts a second instance on Windows and Linux; the stage header readout is still cut at 1440 px with a scene site; a look for the chosen filter chip needs a new behaviour state and was left. The line "Blocked script execution in 'about:srcdoc'" seen in earlier runs comes from the test harness's injected script, not from the canvas: with no injected script a canvas resize logs nothing.

## Home on formdeck's structure · v0.21.0 (2026-11-15)

Commit eb576b4. `ui/home.ts`, `ui/home-files.ts`, `ui/unsaved.ts`, the Home block of `styles/shell.css`, and `index.html` (a `link rel="icon"`, which ends the favicon 404 on every load). Home keeps one mounted tree: a navigation column (Start, Recent, Projects, the Create actions, Back to Editor, Preferences, Help) that can be resized and hidden (`formweb.homeNav`, `formweb.homeNavHidden`), and the pages beside it. Recent is the newest twelve saved projects (`RECENT_MAX`), Projects is all of them, so the two counts differ. The Demos section is drawn from `DEMOS` and is absent while the list is empty. A template tile, and later a demo, asks before it replaces a site with changes that are not saved as a project: `unsavedWork()` is true when the engine can undo and the open project is not among the saved ones, and the question is a reserved row inside Home (`askReplace`), not a browser dialog, so it is translated and moves nothing. Escape leaves Home only when no menu, palette or dialog is open, which ends the two cases where one Escape closed a menu or Preferences and Home together. Every label is a key in all languages; text that changes length with the language has reserved room (the hero paragraph by a container query on its column, the hero buttons 268 px, the section titles and the sort label by minimum widths), measured from the longest translation. Checked (the Home task's `probe.py`, `rundiff.py`, `verify.py`): no layout-shift entry on load or first show at 1440 x 900, 1000 x 700 and 760 x 640 in English, German and Russian, with no project and with fifteen; a live switch through all 26 languages moves no element of Home; 42 behaviour checks. Known: the top bar's own tools stay live while Home covers the editor; cards show a globe, not a thumbnail.

## One menu model for the window and the macOS menu bar · v0.21.0 (2026-11-15)

Commit 2329301. `ui/menus.ts` (new) builds the menu model from the command registry once: `MENU_LABELS` is the only list of top-level menus (File, Edit, View, Insert, Page, Arrange, Vector, CMS, Window, Help, with View third as in formvis), and `menuModel()` gives each menu its rows and separators. `Menubar` (`ui/shell.ts`) and `NativeMenus` (`ui/native-menu.ts`) both read that model, so the two can no longer drift and a new menu is one entry. The native menu writes every label through `t()` and is built again on `onLanguage`, so the macOS menu bar is translated and follows a live switch. New in the table: `file.home` ("Home", first in File, Alt+Mod+H), which is also what the palette finds, and Mod+comma on `edit.settings`. The bar works from the keyboard: F10 or a lone Alt enters it and underlines the first letters, Alt+letter opens a menu, arrows, Home, End and typing a letter move, Escape returns focus to where it was; `menubar.blocked` keeps the bar shut while the dialog or the studio is open. Checked (the menubar task's `keys.py`, `items.py`, `shift.py`): 77 to 85 checks per run at three sizes in English, German and Russian; the disabled and checked state of every row equals the registry, and 116 rows were clicked; the bar buttons keep their rectangles. Known: the native menu was exercised through a stand-in for the Tauri bridge, not on macOS; a live language switch still moves inspector fields and the title tools, as before this change.

## Splash page, entitlements and the app's name · v0.21.0 (2026-11-15)

Commits 93494d9, a73fdf7 and 4a07f11. `frontend/public/splash.html` is formvis's page line for line with FormWeb's icon and name (the two style rules and the image tag lost what formvis does not have), so the family's splash hand-off behaves the same in every app. `src-tauri/entitlements.plist` and the `bundle.macOS` block of `tauri.conf.json` (`hardenedRuntime`, `entitlements`, `minimumSystemVersion` 12.0): the frozen engine is one PyInstaller file whose inner libraries are signed ad hoc; under a Developer ID signature with the hardened runtime, library validation refuses to load them because their Team ID differs, and the app would show its splash and then "The FormWeb service did not answer within 60 seconds". `disable-library-validation` lets the engine load its own libraries and `allow-unsigned-executable-memory` is what formdeck, which ships the same one-file layout, sets beside it. `build_app` names the macOS app, its disk image, the volume and the installed copy FormWeb, after the product name, where it wrote formweb. Checked: the window smoke test (splash and editor, zero layout shift, no console error); both shell type checks. Known: no signed build was made or opened here; whether the bundler passes the entitlements to the sidecar rests on formdeck doing the same.

## A language switch that keeps Preferences and the inspector still · v0.20.8 (2026-11-11)

Phase 57 (commit 99d4587). Layout-shift sources during live switches (en>de, de>ru, ru>ja, ja>en, en>ru with an element selected and Preferences open, CPU 4x) had four causes. Japanese, Chinese, Korean and Thai fell back to system fonts whose line boxes are 2 to 5 px taller than Inter's under `line-height: normal`, so Preferences reflowed: `styles/fonts.css` declares local faces `FW Han JP`, `FW Han SC`, `FW Han TC`, `FW Hangul` and `FW Thai` (regular and bold, `unicode-range` limited to their scripts) with Inter's `ascent-override: 96.875%`, `descent-override: 24.12%` and `line-gap-override: 0%`, and `styles/tokens.css` puts `--font-script` after Inter in `--font-ui`, choosing the Han face by `:root:lang(zh-CN)`, `zh-TW` and `ko`; Latin text never uses them. The inspector went to two tab rows (`fw-tabs2`) and one field column (`fw-fields1`) in German, Russian and other languages where English did not: `.inspector-tabs button` pads 2 px (`styles/app.css`), `.field-label` wraps and `.fw-reset` takes `margin-left: auto` so a long Reset pill wraps under its label (`styles/shell.css`), and `ui/fit.ts` drops the Reset width from the column test and keeps English's old thresholds (`ENGLISH_FIELD_NEED = 104.32`, `ENGLISH_TABS_NEED = 242.98`) as minimums for every language, so English flips at the same widths as 0.20.7. CLS per switch at 1440 went from 0.024, 0.021, 0.071, 0.085 and 0.035 to 0.013, 0.004, 0.028, 0.021 and 0.017; the Japanese steps at 1100 and 760 from 0.07 to 0.11 to 0.01 to 0.02; opening the editor stays at 0. English element boxes are identical at 1440, 1100 and 760 except the four inspector tabs at 1440, now equal 65.3 px columns. Known: the rest is text-width reflow (menu buttons, the top actions group pushing the title tools, Export and Audit, the dialog's Done and Save, the status message, segmented buttons wrapping in German, Japanese fields in one column), which only a reserved width would remove, and that changes English; the fallback face names were verified on Linux only.

## Command palette in the current language · v0.20.8 (2026-11-11)

Phase 56 (commit a1eab2e). `CommandSearch.refresh()` in `ui/shell.ts` folds the query and each command's text with `fold()` (NFD, combining marks removed, lower case), so case and accents are ignored, and matches every query word against the menu name in the current language and in English, the label in both (`t(commands.label(item))` and `commands.label(item)`) and the id. Outside English, results whose label starts with the whole query in either language come first, then the rest in catalogue order, cut to 12; in English the order is the catalogue order as before, and English result sets for the same queries are identical to 0.20.7. Rows show `t(menu) › t(label)`; the keyed row markup is unchanged. `onLanguage()` rebuilds the list while the palette is open, and a closed palette refreshes on its next `show()`. No new strings. Checked: 26 English queries unchanged; German, Russian, Japanese and French by translated prefixes, inner words, unaccented forms, English names and ids; live switches with the palette open and closed; CLS 0 while typing with the CPU throttled 4x. Known: a command whose label is registered already translated (a `t()` at registration) matches only by its current-language text and id; "Align horizontal centers" and other composed labels stay English in every language.

## Labels carry their English source · v0.20.7 (2026-11-11)

Phase 52 (commit bd640f6). `i18n/index.ts` gains `label(node, english, values?)`, `labelText(textNode, ...)` and `labelAttr(el, name, ...)`: each sets the text or attribute through `t()` and writes `{en, shown, redo}` into the page translator's per-node record (`textEn`, `attrEn`), in English too, before any observer pass. The translator honours a record while the node still shows `rec.shown` and never consults the reverse map for it, so a string two keys share (such as "Heading" in Russian, or "Preset" in Italian) can no longer go stale; `label()` records only when the node holds exactly one text node, and `labelText()` is for a text node beside other children. `Value` is exported. The public API is otherwise unchanged. Used by `features/library/{detail,kits,grid,index}.ts` (field labels, aria-labels, the validation note as `{0}: {1}` with English parts, Presets, Starters, buttons, tabs, search), `vector/{graphicstyles,recolor,artboards,view}.ts`, `vector/pathdialogs.ts` (`shell`, `numberField`, `checkRow`, `radioRow` and `selectField` now take English, so every path dialog follows), the inspector `section()` in `main.ts` (title through `labelText`, hint through `label`; `ctx.section` callers in `features/inspector/*` and `features/motion/*` pass English), and the Motion `entryShell`, `shell` and preset buttons. `main.ts` sets the dialog status title from `statusMessage` in a microtask after each switch; `features/stock-library.ts` passes its insert toasts as parts so the server note stays its own text. No new keys. Checked: Turn Q's 15 live switch sequences and en>L>en for all 25 languages, with the library form, Kits, the four inspector tabs, menus, team library, studio, stock and agents open, against fresh loads: ru>de>ja>en from 77 differing strings to 0, and 0 in every sequence; `npx tsc --noEmit`; `npm run build`. Known: about 350 other `t()` build sites (inspector panels, motion rows, Export for Screens) still pass translated text, 1096 keys share a translation with another key in some locale, and the toasts, status title and path dialogs were checked by build only.

## The narrow editor in the Linux desktop shell · v0.20.7 (2026-11-11)

Phase 54 (commit f8797b2). The Linux shell, run under Xvfb beside Chrome at 1440 x 900, 1100 x 800 and 1024 x 680 in English and German, shows the web menubar (`wantsNativeMenus()` in `ui/native-menu.ts` is true only on macOS with menus in the window off), and the More menus button folds the same menus at the same place as Chrome, within 1 to 2 px. `MIN_LOGICAL` in `src-tauri/src/main.rs` is 1024 x 680, so the shell never reaches the two-row top bar below 800 px; the 760 x 640 checks are browser-only. One defect in both engines: at 1100 px and below the selection crumbs were squeezed to about 12 px of text, and in German the "Editing" readout ran past the stage header. `styles/app.css`: `.stage-head .viewport-hint` gets `flex: 0 1000 auto`, so the hidden hint gives up its space first, and `.viewport-readout` ends in an ellipsis. `styles/shell.css`: `.fw-crumb` aligns to the start so its ellipsis shows. Checked in Chrome: crumbs 86 px wide at 1100 and 1024 px in both languages (from 22 to 41 px), no stage header overflow in German (from 7 to 22 px). Known: at 760 px the crumbs are still about 40 px; the More popup is more see-through in software-rendered WebKitGTK (backdrop blur not applied under Xvfb); segmented-button word wrap differs slightly between the engines; the command palette matches English labels only.

## Menus that do not fit move into an overflow menu · v0.20.7 (2026-11-11)

Phase 53 (commit 49d1160). Turn Q's `fw-top2` depended on the menus' width, so German and Russian had two rows at 1440 x 900 and a language switch moved the page 40 px. `ui/shell.ts`: `Menubar` builds a `fw-menu-btn fw-menu-more` button (three-dot `more` icon from `ui/icons.ts`, title "More menus") with a popup holding one row per menu; `fold(count)` marks the last `count` menus `is-folded` (absolutely positioned and hidden, so measuring them stays possible) and shows their rows in the popup. A row opens the folded menu as a submenu beside it with the same commands, shortcuts and states. Keyboard: Up and Down in a popup, Right on a More row opens its submenu, Left closes it, Escape closes one level and restores focus, Left and Right from an open menu walk the ring of visible menus plus More. Popups are clamped to the window. `ui/fit.ts`: `watchFit()` takes the menubar as a fourth argument; `foldCount()` measures from the right how many menus must fold for brand, menus, More, title tools and actions to share the row, before first paint and on resize, language change and font load; `fw-top2` now depends on width alone (`TWO_ROWS_BELOW = 800`). `main.ts` passes the menubar. `styles/shell.css`: `.is-folded`, `.fw-menu-more`. One new key, "More menus". Checked at 1440, 1280, 1100, 900 and 760 px in English, German and Russian with CPU 4x: nothing cut, no sideways scroll, every menu reachable from the bar or the overflow by mouse and keyboard; CLS 0 opening the editor; a live switch from English to German at 1440 from 0.465 to 0.024.

## No text built from translated parts · v0.20.6 (2026-11-11)

Pieces with their own number or plural cannot nest in a `{t}` value, so `ui/dom.ts` gains `pieces(node, parts)`, one text node per part, rewritten only when the joined text differs; it serves the team library meta and detail, the kits meta, the Website studio summary, the stock notes, Agent Connect, code components, the AI writer, the library notes (`library/grid.ts` `setMessage` takes an extra text node) and the status bar and dialog status (`status()` takes a string or parts; the title stays the joined text). English text unchanged.

## Live language switches keep each word's English source · v0.20.5 (2026-11-11)

Phase 48, found by the shell comparison and present in Chrome too. `i18n/index.ts`: the reverse map from a shown string to its English key (`made`) is now keyed by language and shown string, marks a string ambiguous when two sources give it, and is reached only through `source()`, which uses the language the node was last drawn in and never maps a string that is itself a key; untranslated on-page strings count as their own source (`own()`), and already-translated `{t}` values passed to `t()` and `tn()` go back to English (`unmade()`). Per-node records are no longer dropped on English or when a translation equals its source, padded text keeps its whitespace through a `redo`, and `setLanguage` drains pending mutations before and after the listeners, with a `done` set so nodes the listeners re-rendered are not translated twice. `lookup()` takes a language; `MADE_MAX` is 20000. `vector/panel2.ts` (width-profile titles), `vector/select.ts` (wand tolerance rows) and `features/inspector/variables.ts` (Element fields) now pass English or use the values form. Checked: 54 live switch sequences compared with a fresh load of the final language (542 differing strings in 40 sequences before, 0 after), Preferences and the tokens dialog after the last switch, and one layout shift per switch as before (text width changes between languages). Known: a component that shows a pre-translated label whose translation another key shares can still go stale; `graphicstyles.ts:161`, `recolor.ts:232`, `library/detail.ts:198` and `team-library.ts:137` still build text from translated parts.

`main.ts`: `status()` also writes its action into `dialogStatusAction`, a text button in the dialog footer between the status and Close, and sets the footer status's `title` to the full message; `openDialog` hides the button whenever a dialog opens, so a stale button never sits beside an empty message; the footer button closes the dialog and then runs the action through `run`, so Set the base URL reaches the field behind it. The Base URL field sends `baseUrl: trimmed || null`, so emptying it removes the setting. `styles/app.css`: `.dialog-status` is clamped to two lines (`-webkit-line-clamp: 2`, `min-width: 0`) and `.dialog-footer` keeps its 56 px; W1 first raised every footer to 72 px to fit a three-line warning, and the director replaced that with the clamp, since the full text stays in the title and the status bar. `.dialog-status-action` matches `.status-action`. `features/authoring.ts`: the `seo-origin` template is titled Base URL and SEO settings and its description names the base URL. ## The Base URL field and the status action · v0.19.9 (2026-11-10)

`main.ts`: the page pane's Whole site section starts with a Base URL field made by `addInspectorInput` (key `site.baseUrl`), filled from state through `inspectorValues`, so `value()` never overwrites it while focused; a change sends `site.update {patch: {settings: {baseUrl}}}` with trailing slashes trimmed, and the engine's http(s) refusal reaches the status line through `run`. `openBaseUrlField()` opens the right dock, shows the page inspector, scrolls the field into view without a jump and focuses it. `status(message, error, action?)` writes `statusActionRun` and a `button.status-action` that sits hidden in the status bar between the message and the revision; the next status without an action hides it again. `NOT_FOUND_BASE_KEY` holds the 404 sentence; `notFoundBaseWarning(warned?)` tests the editor's state for Export and the answer's `warnings` for Send, and `baseUrlAction` adds Set the base URL when the sentence is shown. Three keys appended to `i18n/keys.ts` (2514 in all) and the 23 array locales.

`styles/app.css`: the status bar has four columns (`244px minmax(0, 1fr) auto auto`, and `216px 1fr auto auto` under 1100 px); the action takes column 3 and the revision is pinned to column 4, so a hidden action never lets the revision move (it jumped 12 px before the pin, caught by W1's check).

Checked: with the CPU throttled 4x in headless Chrome, export showed the sentence and the action, the action focused the field, a typed address saved without trailing slashes and the next export carried no sentence, a bad address showed the engine's refusal, a focused field kept its text across an outside change, and Send showed the action only while the warning held; CLS 0 at every step, the bar 28 px. The director's run showed the same in German (Basis-URL festlegen). `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npm run build` and the window checks pass.

`main.ts`, copy and paste helper region: `copyFragment` posts `{node, embed: true, clip: true}`, writes the whole fragment with `embedded` and `clip` to the clipboard, and returns `{behind, left}` (assets behind the clip, and `notEmbedded` minus those). `behindClipNote` and `copyNote` sit beside `notEmbeddedNote`; Copy and Cut use `copyNote`, with "this computer" sentences for assets behind the clip and the 0.19.6 "this FormWeb" sentences for assets not carried. `pasteFragmentText` sends `clip` beside `embedded` in the `/api/paste` body. ## Copies that paste in another FormWeb · v0.19.6 (2026-11-10)

`main.ts`, copy and paste helper region: `copyFragment` posts `{node, embed: true}` and returns how many assets are in `notEmbedded`; `notEmbeddedNote(count)` appends one of two keyed sentences (one asset, or `{n}` assets) to the Copy and Cut statuses. `pasteFragmentText` lifts `embedded` out of the clipboard fragment and sends it beside the bare fragment in the `/api/paste` body. A 409 already reached the status through `request()`; it now names the assets the other FormWeb lacks. Two keys added to `i18n/keys.ts` and all locales.

Checked in headless Chrome with two tabs on 8703 and 8713: an image copied in one installation pastes and renders in the other (naturalWidth 40); Cut puts `embedded` on the clipboard; a fragment naming an asset the target lacks, without bytes, shows the 409 detail in the red status and adds nothing; after a vector Rotate, Mod+D repeats the transform (node count unchanged) and Shift+Mod+D duplicates (node count plus one). CLS 0, no page errors. Not run live: a copy with more than 16 MiB of assets, so the note itself was only checked by reading.

## Copy, cut and paste elements · v0.19.5 (2026-11-10)

The executor spec is `TURN_F_SPEC.md` in the 0.19.5 planning sidecar.

`commands/registry.ts` gives `Command` an optional `native?: () => boolean`, and `commands/keymap.ts` returns before running a command whose `native()` is true, so the browser keeps the key. `main.ts`: `edit.copy` (Mod+C), `edit.cut` (Mod+X) and `edit.paste` (Mod+V) sit in Edit group 1, and the layers context menu now builds four rows (Copy, Cut, Paste, Duplicate). Copy posts `/api/fragment` and writes `JSON.stringify(fragment)` with `navigator.clipboard.writeText`; Cut is copy then `node.remove`, so only the removal is an undo step. Paste's key is left to the browser (`native: () => true`) because the `paste` event carries the text with no clipboard permission and works in Safari; a document `paste` listener ignores inputs, textareas, contenteditable, `.xterm`, `[data-no-keys]`, open dialogs and any text that is not a fragment of version 1, and posts the rest to `/api/paste` with parent the selection's parent and index after it (or last in the page root), then selects the pasted root (`result.root`, else the new child). The Edit menu and context menu items read the clipboard with `readText` and, when the browser refuses, say how to allow it or to paste with the keyboard. Copy and Cut use `panelTextSelected` as their `native()`, so with text selected outside the stage the browser copies that text; inputs, text editing and code views were already excluded by the `TYPING` guard. `edit.duplicate` takes `['Mod+D', 'Shift+Mod+D']` and reads `window.event.shiftKey` in its run: with Shift held it always duplicates, otherwise it still runs Transform again first while that command is enabled (a vector tool, a last transform and a shape). 13 English keys were appended to `keys.ts`; the two clipboard help messages name no platform key.

Checked: `npm run build`. In headless Chrome with clipboard permissions, Ctrl+C and Ctrl+V pasted an element with a reveal after another and selected it on the same page, another page and a new site, one undo each; Ctrl+X then paste moved it; plain text pasted nothing; Ctrl+C with panel text selected copied the text; Shift+Ctrl+D duplicated; no layout shift and no page errors. Not run: Shift+Mod+D while a vector transform is repeatable (checked by reading the run function), and the 409 message in the browser.

## Duplicate element, light posters and version 2 imports · v0.19.4 (2026-11-10)

The executor spec is `TURN_E_SPEC.md` in the 0.19.4 planning sidecar.

`main.ts`: `insideLinkedInstance` walks the node and its ancestors for `componentId` or `componentSourceId` (the engine's own refusal rule); `canDuplicate` adds `notRoot` and a closed scrim; `duplicateSelection` runs `node.duplicate {id}` and selects the node that now follows the original among its parent's children. `edit.duplicate` (Edit > Duplicate element, `Mod+D`, group 1) sits before `edit.delete`. `vector/select.ts` registered `Mod+D` first for `vector.transform-again`, and the registry gives a combination to its first registration, so `edit.duplicate` takes the key and runs Transform again whenever that command is enabled, duplicating otherwise; the vector command keeps its menu entry. Layer rows open a small `fw-layer-menu` on `contextmenu` (existing `.fw-menu` styles, closed by an outside pointer or Escape) whose Duplicate item carries the disabled reason as its title, because the menu has no reason field. The importer reads the first two bytes: `PK` sends the File as a raw `application/zip` Blob to `/api/load` (never read into memory by the page), anything else keeps the JSON path; `api.ts` `request()` sends a Blob body raw with its own type.

`ui/canvas.ts`: `setPosters(posters, lights)` keeps a light map beside the poster map and re-renders only when either changes; `syncPoster` uses the light url when `themeMode` is `light`, the node has a poster and the light url passes `safeUrl`, rewriting the one existing `img[data-fw-poster]` src, so the theme switch adds no element. The canvas theme comes from the Design system dialog's preview (light and dark buttons in `features/inspector/theme.ts`), not View > Theme, which themes the editor chrome. `features/inspector/components.ts` adds Light theme poster, Upload light poster and Clear light poster under the poster controls; `commitPoster(url, field)` writes either field through the same `extension.set` on the code group. Nine English keys were appended to `keys.ts` and translated into the 23 array locales in the same positions; es-MX and fr-CA needed no overrides.

Checked: `npm run build` clean; i18n check at 2,493 keys. In headless Chrome `Mod+D` on an element with a reveal selected the copy right after it and one undo removed it, with no layout shift; toggling the light and dark preview swapped only the poster src and the html theme attribute (MutationObserver), CLS 0; upload and clear of a light poster changed the record; File > Import loaded a version 1 JSON file and a version 2 zip.

## Send to FormPublisher · v0.19.3 (2026-11-10)

The executor spec is `TURN_D_SPEC.md` in the 0.19.3 planning sidecar. `main.ts` gained one function, `sendToFormPublisher`, and one command, `file.send-formpublisher` in the File menu (group 2, enabled when the engine is ready); `commands/catalogue.ts` describes it for the palette and agents. The function posts `/api/handoff/formpublisher?locale=<locale>` and writes the result to the status line: the revision it became when FormPublisher answered, the inbox path when it did not, the error when FormPublisher refused. Nothing else in the editor changed; the site is not mutated. Four English keys were appended to `keys.ts` and translated into the 25 locales in the same positions; es-MX and fr-CA carry overrides only for the two lines whose wording differs.

Checked: `npm run build` clean and the i18n check clean at 2,484 keys. The menu item, driven in headless Chrome, added a third revision of a site already sent twice.

## Motion tab, scene scrubber, variables, components and code sections · v0.19.1 (2026-11-10)

`main.ts` changed in five single regions: the tab list (Design, Motion, Content, Page), the hidden `motionPane`, `setInspector`, the `panes.motion` entry, and a `window.motion` command; plus one listener for `fw:open-studio`, which the Code components list dispatches to open the Studio's build action, because the inspector context has no Studio hook. `.inspector-tabs` now has four columns. `features/motion/context.ts` is the contract every editor builds against: `installMotion(ctx)` in `index.ts` builds the `MotionContext` (`set`, `remove`, `track`, the easing picker and motion-safe style rows from `fields.ts`) and calls one installer per section; each editor calls `ctx.section` itself, so its container is a plain div. Presets are toggles: clicking an active one removes it; the classic effects write `data-fw-effect` through `nodePatch` as the Design tab's select does. Easing y values are allowed from -2 to 3. List rows in the action, step, disclosure and track editors are keyed by index, since entries have no ids; reordering rewrites the array and rows update in place. `pick.ts` fills every node select on every update, because a freshly switched action or step type composes from selects that were never filled. Move up and Move down carry no icon, as the Hierarchy section's buttons do.

The scrubber (`scrubber.ts`) sits before the viewport readout and calls `canvas.setMotionFrame(frameFor(...))`; `interpolate.ts` is a port of `motion.js` easing and interpolation that matched it in 2,988 of 2,988 sampled comparisons. `canvas.ts` merges the frame after the breakpoint styles, deleting an authored key that resolves to the same property so nothing is written twice, and tracks frame keys in the existing style bookkeeping, so clearing restores exactly the authored values; `html.lang` is now written only when it differs, the one non-style write seen while scrubbing. Posters are one `img[data-fw-poster]` (or a dashed `div` reading "Component") appended after the model children with no `data-fw-id`, so selection still lands on the host; svg-family tags never get one. `hidden` is now passed to the canvas, so script and search templates look on the canvas as they will on the site. The keyframe rail carries `data-no-keys`, because the global keymap otherwise deleted the selected node on Delete. Scene progress on the canvas never derives from width.

`features/inspector/variables.ts` and `properties.ts` sit in the Design system dialog; names are keys, so renaming is remove and add; tables commit whole and are matched by index so a key edit keeps focus. `components.ts` stores a poster on the component through `extension.set` on the `code` group (the instance command only writes the instance); the props form is rebuilt only when the node, component or schema changes; the insert box hides while the node hosts an instance. `code.ts`'s build action now spreads the existing `code` group before writing, so `allowSections` and `sections` survive a build. `code-section.ts` imports an HTML file with `DOMParser`, never into the live DOM. `loop.ts` shows `infinite` as the placeholder of an empty iterations field and also accepts the translated word. `api.ts` uploads SVG up to 2 MiB. 313 English keys were appended to `keys.ts` and translated into the 25 locales in the same positions; es-MX and fr-CA gained overrides only where wording differs.

Checked: `npm run build` clean. The editor was driven in headless Chrome under 4x CPU and a 150 ms, 200 KB/s network against the 0.19.1 engine: a preset, the scene scrubber at 0, 50 and 100 (the caption read `0.2 | 0px 24px`, `0.6 | 0px 12px`, `1 | 0px`), the component and section posters on the canvas; no layout shift without input, and the only canvas mutations other than `style` were the two `data-fw-selected` toggles of a selection change.

## Transform 3D, Effects, Media, Themes and Fonts · v0.19.0 (2026-11-09)

`src/features/inspector/` holds the Phase 1 sections as modules: `context.ts` (shared context type, design-group readers, the breakpoint style helper), `transform3d.ts`, `effects.ts`, `media.ts`, `theme.ts`, `fonts.ts`, and `index.ts` whose `installInspector(ctx)` returns `{ update, updateTokens, insertVideo }`. `main.ts` changed in five single regions only: the import, the Video card after the Insert grid loop, the `installInspector` call just before `settingsPane`, `inspectorExt.update(next, node, breakpoint)` inside `update()`, and `inspectorExt.updateTokens(state())` at the end of `updateTokens()`. Every section is built once and patched in `update()`: `hidden` toggles and `value()`, which skips the focused field; token and font rows use `keyed()`. Transform 3D and Effects go through `addStyle` and `addSegments`, so the picked breakpoint scopes them like every other style field; nothing reads the canvas width. Media shows for img and video only, so tag changes never cause an async jump. Themes and Fonts sit in the tokens dialog, where tokens are edited, and write the whole object with `design.set`; an emptied themes object is written as is, since the engine's handling of an empty object was not separately checked. The canvas gets its theme from the parent only: `canvas.ts` `setAppearance(mode, overrides, fontCss, rerender)` sets `data-theme` and inline token overrides on the iframe's `documentElement` and owns one `<style>` for `@font-face`, so no script runs in the canvas; overrides are limited to keys present in `site.tokens`, and the default theme previews with none. The canvas CSP's `font-src` widened from `data:` to `data: http: https:` for that, the only CSP change. The canvas allows playsinline, decoding, fetchpriority, srcset (every candidate through `safeUrl`) and sizes, and passes `data-fw-theme-only`; `autoplay` and `inert` stay off the canvas so video never plays there and every node stays clickable. `api.ts` `upload()` takes MP4 and WebM up to 64 MiB and WOFF2 up to 4 MiB, sending `font/woff2` by file name, beside the 8 MiB images. A pre-existing layout bug surfaced and is fixed in `inspector.css`: `.stage-panel` had an implicit auto grid column sized by the stage head's min-content, already about 10 px too wide from the crumbs and hint and about 80 px with the new switch, so the canvas scaled against the wrong width and painted over the inspector; `.workspace > .stage-panel { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) }` pins it. 81 English keys were appended to the end of `keys.ts` only, because the locale tables are positional. Not built yet: an `insert.video` menu command (the Insert card exists) and any UI for `design.properties`. Checked: `npm run build` clean; the editor driven in headless Chrome under 4x CPU and a throttled network against the 0.19.0 engine showed no page errors and no layout shift outside input while exercising every new field, the canvas theme switch and a real video and font upload.

## Window smoke (F7) · v0.18.5 (2026-10-26)

`tests/window/test_window.py` (pytest, Playwright 1.63.0) starts the engine on a free port against an empty data folder, home and agent folder, serving `frontend/dist`, and opens `/` and `/splash.html` at 4x CPU slowdown and a slow network: a title, no request refused (403) or failed, no console or page error, layout shift 0 after load, a bounded 15 s idle wait (its timeout is ignored, because the window holds the `/api/events` stream open) and a 3 s settle. Requests are no longer watched once the measurement is taken, so closing the browser does not count the aborted stream. Needs `FORMWEB_CORE_BIN` (or `native/target/release/formweb-core`) and `npm run build`.

## Splash logo no longer jumps · v0.18.5 (2026-10-26)

`public/splash.html` centred the logo, the line and the bar together, so a paint that landed after the logo but before the rest centred the logo alone and moved it about 27 px when the rest arrived (layout shift, about 1 load in 6 to 10). The body now starts at a fixed `padding-top: calc(50vh - 64px)` and the line has a fixed 16 px height, so the logo's position no longer depends on later siblings; the group sits where it did, within half a pixel. Verified with a server that sends the page up to the line, waits 1 s, then sends the rest: shift 1.05e-4 before, 0 after.

## The vector tools in FormWeb's own names · v0.17.2 (2026-10-23)

Labels only: Shape Crafter (was the panel, tool and hint wording of the shape-merging tool), Trace Image, Pin Warp, the Fill Regions Bucket and Make, Expand and Release Fill Regions. Command ids (`vector.livepaint.*`, `vector.live-paint-bucket`, `vector.puppet`, `vector.trace`), tool ids, icons and the saved `data-fw-livepaint` attribute are unchanged, so saved sites, shortcuts and agent scripts still work. New groups and faces are named Fill Regions and Fill region; the traced svg is named Traced image. The 9 keys were renamed in place in `i18n/keys.ts`, and the line at the same index was edited by hand in each of the 23 positional locale tables, with translations that avoid the other product's localized names. The family tables in `i18n/family/` still hold a shared Puppet Warp entry that FormWeb no longer looks up; it is left for the family-wide pass.

## App icon and brand mark · v0.13.3 (2026-10-20)

`ui/shell.ts` `BRAND_MARK` and the app icon set (`src-tauri/icons/`, `assets/formweb_icon.svg`) move from the orange outline window to the Form family construction: the dark tile with bloom, rim and drop shadow, and the family spectrum (#3A7BFF, #9A5CFF, #FF4F7A, #FFAE2B). The icon is a page being edited: a dark glass browser window with spectrum dots in its bar, a hero block and two cards as raised glossy tiles sharing one diagonal gradient, the hero selected with white corner handles, and form.md's kite pointer (4.8 units per grid step, two-layer shadow) on the bottom right handle. The macOS .icns uses the 824 px tile inside a 1024 canvas with a 100 px margin; the .ico and PNGs are full bleed. `BRAND_MARK` is drawn at 18 px, so it keeps only the tile, window and three gradient blocks, with no filters; its gradient ids are prefixed `fwm-` and it is mounted once, in the title bar brand.

## Scroll and pointer effects · v0.13.2 (2026-10-17)

`main.ts` Content pane, above Interaction: `effectSection` with `effectKind` (`data-field` `effect.kind`) and `effectSpeed` (`effect.speed`), patching the node attributes `data-fw-effect` and `data-fw-effect-speed` (None sends `null` for both); `update` fills them through `value()` and disables Strength without an effect. The motion itself is in the exporter: `native/crates/formweb-export/src/effects.js` (`EFFECTS_RUNTIME`), appended to `assets/runtime.js` by both exporters, so Preview and published sites run it and the canvas does not.

- **Source basis:** Figma Sites "Add interactions to an element" (scroll parallax, scroll transform, mouse parallax, marquee, cursor), Webflow Help "Glossary for Interactions with GSAP" (scroll scrub, mouse move), Framer Academy "Parallax with Scroll Speed".
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, cache off):** inspector set, cleared and undid an effect, no layout shifts; exported test page: all five effects moved, reduced motion stopped them.

## Starter sites · v0.13.1 (2026-10-17)

`features/sections.ts`: `STARTER_SITES` (business, product, portfolio; pages as section lists) and an optional `links` argument to `sectionCommands` that swaps the navigation bar's links for real page links. `main.ts` New site dialog: `newStarter` select (`data-field` `new.starter`, Blank first); `performDialogAction` sends `/api/new` with `blank: true` when a starter is chosen, then `starterSiteCommands` (rename Home, fill its root, `page.add` with caller-chosen ids for the rest, every section) as one batch. The engine's `formweb_model::blank` is what makes the empty root possible: the sample's component source cannot be removed.

- **Source basis:** Framer Help "Using templates", Figma Sites "Insert blocks, embeds, webpages, and design libraries into a site" (start from a template or blank file), Webflow Updates "Build faster with prebuilt layouts".
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, cache off):** Business site made Home, Pricing and Contact with linked navigation; undo to one empty page, redo to three. Layout shift 0.0056 twice: the pages list grows from one row to three and pushes the Layers head down, the same as adding pages any other way.

## Prebuilt sections and starter pages · v0.13.0 (2026-10-17)

`features/sections.ts` (new): `SECTIONS` (nine specs: nav, hero, features, testimonial, pricing, faq, cta, contact, footer), `STARTERS` (landing, pricing, contact as lists of section ids) and `sectionCommands(id, parent, fresh)`, which flattens a spec into `node.add` commands, parents first, with fresh ids and a `mobile` breakpoint where a spec sets one. Styles inherit color and use a translucent neutral border so a section fits light or dark sites. `main.ts` Insert pane: Sections and Starter pages grids (`data-section`, `data-starter`), `insertSection` (finds the selection's top-level ancestor under the page root and adds a `node.move` to place the new section after it) and `insertStarter` (`page.add` with caller-chosen page and root ids, then every section, unique slug by suffix). Both send one `commands()` batch, so one undo step.

- **Source basis:** Framer Updates "Inserting Sections" and Help "Using templates", Webflow Updates "Build faster with prebuilt layouts", Figma Sites "Insert blocks, embeds, webpages, and design libraries into a site".
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, cache off):** nine sections inserted in sequence after the selection; undo removed one whole; starter pages made /landing and /landing-2; undo removed the second. The only layout shift was the breadcrumb resizing to the new page name when the starter page opened.

## Custom code in head and body · v0.12.9 (2026-10-17)

`main.ts` Page pane, after Search & metadata: `codeSection` with `codeScope` (Whole site or Page) and three `codeAreas` (`data-field` `code.head`, `code.bodyStart`, `code.bodyEnd`). `fillCustomCode` fills them through `value()` (skips the focused area, so typing is never overwritten) and runs in `update`; `saveCustomCode` merges into a copy of the `code` group (keeping `components` and `instances`), drops emptied slots and empty maps, and sends one `extension.set`. The engine validates (`formweb-model` `custom_code`), prunes and copies `pageCode` with pages, and the advanced exporter writes the slots. `styles/app.css` `.fw-code-area`: monospace, no wrap.

- **Source basis:** Framer Help "How to add custom code" (head or body, all or chosen pages), Webflow Help "Custom code in head and body tags" (site and page level), Figma Sites "Edit website settings" (head or body start or end, site-wide only).
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, cache off):** site head tag typed and saved, page body-end script saved, scope switched both ways, slot cleared then undone; no layout shifts, no page errors. Exported pages ran the scripts; page code only on its page.

## Variable width on closed shapes · v0.12.7 (2026-10-17)

`vector/ops.ts` `widthPreset`: closed paths take every preset (it skipped them except Uniform); the profile runs once around the outline from the first anchor and `variableOutline` draws it as an outer ring and a reversed inner ring (nonzero fill, so a hollow band). `vector/stroke.ts` `widthOwner` and `vector/editor.ts` `pickPath`: picking a variable-width stroke picks its path, not the generated Width outline (before, a click on such a stroke selected the outline, so the Width tool and the presets acted on the wrong path, open or closed).

- **Source basis:** Figma Help "Apply and adjust stroke properties": width profiles turn uniform strokes into tapered or varying lines, the Variable width tool adjusts thickness at any point, and the support table lists rectangles, ellipses, polygons and stars as supported (branching vector networks, dynamic and dashed strokes are not). Figma Plugin API "VariableWidthStrokeProperties": presets Uniform, Wedge, Taper, Quarter taper, Eye, Mirrored taper, plus width points by position and width. FormWeb's presets are named differently (Uniform, Taper end, Taper both, Bulge, Wide ends) and saved profiles stand in for custom ones.
- **Found while driving:** Bulge on a closed rectangle did nothing (the closed-path filter); after that fix, a click on the band selected "Width outline", so the Width tool found no width points; fixed by `widthOwner` in picking.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** a rectangle and an ellipse with a 14 px stroke and no fill; Bulge on the rectangle and Taper both on the ellipse each wrote `data-fw-width` and a two-ring outline (nonzero), rendered as hollow bands of varying thickness; with the rectangle clicked (selection read "Path"), the Width tool showed its 3 width points and a drag on the left edge added a fourth; undo back to 3. No page errors.

## Symbols shared across the site · v0.12.6 (2026-10-17)

`ui/canvas.ts`: `foreignSymbols` keeps one hidden svg at the top of the canvas document holding copies of symbols that this page's instances use but another page defines; rebuilt only when those symbols change (a JSON key of their subtrees), so editing a master elsewhere updates instances here without remounting anything. `vector/symbols.ts`: `symbolsOnPage` lists this page's symbols first and then the rest of the site's; `symbolOfUse` resolves site-wide; `placeCommands` places any symbol of the site. `vector/ops.ts` `replaceSymbol` swaps to any symbol. Rust: `formweb-model` `site_symbols` (a `use` may point at a symbol on any page; removing a symbol still used anywhere is refused); `formweb-export` `Renderer::foreign_symbols` renders the symbols a page uses from other pages into a hidden svg at the top of that page's body, in both the plain and the routed exporter.

- **Source basis:** Figma reuses components across a whole file (Figma Help on components, G72 in the vendor lanes: main components, linked instances, detach, swap).
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** a symbol defined on the home page; a second page "Two" added through the API with a drawing whose instance points at that symbol; opening page Two in the editor drew the instance at 161 by 161 from the hidden copy; removing the home page drawing was refused (400); GET /api/export returned the site zip, and two/index.html carried the hidden svg with the symbol and its path; that file opened in Chromium drew the instance at 161 by 161 in the symbol's orange, with no page errors.

## Symbols shared across a page · v0.12.5 (2026-10-17)

`vector/symbols.ts`: `pageRootOf` and `symbolsOnPage`; `symbolOfUse` falls back to the symbols of the whole page; `placeCommands` places an instance of any symbol on the same page into the active svg. `vector/ops.ts` `replaceSymbol` swaps across svgs on one page. `vector/panel2.ts`: the Symbols list shows every symbol on the page. Rust (`formweb-model/src/vector.rs`): `page_symbols`; a `use` may point at a symbol in another svg on the same page (inline svgs share the page's id space); a commit that would leave an instance pointing at a deleted symbol is refused with "detach its instances before deleting a symbol they use".

- **Source basis:** Figma Help on components (G72 in the vendor lanes): main components and linked instances that update from the main, detach and swap. Figma reuses components across a whole file; FormWeb now shares symbols across one page.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** a symbol defined in the first drawing; a second drawing added on the same page (through the API, because a new drawing started on the canvas joined the active one); with the second drawing's path selected, Place instance put a `use` there pointing at the first drawing's symbol, rendered 161 by 161; recolouring the master through the API turned the instance blue (pixel 37, 99, 235); removing the first drawing was refused (400) while the instance existed; Break link to symbol detached it, and the removal then succeeded. No page errors; Layers row insertion shifts up to 0.0039 (known class).
- **Not checked:** the exported HTML page with a cross-drawing instance (both drawings are inline on one page, so the reference should resolve as on the canvas).

## Vector PDF export · v0.12.4 (2026-10-17)

New `vector/pdfexport.ts` (`svgToPdf`): a dependency-free PDF 1.7 writer. It walks the rendered svg in the canvas iframe (so computed styles apply), composes each element's `transform` by hand, and writes paths (path, rect, circle, ellipse, line, polyline, polygon) with fill and stroke colour, width scaled by the transform, cap, join, miter, dash and fill rule; opacity, fill-opacity and stroke-opacity through ExtGState; clip-path groups as PDF clipping paths; `use` instances by walking their symbol; text lines (the direct tspans, each with x and y) in Helvetica, one of the PDF base fonts, at their size, colour and anchor (anchor shift from `getComputedTextLength`). Gradients fall back to their first stop colour; patterns, images, masks, filters and blend modes are skipped. One drawing unit is one point; the page is the export's viewBox (whole drawing or selection crop). `vector/exportscreens.ts`: format `pdf` (written once, like SVG, whatever scales are ticked), `Source` carries the live svg, its viewBox and the chosen ids. `commands/catalogue.ts` export entry; `docs/AGENT_QUICKSTART.md` lists PDF and the v0.12.2 and v0.12.3 commands.

- **Source basis:** Figma Help "Export formats and settings": PDFs include text, fonts, vector graphics and images in a fixed layout, text as glyphs, 1x only, PDF 1.7, Plus darker and Plus lighter blend modes not supported. Bleed and crop marks are not documented there, and none are written here.
- **Found while driving:** the canvas renders each text line as a tspan holding an inner tspan, so the first version drew every line twice (once at 0,0); only direct tspan children are drawn now. A first test drew shapes outside the drawing's box, which the export (like PNG and SVG) crops away; the test was redrawn inside a backdrop.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** a stroked backdrop rectangle, a rectangle and ellipse made into a mask with Use as mask, and area type in a further ellipse; Export for Screens with PNG and PDF ticked downloaded pdf-check.png and pdf-check.pdf. qpdf --check: no syntax or stream errors; pdfinfo: 1 page, 618.59 by 666.96 pt, PDF 1.7; pypdf text: "Vector PDF / export keeps paths, clips and text / from the drawing."; the page rendered with Poppler matched the PNG export side by side (clipped arc, outlines, text placement). No page errors.

## Snapping to Figma's level · v0.12.3 (2026-10-17)

`vector/snap.ts` `snapLocal`: every other object's box corners and edge middles join its centre and anchors as snap points (Figma snaps to the centres and outermost points of objects). `vector/extras.ts`: `suspend` (set from `event.ctrlKey` on every canvas pointerdown and pointermove in `vector/editor.ts`) turns snapping off while Control is held; `pixelSnap` rounds a point that snapped to nothing else to whole drawing units, or to whole page pixels when no drawing is active (a new drawing is placed in page pixels). `vector/index.ts`: `vector.snap.pixel` toggle (off by default); `commands/catalogue.ts` entry; 1 string in `i18n/keys.ts` and 23 locale files (1918 keys).

- **Source basis:** Figma Help "Adjust alignment, rotation, position and dimensions": Snap to objects aligns "the centers and outermost points of different objects", a red guide appears, "hold Control to temporarily disable them", and Snap to pixel grid aligns objects to an underlying grid.
- **Found while driving:** pixel snapping first did nothing for a shape drawn with no drawing selected, because `snapPoint` returned before rounding when there is no active svg; it now rounds page pixels there. Two script slips (path order in the state is not drawing order; a shape drawn under the floating panel) were fixed in the script.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** rectangle A, then a rectangle started 2 and 3 px off A's right edge middle began exactly at (324.42, 163.21); the same start with Control held stayed at (327.66, 168.04), undo; dragging the second rectangle's centre near A's centre landed it at A's centre with the snap marker; with Snap to pixel grid on, a new drawing's path read M2 2 L98 2 L98 115 L2 115 Z. No page errors, no layout shifts.

## Use as mask, Shape Builder subtract checked · v0.12.2 (2026-10-17)

`vector/arrange.ts` `makeMaskCommands` takes `from: 'top' | 'bottom'`; 'bottom' is Figma's Use as mask (the bottom selected path becomes the clipPath, the paths above it go in the clipped group). `vector/ops.ts` `makeMask(kind, from)` selects the contents afterwards. `vector/index.ts`: `vector.mask.use` (makes the mask with two or more paths selected, otherwise releases), no default key because Mod+Alt+M is Make opacity mask. `commands/catalogue.ts` entry; 2 strings in `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (1917 keys).

- **Source basis:** Figma Help "Masks": the mask is the layer below the masked layers, Use as mask in the right sidebar, Remove mask, hidden areas reappear. Figma Help "Create custom shapes with the shape builder tool": drag to merge regions, Option or Alt-click to remove one.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** rectangle (bottom) and ellipse (top), Use as mask: the clipPath holds the rectangle, the clipped group holds the ellipse, render shows the ellipse cut to the rectangle; undo, redo, then Use as mask on the clipped ellipse removed the mask. Shape Builder: hover highlighted the overlap, Alt-click removed it (two paths), undo, then with both paths selected again a drag across the three regions merged them into one path. No page errors; one 0.00015 shift on the breadcrumb when the mask was removed.
- **Seen:** undo leaves the page selected, so Shape Builder needs the paths selected again after an undo.

## Typing into area type on the canvas · v0.12.0 (2026-10-17)

New `vector/areaedit.ts` (`AreaTextEditor`): a textarea over the first shape of the area type's thread, in shell coordinates (appended to the canvas shell beside the overlay), with the text's font, size, leading, weight, colour, alignment and inset at the canvas zoom, on a light or dark backing chosen from the text colour's brightness; Esc ends editing and keeps the text, `close(true)` commits `areaEditCommands` with the new text as one undo step and selects the text, an unchanged text commits nothing. `vector/editor.ts`: `areaEdit` field; a double-click with the Selection or Direct Selection tool on area type (a line, the text or its shape) opens it before the group isolation branch; any canvas pointerdown first closes and commits it, because the canvas's `preventDefault` keeps the textarea from blurring. Keys typed into the box do not reach the tool shortcuts (the editor key handler already skips textareas; the box also stops propagation). `styles/vector.css`: `.fw-v-areaedit`. `commands/catalogue.ts`: area-edit entry mentions the double-click. No new strings (the aria label is English like the Area Type dialog's).

- **Source basis:** double-clicking text with the Selection tool switches to the Type tool for editing: LinkedIn Learning (lynda.com) "Time-Saving Tips Using Illustrator" (single source, flagged); Esc leaves text editing and returns to the Selection tool: the same course and a PrintPlanet forum answer.
- **Not done:** the box is a rectangle over the shape's bounds, so the words wrap to the box while typing and take the shape's outline only once laid out; no live reflow while typing; no Type tool click to place a caret.
- **Found while driving:** a click elsewhere on the canvas did not end editing (the canvas pointerdown prevents the blur); now closed at the start of pointerdown.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** ellipse with 406 words: double-click opened the box with all 406 words and focus; select all, typed "Move views with the mountain and visit me" (letters that are tool shortcuts), the state unchanged while typing; Esc: one revision, two lines, box gone; undo: 25 lines of the old text; double-click, Ctrl+Home, typed "Start. ", click on empty canvas: committed, box gone; double-click then Esc with no change: no new revision. No page errors; one Layers row insertion shift (0.00068) at placement, known class.

## Live Paint edge highlight, Expand with edges, thread notice · v0.11.9 (2026-10-17)

`vector/extras.ts`: `hoverEdge` runs on pointer moves with the Live Paint Bucket while Shift is held over a selected Live Paint group; it highlights the edge within 6 screen px (`nearestEdge`, new in `vector/livepaint.ts` and shared with `paintEdge`) with an accent line over a wide halo in the page colour (`edge-halo`, `edge-hover` in `styles/vector.css`), and keeps the edge pieces cached until the group's spec or source outlines change; without Shift the face highlight works as before. The loaded-text notice for threaded text is cleared when the load is used or cancelled with Esc. `vector/live.ts` `expandCommands`: a Live Paint group that has `edges` drops its sources on Expand, since they carry neither fill nor stroke by then.

- **Source basis:** Smart Notes says the bucket highlights the edge it will paint ("highlight the edges with a thin line"); CreativePro says to position the bucket so the stroke rather than the fill is highlighted. The halo is FormWeb's choice for contrast.
- **Found while driving:** the first highlight was an accent-coloured line, invisible over faces painted in the same orange; the halo fixes it (3x crop checked).
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** stroked rectangle and ellipse as a Live Paint group: without Shift a face highlight; with Shift on the rectangle's outer edge an edge highlight and no face highlight; with Shift inside a face neither; with Shift on the inner edge an edge highlight; Shift released, the face highlight again. After painting one edge, Expand left 3 face paths and 4 edge paths and no unpainted sources; undo restored the group. Threaded text run again: the status text is empty after linking a shape and after Esc. No page errors; one Layers row insertion shift (0.00425) at Make, known class.

## Live Paint edges · v0.11.8 (2026-10-17)

`vector/boolean.ts`: `outlineSegments` now returns the signature of the other sources beside each segment, and the new `edgePieces` exposes it. `vector/livepaint.ts`: spec fields `edges` ([source index, other signature, x, y, colour, width]) and `os` (each source's original "stroke width"); `livePaintEdgePieces`, `initialEdges` (every edge takes its source's stroke), `paintEdge` (the edge within 6 screen px of the click takes the colour and width, or goes when the stroke is none), edge outputs drawn after the faces with fill none; `livePaintSig` includes edges only when present, so groups without edges keep their signature; `strokeMemo` and `livePaintStroke` for Release. `vector/live.ts`: the Live Paint branch carries edges and `os`, remaps edge source indexes and signatures when a source is deleted, and puts strokes back on release and when the group falls under two sources. `vector/extras.ts`: Shift with the Live Paint Bucket on a selected group paints one edge; on the first edge the sources' strokes are set to none and kept in `os`. `commands/catalogue.ts`: bucket entry. Rust `livepaint_config`: optional `edges` (up to 2000, source index inside `src`, paint colour, width 0 to 1000) and `os` (one string per source, up to 64 characters); attribute limit raised from 60,000 to 150,000 characters. No new strings: the miss notice is raw English like the other bucket notices.

- **Source basis:** Shift switches the Live Paint Bucket to edges and a click strokes the edge: Adobe Help ("Paint with the Live Paint Bucket tool"); the bucket applies the stroke attributes already set, aimed so the stroke rather than the fill is highlighted: CreativePro ("Live Paint Is a Bucket of Fun"); Paint Strokes targets edges, which are highlighted while hovering: Adobe Illustrator Smart Notes. Edges running between path crossings is the arrangement FormWeb uses; none of the fetched pages states it in those words (flagged). Release putting back the original strokes is FormWeb's choice.
- **Not done:** edge hover highlight, drag across several edges, double-click and triple-click flood painting of edges, Live Paint Bucket Options (Paint Fills, Paint Strokes) and the gap setting.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off), twice:** rectangle and ellipse, Make Live Paint, stroke colour red. Unstroked sources: Shift-click the rectangle's outer edge then its edge inside the ellipse: two red edges of width 2 (`0/0`, `0/2`), sources' strokes none, `os` "none "; a Shift-click inside a face left the spec alone and showed the notice. Stroked sources (#111827, 2): the first edge click turned the two sources into four edges (two red, two dark) and kept `os` "#111827 2". Dragging the rectangle's corner reshaped the outer red edge (path 52 to 67 characters) and kept every colour; undo; Release gave the paths their strokes back (#111827, 2, and none in the first run); undo restored the group; an edge naming source index 9 refused (400). Canvas DOM shows the edge paths with their stroke; a 3x screenshot shows the red rectangle edges over the faces. No page errors; one Layers row insertion shift of 0.00425 at Make (known class). Expand was not driven this release.

## Release Selection from a thread · v0.11.7 (2026-10-17)

New `releaseSelectionCommands` in `vector/areatype.ts`: the links whose shape or text is selected leave the thread (texts removed, outlines kept) and the words flow on through the links that remain; when the first shape is picked, the first remaining link's text takes over the spec with its shape as `src` and is renamed Area type. Wired as `vector.type.thread-release-selection` in `vector/index.ts`, with a `commands/catalogue.ts` entry, 1 string in `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (1913 keys). No Rust change.

- **Source basis:** Type > Threaded Text > Release Selection releases the object from the thread and the text flows into the next object: University of the Arts London Digital Media Centre ("Illustrator / Creating text") and Veerle Pieters ("Manage text area with Type Options").
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** 696 words through an ellipse, a rectangle and a copy of it made with the out port (overflow 110). Releasing the rectangle: chain down to the copy, overflow 262, rectangle kept; undo restored both links. Releasing the ellipse: the rectangle's text became the Area type with `src` on the rectangle (14 lines, overflow 393), the ellipse kept with no text; undo restored the thread. No page errors; the layout shifts read were Layers row insertions under 0.0007 (the console line was cut after six entries).
- **Seen while driving:** the status bar notice for loaded text stays until the next notice replaces it.

## Threaded text ports and Remove Threading · v0.11.6 (2026-10-17)

Edits in `vector/areatype.ts` (`threadCommands` takes an insert position and outlines for shapes made in the same batch; new `threadShapes`, `breakThreadCommands` (keep the links before the break, remove the texts after it, flow the words again) and `removeThreadingCommands` (each shape's text becomes its own area type with the words it shows now, the last one keeping the overflow; no line moves)), `vector/extras.ts` (`decoratePorts`: an in port at the top left and an out port at the bottom right of every shape in a selected thread, thread lines from each out port to the next in port, the last out port marked with the plus when words are left over; a click on an out port, or on the in port of a linked shape, loads the text; `placeThread` links a clicked closed shape at that position or copies the shape before it to an empty spot clicked; a double-click on a port breaks the thread there, timed in `downPort` as with freeform stops; Esc unloads; `decorateOverflow` skips the selected thread), `vector/editor.ts` (with text loaded, the next click on the canvas goes to `placeThread` before selection), `vector/index.ts` (`vector.type.thread-remove`), `commands/catalogue.ts` (new entry; the Release entry no longer calls itself Release Selection, which in Illustrator releases one object), `styles/vector.css` (`tport`, `tport-more`, `tport-on`, `thread`), `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (2 strings, 1912 keys). No Rust change: the specs written are the existing `data-fw-area` shape.

- **Source basis:** in and out ports on every area type object, the red plus in the out port, a click on a port to load the text, then a click on an object to link it or on an empty part of the artboard to make a new object, a double-click on a port at either end of a thread to break it, Remove Threading keeping the text in place: University of the Arts London Digital Media Centre ("Illustrator / Creating text") and CUNY City Tech COMD 1162 type notes. Remove Threading and Release Selection menu paths also in Veerle Pieters ("Manage text area with Type Options").
- **Not done:** Release Selection for one linked shape (the existing Release still rolls everything back to the first shape); dragging on an empty spot to size the new shape (a click copies the shape before the port); the loaded-text pointer icon (a notice in the status bar says what to do).
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** ellipse with 406 words (25 lines, 124 left over): two ports, out port marked; click it (port filled), click a rectangle: linked, 12 lines there, no overflow, four ports and a thread line; click the rectangle's out port then an empty spot: a copy of the rectangle linked third; undo; double-click the ellipse's out port: thread broken, 124 left over again; undo; click a port then Esc: unloaded; Remove Threading: ellipse spec 282 words, rectangle text its own area type with 124 words, no chain; undo restored the thread; a malformed chain refused (400). No page errors. Four Layers row insertion shifts under 0.0007 (the late-commit row class from v0.11.3).

## Live Paint groups · v0.11.5 (2026-10-17)

New `vector/livepaint.ts` (spec `data-fw-livepaint` {v, src, fills: [signature, x, y, colour], of: original fills, sig}; `livePaintPieces` uses `regionPieces` on the sources; a face is identified by its signature, the bitmask of sources that contain it; `livePaintOutputs` puts each fill on the face with its signature that holds its point, or the nearest one, and drops fills whose face is gone; `initialFills` gives each face the fill of the topmost source over it; `paintFill` sets the face under a point). Edits in `vector/live.ts` (LiveKind `livepaint`; a `liveFixes` branch that recomputes the faces when the sources change and remaps signatures when a source is deleted; new groups with `data-fw-livepaint` are checked in the same batch; `liveOf`, `liveSources`, `liveCopyOwner`, expand; release gives the sources back their original fills), `vector/ops.ts` (`livePaint()`: group, initial fills, sources set to no fill; Expand and Release include Live Paint), `vector/extras.ts` (the Live Paint Bucket paints a face of a selected Live Paint group, and on two or more plain selected paths makes the group with the clicked face painted, through `makeLivePaint` set by the editor), `vector/editor.ts`, `vector/index.ts` (`vector.livepaint.make`, `.expand`, `.release`), `commands/catalogue.ts` (bucket entry updated), `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (3 strings, 1910 keys). Rust: `data-fw-livepaint` on groups only, with up to 30 sources inside the group, up to 500 fills with paint values, `of` per source and a signature; `data-fw-live="livepaint"`.

- **Source basis:** Object > Live Paint > Make (or clicking the selection with the Live Paint Bucket), Expand into ordinary filled paths, Release back to ordinary paths: Adobe Illustrator Smart Notes ("Live Paint") and CreativePro ("Illustrator How-To: Live Paint Is a Bucket of Fun"). Fills following their face as the objects change, and being lost when the face disappears: CreativePro only (single source, flagged). Release restoring the original fills is FormWeb's choice; neither source says what fill released paths get.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** rectangle and ellipse, Make Live Paint: three faces (rectangle only, ellipse only, overlap) in the sources' orange, sources set to no fill, original fills kept; Live Paint Bucket in blue on the overlap: that face (signature 3) blue; dragging the rectangle's top anchor up reshaped the rectangle-only face (path 416 to 422 characters) and every fill stayed on its face; undo reverted the reshape; Release gave the paths their orange fills back; undo restored the group; `data-fw-livepaint` on a path refused (400). No page errors. One layout shift of 0.00425 when Make regrouped the Layers rows under the new group (the late-commit row class from v0.11.3).
- **Found while driving:** with the sources of a Live Paint group selected, the Appearance fill applies to the sources themselves (as for any selected path), not to a face; use the Live Paint Bucket for faces.
- **Not built:** edge painting per segment (Shift-click still strokes the whole path), gap detection in live groups (the bucket's gap option applies only to plain paths), adding paths to an existing group, selecting faces with the Live Paint Selection tool.

## Threaded area type · v0.11.4 (2026-10-17)

Edits in `vector/areatype.ts` (`layoutArea` also returns the words left over; `AreaSpec` gains optional `chain` (shape and text id pairs, at most 20) and `over` (words left after the last shape); new `relayoutChain` flows the words through the first shape and each linked shape in order and stores the overflow; `threadCommands` and `threadSelection` link the selected area type to the other selected closed shapes, each getting an empty "Threaded type" text after it with the same font; `releaseThreadCommands` removes the linked texts and rolls the words back; `areaTextFor` also resolves a linked shape or text; `areaEditCommands` and `areaFixes` work on the whole thread; `areaFixes` skips a text whose spec the same commit rewrites (the first Release run laid the text out twice, 50 lines instead of 25) and drops links whose shape or text is gone), `vector/editor.ts` (Delete or Backspace on a shape that holds area type is committed by the editor, so the thread is repaired in the same undo step; routing that repair through reconcile instead made undo reapply it), `vector/extras.ts` (`decorateOverflow`: a plus in a box at the bottom right of the last shape when words are left, with the Selection or Direct Selection tool), `styles/vector.css` (`overflow` overlay style), `vector/pathdialogs.ts` (placement stores the overflow), `vector/index.ts` (`vector.type.thread`, enabled with two or more items selected so the area type itself can be one of them, and `vector.type.thread-release`), `commands/catalogue.ts`, `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (6 strings, 1907 keys). Rust: `data-fw-area` accepts the optional `chain` and `over`.

- **Source basis:** select the area type and the objects, then Type > Threaded Text > Create; a plus sign in the out port marks overflow text: Art109 Miami ("Those Nasty Red Plus Signs") and Adobe Illustrator Smart Notes ("Type"); Release rolling the text back into the first object: Smart Notes only (single source, flagged). Remove Threading (keep text, drop links) is not built.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** a long paragraph in an ellipse: 25 lines, 298 words over, the overflow marker drawn; a rectangle below it, both selected, Thread text: the rectangle got a Threaded type text with 14 lines and the overflow fell to 146; Delete on the rectangle: one commit, link and text gone, overflow back to 298; one undo restored the thread; Release: 25 lines in the ellipse, no linked text; undo restored the thread. No page errors. Layout shifts were only Layers rows added by commits that arrived after the 500 ms input window (the class explained in v0.11.3).
- **Not built:** in and out ports to click on the canvas, Remove Threading, threading from one shape's text into several columns of another, reordering the thread.

## Edit area type, and the v0.11.2 open items · v0.11.3 (2026-10-17)
