Edits in `vector/areatype.ts` (new `areaTextFor` finds the live area text for a selected text, line or source shape; `areaOptionsOf` rebuilds the options from the stored spec and the text's font attributes; `areaEditCommands` updates the font attributes and spec and replaces the lines in the same shape), `vector/pathdialogs.ts` (Area Type has an edit mode: prefilled, button Update text, commits on the same text node; its note now says the text reflows), `vector/index.ts` (`vector.type.area-edit`, enabled only when the selection resolves to live area type), `commands/catalogue.ts` (both area entries describe reflow and editing), `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (the stale note replaced in place, 3 strings added, 1901 keys).

- **v0.11.2 Layers shift, explained:** recording every layout-shift entry with its `hadRecentInput` flag shows the same 0.00068 shift (the new "Area type" row pushing the rows below) in v0.11.1 too; there it landed 539 ms after the Place text click and was marked as near input, here 776 ms after and counted. Not a regression: any Layers row added by a commit that arrives more than 500 ms after the click moves the rows below it. Left as is; a general fix belongs to the Layers list.
- **v0.11.2 anchor drag, explained:** a Direct Selection drag on the right anchor commits both on a plain ellipse (614.59 to 711.32) and with area type present, and the area text reflows to four lines with centres following the wider shape. The failed run was the test script, not the editor. The canvas holds two tspans per line (the line and its text), so 10 tspans for 5 lines is expected.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** area type in an ellipse; Edit area type prefilled the text, size 16 and Update text; new words at size 20 gave four lines on the same text node; one undo restored the five lines at 16; the command is unavailable on a plain rectangle. No page errors; the only shift is the placement one above.
- **Not built:** typing straight into the canvas text, threaded text.

## Live area type reflow · v0.11.2 (2026-10-17)

Edits in `vector/areatype.ts` (`AreaSpec`, `areaAttr`, `readArea`, `areaFixes`; `areaCommands` takes the source shape id and stores `data-fw-area` {v, src, text, leading, align, inset, columns, gutter} on the text), `vector/pathdialogs.ts` (Area Type passes the shape id), `vector/editor.ts` (`areaFixes` joins `typeFixes` in every commit: when a command changes a source path's `d`, the text's tspans are replaced by a fresh layout from the merged path in the same batch; when the source is removed the spec is dropped and the text stays as placed). Rust: `text` accepts `data-fw-area`, checked for its eight keys, a valid source id, text up to 20000 characters and number ranges.

- **Source basis:** none new; area type was sourced in v0.9.9, and reflow keeps the text inside its shape as the shape changes.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** a paragraph placed in an ellipse (5 lines); moving the ellipse with the Selection tool moved every line by the same amount in the same commit (308.3/20.8 to 372.78/69.16); with Direct Selection the top anchor nudged down six times by 10 units (50.36 to 110.36): the paragraph rewrapped into four lines starting at y 129.16; each nudge was one undo step and undo stepped the lines back with the shape; a bad `data-fw-area` through the API was refused (400). No page errors.
- **Open:** one 0.00068 layout shift in the Layers list when the area type is placed (the new "Area type" row moves the 17 rows below it by 32 px). The v0.11.1 build, which inserts the same row, reported no shift in the same script; the reason for the difference was not found before the time cap. Also open from this drive: a direct-selection anchor drag on the ellipse did not commit in the script (keyboard nudging did), and Offset Path makes a new path, so it does not reflow area type.
- **Not built:** reflow for edits made only through `/api/batch` (no editor commit), editing the text in place, threaded text.

## Hex pattern tiles and editing patterns · v0.11.1 (2026-10-17)

Edits in `vector/pattern.ts` (TileType gains `hex-row` and `hex-column`, `TILE_TYPES`; `tileCopies` places hex tiles: Hex by Row is a tile 1.5 times the art height with the second row at half a tile across and 3/4 of a tile down, plus the same copies one tile up so overlaps wrap; Hex by Column is the same turned; the options are stored on the pattern as `data-fw-pattern`; new `readPatternOptions`, `copyCommands` (shared by make and edit), `patternEdit` (the selected shape's pattern fill, the art of its first tile, its stored options or a Grid at the current size for older patterns) and `repatternCommands` (replace the tiles, update size and options, keep the id)), `vector/pathdialogs.ts` (Pattern Options lists the hex types; the brick offset is enabled only for brick types; edit mode prefills the stored options and its button reads Update pattern), `vector/index.ts` (`vector.pattern.edit`), `commands/catalogue.ts`, `main.ts` (Layers no longer lists patterns and gradients at all; v0.9.10 already hid their children), `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (6 strings, 1898 keys). Rust: `pattern` accepts `data-fw-pattern`, checked for its eight keys, tile type and number ranges.

- **Source basis:** Hex by Row staggers tiles horizontally and Hex by Column vertically: Training Connection ("Pattern Madness In Adobe Illustrator") and SitePoint ("Killer Backgrounds with Illustrator's SVG Pattern Tool"); editing an existing pattern by double-clicking it to reopen Pattern Options: both. The 3/4 spacing is hexagon packing chosen here; neither source gives Illustrator's exact hex geometry.
- **Found while driving:** making a pattern gave a 0.00056 layout shift in the Layers rows, also present in v0.11.0 with the same value: the new pattern row pushed the rows below it. Paint definitions are no longer listed in Layers; the rerun has no shift. Also caught before commit: the Simplified Chinese hex labels first read "hexadecimal".
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** rectangle and circle, Make pattern with Hex by Row (offset field disabled): pattern 48.37 by 72.54 with five tiles at 0, plus or minus half a tile across and 3/4 down or up; Edit pattern reopened with Hex by Row shown and Update pattern on the button; Hex by Column: the same pattern id, 72.56 by 48.36, tiles turned; one undo returned to Hex by Row; a bad `data-fw-pattern` through the API was refused (400). Both fills rendered staggered on the canvas. No layout shifts, no page errors.
- **Not built:** a pattern editing mode on the canvas (the art is edited by remaking from new shapes), a Swatches panel to double-click, Hex tiles that clip the art to a hexagon.

## Artboard order and Rearrange All · v0.11.0 (2026-10-17)

Edits in `vector/artboards.ts` (new `reorderCommands`: Move up or down swaps a board with its neighbour board in the same parent by `moveNode`; new `rearrangeCommands` with `RearrangeLayout` and `RearrangeOptions`: the boards directly under the page root, in panel order, are placed from their top-left corner; Grid by Row fills rows of n, Grid by Column fills columns of n, Arrange by Row and by Column use one row or column; each column is as wide as its widest board and each row as tall as its tallest; Right to left mirrors the columns inside the same total width; the panel rows gain Move up and Move down and the panel a Rearrange button through a new constructor callback), `vector/pathdialogs.ts` (Rearrange All Artboards dialog: Layout, Columns (Rows for Grid by Column, disabled for the single row or column), Spacing, Layout order), `vector/index.ts` (panel callback; command `vector.artboards.rearrange`), `commands/catalogue.ts`, `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (11 strings, 1892 keys; Layout, Columns, Rows, Move up and Move down already existed).

- **Source basis:** four layouts, columns or rows, spacing and Move Artwork with Artboard: Michael Bullo ("Automatically rearrange all artboards in Illustrator") and Envato Tuts+ ("How to Move Artboards in Illustrator"); Layout order Left to Right or Right to Left: Michael Bullo only (single source, flagged); reordering with the up and down arrows in the Artboards panel: Laura Coyle Creative and Envato Tuts+. Move Artwork with Artboard is not offered: in FormWeb the art lives inside each artboard and always moves with it.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** three artboards 128.97 by 96.73, 193.45 by 161.21 and 96.73 by 241.81; Move down on the first row swapped the first two in document order (panel box unchanged); Grid by Row, 2 columns, spacing 20: 124.13/257.93, 337.58/257.93 (124.13 + 193.45 + 20), 124.13/439.14 (257.93 + 161.21 + 20); Arrange by Row, Right to left: 389.83, 240.86, 124.13 on one row (mirrored in the same 459.15 width), Columns disabled; one undo restored the grid. No layout shifts, no page errors.
- **Not built:** drag and drop reordering in the panel, a reference point other than top-left, rearranging boards nested in groups or across pages.

## Artboard marquee and arrow-key nudging · v0.10.9 (2026-10-17)

Edits in `vector/editor.ts`: the `board` gesture gets `marquee` (Shift held, started off any artboard), drawn with the existing marquee rectangle; new `marqueeBoards` selects every artboard whose screen box touches the marquee (the first as the selected one, the rest in `boardPicks`); new `nudgeBoards`, `flushNudge` and `holdNudges` with `boardNudge` (pending positions) and `nudgeTimer`: arrow keys with the Artboard tool move the selected artboards 1 px or 10 px with Shift in canvas px, the canvas elements move at once, and one commit goes out 300 ms after the last key (retried while another commit is in flight); after each engine update the pending positions are put back in a microtask, after the canvas has applied the state and before paint. The key guard lets arrow keys through while a commit is busy for the Artboard tool only (other tools still drop keys while busy, as before). `commands/catalogue.ts` describes both.

- **Source basis:** Shift-drag a marquee around artboards with the Artboard tool to select several: one Adobe Community answer ("How to move multiple artboards in Illustrator?"), single source, flagged. Arrow keys moving a selected artboard: only found on a content-farm page, which the sourcing rules exclude, so this is built as FormWeb's own nudge convention (the same 1 and 10 px steps as anchor nudging) and not claimed as Illustrator behaviour.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** two artboards; Shift-drag across both: two outlines, no handles; five ArrowRight, Shift+ArrowDown, then three ArrowLeft 150 ms apart: both artboards ended 2 px right and 10 px down in the engine (156.37 to 158.37, 478.79 to 480.79; 257.93 to 267.93); the left value sampled every frame went 156.37 up to 161.37 and back to 158.37 with the only decreases being the three ArrowLeft presses (no older engine answer shown); two bursts gave two undo steps and two undos restored the start. No layout shifts, no page errors.
- **Not built:** a marquee without Shift (a plain drag on empty canvas still draws a new artboard), marquee that adds to an existing selection, nudging with other tools while a commit is in flight.

## Move several artboards at once · v0.10.8 (2026-10-17)

Edits in `vector/editor.ts`: field `boardPicks` (artboards added with Shift-click besides the selected one); new `selectedBoards`; `downBoard` takes Shift: Shift-click on an artboard outside the set adds it and makes it the selected one, Shift-click on one inside a set of two or more drops it; a plain drag on an artboard in the set carries the others in the `board` gesture (`others` with their start positions), previewed on every one and committed in one batch (all put back if the commit fails); a click without a drag clears the set; `decorateBoard` outlines each artboard of a multi-selection with the `tbox` style and shows the resize handles only for a single one. `commands/catalogue.ts` describes it and no longer says new artboards go in the selected container (they go under the page root since v0.10.3).

- **Source basis:** Shift-click selects several artboards with the Artboard tool: Illustrator How ("2 Quick & Easy Ways to Move Artboards") and CreativePro ("Essential Tips for Using Artboards in Illustrator"); moving the selected ones together: Illustrator How only (single source, flagged).
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** two artboards drawn with Shift+O; click A, Shift-click B: two outlines, no handles; dragging A 70 by 50 screen px moved both from 478.79/156.37 px to 559.39/236.97 px left and 257.93 to 306.29 px top, screen boxes mid-drag equal to the committed ones; one undo restored both; Shift-click B again: back to one artboard with eight handles; a plain click then a drag moved only B. No layout shifts, no page errors.
- **Test harness note:** a select-tool drag that starts on the page's headline text stalls Playwright's `mouse.move` (in v0.10.7 too, with and without throttling) while the page keeps polling the server, which points to a native text drag rather than a frozen editor. Not changed; noted for test scripts (start drags on empty canvas or with a vector tool).
- **Not built:** marquee selection of artboards, arrow-key nudging of artboards, Shift-drag constraint while moving, resizing several at once.

## Freeform gradient Lines mode · v0.10.7 (2026-10-17)

Edits in `vector/freeform.ts` (optional spec keys `ln`, a line number per stop with 0 for a free point, and `sg`, the sample gradients; `freeformSamples` interpolates colour and spread between neighbouring stops of a line, one sample per 0.05 of the box and at most 12 a segment, 160 in all, from the stop positions only; `writeStops` keeps or creates sample gradients so the count is known when stops are written; `freeformGradient` now takes a point and colour; sample layers come before the stop layers), `vector/live.ts` (positions sample gradients, keeps them from the unused-gradient cleanup, release removes them), `vector/extras.ts` (`ffMode`, the open line `ffLine`; in Lines mode a click inside adds a stop on the open line or starts a new one; Escape closes the line; the overlay draws each line through its stops), `vector/panel2.ts` (Freeform draw: Points or Lines), `commands/catalogue.ts`. Rust: `data-fw-freeform` accepts `ln` (same length as the stops, 0 to 20) and `sg` (at most 160 ids, unique, not stop gradients); size limit 16384.

- **Source basis:** Lines mode draws a path of colour whose stops colour the points on the line: Adobe Learn ("Freeform gradients") and Lessons in Design (read for v0.10.6). Escape to stop drawing a line: Adobe Learn only (single source, flagged).
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** Freeform, G, Lines; three clicks made line 1 (`ln` 1,1,1) with 16 sample gradients and 24 layers; Escape, two clicks made line 2 with 28 samples and 38 layers in all, two lines drawn on the overlay; dragging the middle stop of line 1 moved its samples (first sample cy 0.8785 to 0.8889); Backspace removed that stop, line 1 kept two stops and the samples dropped to 24 with the unused gradients removed (33 nodes); Points then made a free stop (`ln` 0); undo stepped back one action at a time. One 0.00017 breadcrumb shift from Make (as in v0.10.6), no page errors.
- **Performance:** with 38 layers the interface took 2.7 to 4.5 s to show a new stop under CPU 4x (the engine answers sooner); each sample is a full copy of the path, so long lines on complex paths are heavy.
- **Not built:** clicking an existing stop to join it to a line, curved lines (segments are straight), a closed line, opacity per stop; sample layers are not previewed while a stop is dragged (they follow on release).

## Freeform gradient, Points mode · v0.10.6 (2026-10-17)

New `vector/freeform.ts` (spec `data-fw-freeform` {v, src, grads, pts, cols, sig}, `readFreeform`, `freeformSig`, `sourceBox`, `averageColor`, `freeformGradient`, `freeformOutputs`, `stopsOf`, `writeStops`, `defaultStops`, `turnHue`). Edits in `vector/live.ts` (LiveKind `freeform`; container names; a `liveFixes` branch that rewrites each stop's radial gradient and refreshes the layer paths when the signature changes and removes gradients no stop uses; `liveOf`, `liveSources`, `liveCopyOwner`, expand and release), `vector/ops.ts` (new `freeform()`; Expand and Release include freeform), `vector/extras.ts` (gesture `ff`; Gradient tool stops: drag, ring handle for spread, click inside to add, drag outside or Backspace to remove, double-click for the stop dialog timed by the tool since pointer events do not report a click count), `vector/editor.ts` (Backspace asks `deleteFreeformStop` first), `vector/panel2.ts` (Freeform in the gradient type switch; the selection check is guarded because the panel updates before the engine connects: the first build threw "The site engine is not connected" and the app stayed blank), `vector/pathdialogs.ts` (Freeform Gradient Stop dialog), `vector/index.ts`, `commands/catalogue.ts`, `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (4 strings). Rust: `formweb-model/src/vector.rs` allows `data-fw-freeform` on groups only, checks its shape (1 to 20 stops, unit coordinates, spread 0.02 to 1, hex colours, signature) and that `src` is a path inside the group, and accepts `data-fw-live="freeform"`.

- **Source basis:** click inside the shape to add a stop, Backspace to remove, a spread per stop, Points and Lines modes: Adobe Learn ("Freeform gradients") and Lessons in Design ("Using Illustrator's Freeform Gradient Tool in 6 Steps"). Dragging a stop outside the shape to remove it and double-clicking a stop for its colour: Adobe Learn only (single source, flagged). Initial colours are random in Illustrator (Lessons in Design); here they are deterministic hue turns from the fill.
- **Rendering:** SVG has no freeform or mesh paint. Each stop is a copy of the source path filled with an objectBoundingBox radial gradient (opaque at the stop, 60 percent at 0.35, clear at the edge; a vertical scale keeps the spread circular), drawn over the source, which takes the average colour as its base. Layers stack in stop order, so later stops cover earlier ones where they overlap; the blend is close to Illustrator's but not the same, and the source stroke is partly covered.
- **Found while driving:** `liveFixes` only scanned groups that existed before the commit, so the made group was filled in by a second reconcile commit and undoing that commit triggered it again (Make took two undo steps and the first undo left a broken state). New groups that carry `data-fw-freeform` are now scanned in the same batch; other live kinds keep their path.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** rectangle, Freeform in the Gradient panel: 5 stops, 5 gradients, 5 layer paths, base `#83838c`; G shows 5 stop handles; dragging stop 1 30 by 20 px moved it to 0.325, 0.325 and its gradient `cx` to 0.325; a click inside added a sixth stop and gradient; Backspace removed it and its gradient; dragging the ring handle set the spread from 0.5 to 0.3606; dragging a stop 400 px out removed it (4 stops, 4 gradients); double-click opened the dialog, `#00aa55` at 80 percent applied; editing the source path through `/api/batch` refreshed the layer path and the gradient aspect; export holds the gradients and 4 layer fills. Undo: add, delete, Make each one step, Make undone restores the orange path with no gradients left, redo brings it back. A path given `data-fw-freeform` through the API is refused (400). One layout shift of 0.00017: the breadcrumb gains the new "Freeform gradient" ancestor and the reused Path crumb moves right once (selection stays on the path; selecting the group instead gave an intermediate four-crumb state first). No page errors.
- **Not built:** Lines mode, opacity per stop, Shift-click to select several stops, a stop colour that tracks the Swatches or Color panel, stops kept when the path is reshaped beyond its box.

## Shift and Alt on artboard handles · v0.10.5 (2026-10-17)

Edits in `vector/editor.ts` only: the `boardsize` gesture carries `shift` and `alt`, refreshed from each pointer move; `boardSize` is rewritten as one computation: the dragged sides change by the pointer delta (twice with Alt), Shift keeps the starting width to height ratio (an edge handle drives the other side from its own, a corner follows the larger relative change), and the box is placed against the opposite side, or around the starting center with Alt or for the free axis of an edge handle. Minimum 1 px per side.

- **Source basis:** Shift keeps proportions from Logos by Nick ("Resize the artboard in Illustrator") and SoftwareHow; Alt or Option from the center from Logos by Nick only (single source, flagged).
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off), artboard 200 by 140 on screen with a rectangle inside:** Shift on the bottom-right corner, 60 by 10: 260 by 182 (ratio kept); Alt on the same corner, 30 by 20: 260 by 180 with the corner moved to 441, 316 (centered); plain right edge, 40: 240 by 140; Shift on the bottom edge, 20: 229 by 160 centered horizontally. The rectangle stayed at [521,376,80,60] each time; undo between runs restored the start. No layout shifts, no page errors.

## Artboard resize handles · v0.10.4 (2026-10-17)

Edits in `vector/editor.ts` only: Gesture `boardsize`; `BOARD_HANDLES` (eight handles, corners and edge midpoints); `render()` calls new `decorateBoard` when the Artboard tool is active; `down()` routes role `x-abh` to new `downBoardHandle` (the `x-` prefix is what gives overlay circles pointer events in `vector.css`; `Extras.down` ignores the unknown role and returns false); `move()` and `up()` route to new `dragBoardHandle` and `finishBoardHandle`; new `selectedBoard`, `boardSize`, `boardAttrs`; `ExtraItem` type imported from the overlay.

- **Source basis:** the keyboard cheat sheet read for v0.10.3 ("drag and re-size artboards" in artboard mode) and Dummies ("Resize by dragging the handles", read for v0.10.1). Resizing an artboard does not scale its art (Dummies: Width and Height resize the artboard; the art stays).
- **Behavior:** handles show on the selected artboard (an `svg` selected in the editor) while the Artboard tool is active, drawn with the existing `thandle` style. Dragging moves the matching edges in canvas coordinates (minimum 1 px); the preview writes `width`, `height`, `viewBox`, `left` and `top` on the canvas element and the same values are committed in one `node.update` on release (a failed commit refreshes). The viewBox origin and size change at the artboard's units per pixel, so the art keeps its place and size on the page.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** artboard drawn with Shift+O, a rectangle drawn inside it, artboard clicked: 8 handles at its corners and edges; bottom-right dragged 60 by 40 screen px: the frame went from 200 by 140 to 260 by 180 on screen, mid-drag equal to the committed size, `0 0 419.15 290.17`; top-left dragged 30 by 20: frame 230 by 160 at the new corner, viewBox origin `48.36 32.24`, left and top moved by the same canvas amount; the rectangle stayed at screen box [521,376,80,60] throughout; two undos restored `0 0 322.42 225.69` and the original screen box. No layout shifts, no page errors.
- **Not built:** Shift to keep proportions, Alt to resize from the center, resize cursors per handle, handles on several artboards at once.

## Artboard tool · v0.10.3 (2026-10-16)

Edits in `vector/editor.ts` (Gesture `board`; ToolId `artboard` on Shift+O in `SHIFT_KEYS`; no anchor points shown; `down()` routes to new `downBoard`, `move()` previews, `up()` to new `finishBoard`; the draft draws the new rectangle; new `boardAt` hit-tests artboards by the canvas element's bounding box), `vector/panel.ts` (tool appended last), `vector/icons.ts` (`artboard`), `vector/index.ts`, `commands/catalogue.ts`. No new strings ("Artboard" comes from the vendored formvis tables).

- **Source basis:** Shift+O for the Artboard tool from Illustrator How ("How to Move an Artboard") and a keyboard cheat sheet ("Shift-O opens artboard mode which allows you to drag and re-size artboards"); drawing a new artboard by dragging on the canvas from Dummies (read for v0.10.1). Moving by dragging an artboard from Illustrator How.
- **Behavior:** drag on empty canvas draws an empty svg at that rectangle under the page root (not the selected container: the first run placed it in the selected grid div at top -610 px; artboards belong to the page, as with the panel's New artboard); the root gets `position: relative` when static. Drag inside an artboard moves it: the canvas element's `left`/`top` are written during the drag as the preview and the same values are committed on release (so the canvas patch finds nothing to change); on a failed commit the element is put back. A click without a drag selects the artboard. Each drag is one undo step. Resizing stays in the Artboards panel.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** Shift+O activated "Artboard (Shift+O)"; a 200 by 140 screen drag made a 322.42 by 225.69 svg (canvas zoom 0.62) under `root` at 236.98 px, 257.93 px, selected in the inspector, drawn where the drag was; dragging it 50 by 30 screen px moved the element to 317.58 px mid-drag, the same value as committed; Mod+Z restored 236.98 px in the engine and in the canvas. No layout shifts, no page errors.
- **Not built:** resize handles on the canvas, moving several artboards at once, Alt-drag to duplicate, moving the art with the artboard as a choice (art always moves, since it lives inside the svg).

## Export all artboards · v0.10.2 (2026-10-16)

Edits in `vector/exportscreens.ts` (the format and scale loop moved into `queueFiles`, used by `exportScreens` and new `exportArtboards`; new `pageArtboards`; a third scope radio "All artboards on this page (n)" with the count set on open; the name field becomes "File name prefix" in that scope; `run()`, `open()` and `openExportScreens` accept no active svg when the page has artboards), `vector/index.ts` (Export for Screens is enabled when an svg is active or the page has artboards), `commands/catalogue.ts`, `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (4 strings, 1877 keys).

- **Source basis:** Adobe Learn ("Artboards basics"): Export for Screens exports selected or all artboards, renamed artboards keep their names in exported file names, and a prefix can be added to file names. Single source for the export behavior.
- **Scope:** artboards on the current page only, since export renders from the live canvas element (`buildSource`); artboards on other pages are not exported. Files are named prefix + artboard name (sanitized) + scale suffix; a repeated name gets -2, -3. The prefix keeps a trailing separator (the first version passed it through `safeName`, which trims dashes, so "web-" became "web"; found in the run and fixed before commit).
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** two artboards ("Hero art" and its duplicate) from the Artboards panel; the dialog showed "All artboards on this page (2)"; choosing it emptied the name field and relabeled it "File name prefix" without moving the dialog; prefix "web-", SVG and PNG at 1x and 2x: "Exported 6 files.", downloads web-Hero-art.svg, web-Hero-art.png, web-Hero-art@2x.png and the same three for web-Hero-art-copy. Switching back to Whole drawing kept the typed value. No layout shifts, no page errors.
- **Not built:** a range of artboards, artboards on other pages, a zip of all files (each file is a separate download, 200 ms apart).

## Artboards panel · v0.10.1 (2026-10-16)

New `vector/artboards.ts` (`boards`, `duplicateCommands`, `fitCommands`, `resizeCommands`, `ArtboardsPanel`); edits in `vector/index.ts` (panel built once with the editor's host and `ctx.select`/`ctx.pageRoot`, command `vector.artboards` in Window), `styles/vector.css` (`.fw-v-artboards`, `.fw-v-boardarea`, `.fw-v-boardlist`, `.fw-v-board`, `.fw-v-boardsize`; tokens only, scrollbar hidden), `commands/catalogue.ts`, `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (8 strings, 1873 keys; Close, Custom, Size, Width, Height, Select, Duplicate, Delete and Artboard come from the vendored formvis tables).

- **Source basis:** Adobe Learn ("Artboards basics") and Dummies ("How to Deploy Artboards in Illustrator CC") agree on the Artboards panel, naming, adding artboards, width and height, preset sizes, Fit to Artwork Bounds (Adobe: Fit to Selected Artwork), deleting, and exporting artboards with Export for Screens. Duplicating comes from Adobe Learn only. Neither gives the Shift+O shortcut, so FormWeb sets no shortcut.
- **Model mapping:** an artboard is any `svg` node on any page (searched from each page root; svgs do not nest). No new tag or attribute, so the Rust model, canvas mirror and cleaner are unchanged.
- **Actions:** rename is `node.update` name on change; Select calls `ctx.select`; Duplicate copies the svg subtree with fresh ids, rewrites `url(#id)`, `#id` hrefs and quoted ids inside `data-fw-*` JSON through the old-to-new id map (so a copied gradient fill points at the copied gradient), and places the copy 40 px to the right; Fit to artwork takes the union of path bounds (half the stroke width added) and sets viewBox, width, height and left/top so the art stays at the same pixels on the page; Resize sets width and height from the size fields and scales the viewBox at the current units-per-pixel (the art is not scaled, as in Illustrator); Delete is `node.remove`; New artboard adds `addSvg` under the page root at (40, 40) or to the right of artboards already directly under the root, and sets the root to `position: relative` when it has no position. Each action is one commit.
- **DOM:** the panel is built once and toggled by `hidden`; rows are `keyed()` and patched in place (`value()` skips a focused name field); the list sits in a fixed-height area so adding or removing rows never moves the size fields and buttons below (the first build grew downwards on Duplicate and was fixed before commit).
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** a drawn rectangle with a gradient fill added through the API; panel listed one artboard "326.42 × 229.69 · Home"; rename landed; Duplicate made "Hero art copy" at left 592.41 px with 5 nodes and fill `url(#lineargradient-...)` naming its own copied gradient with 2 stops; panel did not move; Fit to artwork set viewBox `2 2 322.42 225.69` and moved the frame by 2 px while the path's screen box stayed [318,193,322,226] before and after (checked with undo and redo); New artboard 800 by 600 made viewBox `0 0 800 600`; Resize 500 by 400 set `0 0 500 400`; Delete removed it and its row. One layout shift of 0.0007 (the Layers row of the first drawn rectangle), no page errors.
- **Not built:** drawing artboards with an Artboard tool, dragging artboard edges on the canvas, reordering and Rearrange All, artboard options for several at once, centering the view on an artboard (Select selects it), exporting all artboards at once (Export for Screens exports the active one). Fit uses path bounds only; text and symbol instances are not measured.

## Layers list without vector internals · v0.9.10 (2026-10-16)

`main.ts` `walk()` inside the Layers render: a `pattern`, `linearGradient` or `radialGradient` row is listed but its children are not (tile groups and paths, gradient stops), and `tspan` children of a text are skipped (area type lines). The parent row stays selectable; nothing else in the Layers code changed (rows are still `keyed()`, patched in place). The count beside Layers is the number of listed rows. `textPath` stays listed because type on a path is edited through it.

- **Why:** each commit that added many nodes inserted that many rows above rows already in view, so the visible rows below jumped: the throttled runs recorded CLS 0.0061 and 0.0177 for area type (8 and up to 60 line rows) and 0.0039 for Make pattern (7 rows). Those rows were never useful targets: lines, tiles and stops are rebuilt by their commands and cannot be edited one by one.
- **Driven again under CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off:** area type now records 0.0007 per command (the one inserted text row, the same value as drawing any new shape, which pushes the rows below it by one row), Make pattern records no shift; all area type and pattern checks gave the same results as in v0.9.9 and v0.9.7; the Layers list shows one "Area type" row and 32 rows for 89 nodes.
- **Still open:** a single inserted row still moves the rows under it by one row height. It counts as a layout shift only because, under throttling, the commit lands more than 500 ms after the click; unthrottled it falls inside the input window. Removing it would need the new row to appear without moving what is below it, for example by scrolling the list by one row height in the same frame when the insertion is above the first visible row.

## Area type · v0.9.9 (2026-10-16)

New `vector/areatype.ts`; edits in `vector/pathdialogs.ts` (`AreaTypeDialog`, `tn` import), `vector/index.ts` (`vector.type.area`), `vector/store.ts` and `ui/canvas.ts` (`tspan` in the tag sets, `dx` attribute, and a `tspan` node holds its text in an svg `tspan` the way `textPath` does, not an html span), `commands/catalogue.ts`, `native/crates/formweb-model/src/vector.rs` (tag `tspan` with x, y, dx, dy; child of `text`; may hold up to 500 characters of plain text like `textPath`; no children), `lib.rs` (tag list), `backend/app/assets.py` (`tspan`, `dx`), `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (12 strings, 1865 keys; Weight, Left, Center, Right and Leading come from the vendored formvis tables, which cover all 23).

- **Source basis:** Peachpit ("Working with Type" excerpt) and Envato Tuts+ ("Working with the Area Type Tool") agree that any closed path can hold area type, text takes the shape's outline, and Area Type Options cover inset spacing, first baseline and rows and columns; Tuts+ gives the overflow plus indicator. Peachpit says the text reflows when the container is reshaped; FormWeb lays it out once (no live reflow).
- **Layout:** the container's closed subpaths are flattened (`flattenSub`, 0.25); for each line the horizontal spans inside the shape are taken at the top of the line box, at 0.3 em above the baseline and at the bottom, intersected (even-odd crossings), clipped to the column, shrunk by the inset, and the widest span at least one em wide is used. First baseline is the inset plus 0.8 em (ascent approximation), descent 0.2 em. Words are measured with a 2D canvas context set to the chosen weight, size and family (so the browser's fonts decide widths; the canvas iframe uses the same family); greedy fill, a word wider than the line is broken by characters, line breaks in the text start a new paragraph and an empty line leaves a blank line. Columns split the shape's bounding box minus insets into equal widths with the gutter between; text continues in the next column. At most 400 lines; words left over are counted and reported as overflow. Output: one `text` (font-size, font-family, font-weight, fill, text-anchor from the alignment) placed right after the container in its parent, with one `tspan` per line at absolute x and y; one commit, one undo step.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** an ellipse, two paragraphs at 16 on 20 centered: 8 lines, all centered on the same x, each line's two ends inside the ellipse by `isPointInFill` in the canvas, the text right after the ellipse in its parent, export carried the tspans, Mod+Z removed text and lines. Twelve copies of the sentence: "Overflow: 66 words did not fit in the shape." Two columns at 12 on 15 with 14 copies: 33 lines in the left column, 24 in the right starting at x 320.3 (middle plus the 12 gutter). The engine refused a tspan with markup and a tspan inside a tspan (400). No page errors; CLS 0.0061 and 0.0177 from Layers rows inserted for the many new tspan nodes after each commit (the same input-driven row insertion as before, larger here because one command adds up to 60 rows).
- **Not built:** live reflow when the shape changes or the text is edited (run it again), editing the text in place, threaded text between shapes, rows, first baseline choices other than ascent, hyphenation and justification, vertical alignment.

## href only on use and textPath · v0.9.8 (2026-10-16)

`native/crates/formweb-model/src/vector.rs` `check_node`: the v0.9.7 line refusing `href` on a pattern becomes one rule for every vector element: only `use` and `textPath` may carry `href` (both were already required to point at a symbol or path inside the same svg). `href` is in the generic attribute list for every tag and only passes `safe_url`, so before this a gradient, path or svg accepted `https://...` or `#id`.

- **Callers checked:** in `frontend/src/vector` only `ops.ts` (symbol uses), `symbols.ts` and `typepath.ts` (textPath) read or write vector `href`; SVG import (`svg.ts` via `/api/vector/import-svg`) converts files into plain paths and never creates gradient nodes, so gradient chaining (`href="#other"`) in imported files is not affected. A site saved through the API with an `href` on another vector element would now fail validation; none of FormWeb's own tools write one.
- **Driven against the real engine:** `linearGradient` with `https://example.com/a.svg#g` refused 400, with `#g-other` refused 400, plain gradient accepted; `href` on the svg and on a path refused 400; Define symbol still made a `use` with `#symbol-...`; a `textPath` with `#curve1` accepted. cargo build and formweb-model tests pass.

## Make pattern and Pattern Options · v0.9.7 (2026-10-16)

New `vector/pattern.ts`; edits in `vector/pathdialogs.ts` (`selectField`, `PatternDialog`), `vector/index.ts` (`vector.pattern.make`), `vector/store.ts` (`pattern` in `VECTOR_TAGS` and `DEF_TAGS`, so its art is never an editable shape), `ui/canvas.ts` (tag in both tag sets, `patternUnits` and `patternTransform` attributes), `commands/catalogue.ts`, `native/crates/formweb-model/src/vector.rs` (tag, attributes x, y, width, height, patternUnits, patternTransform; child of svg, g and defs; children g and path; a definition for `url(#)` checks; `href` on a pattern refused), `native/crates/formweb-model/src/lib.rs` (tag list), `backend/app/assets.py` (see the backend log), `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (12 strings, 1853 keys).

- **Source basis:** Adobe's "Create a pattern" tutorial (Adobe Learn) and CreativePro ("Pattern Making in Adobe Illustrator") agree on Object > Pattern > Make, Grid as the default tile, Brick by Row with an offset, the tile size, Size Tile to Art and H and V Spacing; CreativePro gives offsets from 1/4 to 4/5. Brick by Column is FormWeb's mirror of Brick by Row and is not in either source; Hex tiles are not built.
- **Geometry:** the tile art is every selected shape except the last clicked one (`host.primary()`), which receives `fill="url(#pattern-...)"`. The art bounds give the tile origin (`x`, `y`, `patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse"`); Size Tile to Art makes the tile the art size plus H and V spacing, otherwise Width and Height. Grid holds one copy; Brick by Row makes the tile twice as tall with copies at (0,0), (offset*w, h) and (offset*w - w, h) so the wrapped half shows; Brick by Column mirrors that. Each copy is a `g` with a translate holding plain path clones (paint and geometry only; live effects on the art are not carried). Pattern, tiles and the fill are one commit, one undo step; the originals stay where they were, as in Illustrator.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** a small circle selected first and a large rectangle last; the dialog opened with Size Tile to Art on (Width and Height disabled, Brick offset disabled until a brick type is chosen); Brick by Row, offset 1/2, H and V spacing 10: the pattern landed with width 58.37 (48.37 + 10) and height 116.72, three tiles at the expected translations, the rectangle filled with it, the canvas drew offset rows of circles, the exported `index.html` carried the pattern, Mod+Z removed the pattern and the fill together. A batch adding `href` to the pattern was refused with 400 (it was accepted before that check was added: `href` is a generic attribute on every tag and passes `safe_url`). No page errors; CLS 0.0039 from Layers rows inserted for the seven new nodes after the commit (the same input-driven row insertion recorded in v0.9.3).
- **Known gaps:** no Pattern Editing mode and no way to reopen a pattern's options; no Swatches panel (the pattern lives on the svg and is used by the one shape); no Hex tiles; no Size Tile to Art follow-up when the art changes (the tile is a copy). Pre-existing, not changed: `linearGradient` and `radialGradient` also accept a generic `href` (an external URL passes `safe_url`); browsers do not load cross-document paint servers, but the model should refuse it as it now does for patterns.

## OpenType features in Typography · v0.9.6 (2026-10-16)

Edits in `native/crates/formweb-model/src/lib.rs` (`safe_style` PROPERTIES gains `font-variant-ligatures`, `font-variant-caps`, `font-variant-numeric`, `font-kerning`), `main.ts` (four `addStyle` fields after Family in the Typography section), `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (4 strings, 1841 keys).

- **Source basis:** MDN pages for `font-variant-ligatures`, `font-variant-caps` and `font-variant-numeric` (values and the OpenType feature each maps to, Baseline widely available since January 2020). One source per property; the W3C CSS Fonts 4 text did not return its property sections. Adobe's Illustrator OpenType panel page rendered navigation only, so this is the web platform's feature set, not a copy of that panel; FormWeb applies it to site text, not to vector `text` elements.
- **Why only the model changed on the engine side:** `safe_style` is the one style gate (node styles, breakpoint styles, extension styles, text preset overrides and prop targets all call it); the canvas `safeStyle` and the exporter pass any allowed property through, and values still go through `safe_css_value`. No keyword check per property, same as every other style.
- **Controls:** Capitals (`normal`, `small-caps`, `all-small-caps`, `petite-caps`, `all-petite-caps`, `unicase`, `titling-caps`) and Kerning (`auto`, `normal`, `none`) are selects; Ligatures and Numbers are full-width text fields because their keywords combine (for example `oldstyle-nums tabular-nums`). Keywords are CSS values and are not translated. Empty inherits, and breakpoint overrides work as for every other style.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** Headline selected from Layers; Capitals small-caps, Kerning none, Ligatures `discretionary-ligatures`, Numbers `oldstyle-nums tabular-nums` all landed in `GET /api/state`, the canvas computed style matched each value, and `GET /api/export` CSS carried all four declarations. A batch setting `font-variant-numeric` to `x}body{color:red` was refused with 400 "unsafe style". Mod+Z cleared the last value. No layout shifts, no page errors (the fields' screen position changed only because the inspector scrolled 1861 px to reach them).
- **Not done:** no feature preview showing which features the chosen font supports; `font-feature-settings` (raw tags such as `ss01`, swashes) is not allowed; vector `text` elements keep their own attribute list in `vector.rs`.

## Live Paint Bucket · v0.9.5 (2026-10-16)

Edits in `vector/extras.ts` (`livepaint` in `EXTRA_TOOLS`, a branch in `down()`, hover in `move()` and `decorate()` shared with Shape Builder, new `paintFace`), `vector/editor.ts` (ToolId), `vector/panel.ts` (tool appended last), `vector/icons.ts` (`bucket`), `vector/index.ts`, `commands/catalogue.ts`, `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (3 strings, 1837 keys).

- **Source basis:** Adobe's help page "Paint with the Live Paint Bucket tool" (body readable): the tool paints faces and edges of Live Paint groups with the current fill and stroke; K selects it; Shift while hovering switches to painting strokes; double-click floods through unstroked edges; triple-click paints all faces of the same color. Illustrator How ("How to Use Live Paint Bucket Tool"): groups are made with Object > Live Paint > Make. Neither page says what happens to fills when paths are edited.
- **What FormWeb builds instead of a Live Paint group:** no group object and no new attribute. With two or more paths selected, faces are the Shape Builder pieces (`gapInputs` with gap 0, then `regionPieces`), so they come from overlapping closed shapes; open lines crossing each other do not form faces (the Shape Builder has the same limit). A click adds the face as a new path named "Live Paint face" with the current fill and no stroke, moved directly under the lowest selected path; if an unstroked, unselected path in the svg already has the face's bounds (within 0.5), its fill is updated instead, so clicking a painted face recolors it. Shift-click paints the stroke of the whole clicked path, not one edge. The selection is kept after every click so the faces stay the same. K stays on the Knife (FormWeb's existing binding), so the bucket has no default key.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** a rectangle and an overlapping ellipse, both selected, tool run through `window.formweb.run('vector.live-paint-bucket')`. Hovering the overlap drew one `region-hover` overlay; clicking it added a fill-only path at index 0 (sources at 1 and 2) whose outline follows the rectangle edge and the ellipse curve; clicking again with the same color added nothing and made no revision; Shift-click on the rectangle set its stroke; three undos removed the stroke, the face and then the ellipse, one each. No layout shifts, no page errors.
- **Not built or not driven:** live reflow when paths change, Expand and Release (faces are already plain paths), double-click flood fill, triple-click same-color fill, per-edge strokes, Gap Options, recoloring with a different color (code path present, not driven).

## Blob Brush · v0.9.4 (2026-10-16)

New `vector/blob.ts`; edits in `vector/editor.ts` (ToolId `blob`, Shift+B in `SHIFT_KEYS`, no anchor points shown, the `paint` gesture starts for `blob`, its draft is the brush outline, `up()` routes to new `commitBlob`, double-click opens the options, `[` and `]` step the size, `commitNew` takes an optional paint override, new `blobScreenSize`), `vector/panel.ts` (tool appended last so menu groups keep their members), `vector/icons.ts`, `vector/index.ts`, `vector/pathdialogs.ts` (`BlobDialog`), `commands/catalogue.ts`, `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (7 strings, 1834 keys).

- **Source basis:** Envato Tuts+ ("How to Use the Blob Brush Tool in Illustrator") and CreativePro ("Using the Blob Brush in Illustrator") agree on Shift+B, bracket keys for size, an unstroked filled shape, merging where strokes overlap, Keep Selected, Merge Only with Selection, Fidelity and Size. Adobe's own Blob Brush page rendered navigation only. Not sourced and chosen by FormWeb: the blob takes the stroke color (fill when stroke is none), and it merges only with unstroked paths of the same fill color; neither source states a color rule. Angle and Roundness are not built (round tip only).
- **Geometry:** the fitted pointer path is outlined with `outlineStroke` at the brush size (round caps and joins) in SVG units. Merge candidates are paths in the active svg (up to 30) with stroke none and the same fill, optionally only selected ones; overlap is tested by `unionSubs([blob, target])` returning fewer subpaths than its inputs (bounding boxes first). The union replaces the first target in document order, the other targets are removed, fill-rule set to nonzero, one commit. With no active svg, or no overlap, the outline is added as a new path through `commitNew` with the override paint.
- **Selection:** Keep Selected selects the new or merged path; otherwise the editor selection is cleared after a new blob and left as is after a merge.
- **Driven (CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, slow network, cache off):** Shift+B activated the tool (palette button title "Blob Brush (Shift+B)"); a stroke crossing the left edge of an orange rectangle merged into it (still one path, left bound 2 to -104.68), Mod+Z restored the rectangle exactly; `]` raised the size to 25 with the status line; double-clicking the canvas opened the options dialog with that size and added no path; with no rectangle (undone) a stroke at size 40 made a new path filled orange with no stroke, about 40 units tall. No layout shifts recorded, no page errors.
- **Not driven:** Merge Only with Selection skipping an unselected path (in the run the rectangle was still selected, so the stroke merged), Keep Selected, Fidelity changes, Escape in the dialog.
- **Known limits:** a blob drawn past the svg's box extends the path outside the viewBox (shown because the svg overflows visibly) but the svg box is not grown; a click without a drag paints nothing; no pressure.

## Tweak and Attach objects to path · v0.9.3 (2026-10-16)

New `vector/tweak.ts` and `vector/objectsonpath.ts`; edits in `vector/pathdialogs.ts` (`shell()` takes an optional `onCancel`, new `TweakDialog` and `radioRow`), `vector/index.ts` (two commands), `commands/catalogue.ts`, `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (9 strings appended, 1827 keys). Driven in headless Chromium against the real engine with CDP CPU 4x, 400 ms latency, 50000 B/s down, 20000 B/s up, cache off, read back from `GET /api/state`, screenshots read.

- **Source basis:** Tweak options from Adobe's own help page "How to reshape objects using effects in Illustrator" (helpx, body readable): random inward and outward distortion, horizontal and vertical amounts as absolute or relative, anchor points, "In" and "Out" control points. Objects on Path from one source only (CreativePro, "Placing Objects on a Path in Illustrator"): first object at the start, last at the end, even spacing between centers, objects rotate with the path, pivot at one of nine points. Adobe's own Objects on Path page did not load. Single source, flagged.
- **Tweak (`vector.effect.tweak`):** applied to the path geometry, not a live effect (the editor's distort effects are destructive too). Relative is percent of the path's bounding box width (horizontal) and height (vertical). Each anchor draws three random offsets from a seeded generator (mulberry32, same as `distort.ts`, kept local): the anchor offset moves the anchor and its existing handles together; the in and out offsets move each handle independently, a missing handle starting at the anchor, which is what curves straight segments. The open path's first in handle and last out handle are skipped (they do not draw). Live corners are baked first (`applyCorners`), as in `ops.distort`. Preview drafts every selected path through `host.draft`; Cancel or Escape drafts the bases back and clears; OK commits one batch (one undo step) and leaves the drafts until the commit lands, so no flash of the old shape. The dialog is built once and toggled by `hidden`; the options persist for the session, not in storage.
- **Attach objects to path (`vector.path.objects-on-path`):** the path is `host.primary()` (the last clicked shape); the other selected shapes in the same svg are placed in selection order. Arc length is sampled 48 steps per curve, 1 per line. Open path: spacing total / (n-1), one object goes to the start; closed: total / n. Each object's bounding-box center lands on the path and the object rotates by the local tangent angle. One undo step. Not live: editing the path later does not move the objects, and there is no spacing or rotation handle and no pivot choice (center only). Groups and non-path objects are ignored.
- **Driven:** three 48 px squares on a sloped line: centers landed at the start (82.61, 405.02), the midpoint and the end (566.25, 195.44), each rotated by the line's angle; notice "Placed 3 objects on the path."; Mod+Z restored all three. Tweak: dialog opened, Preview changed the path in the canvas, the dialog box did not move, Escape restored the canvas and left the engine unchanged, OK turned the square into four curved segments in the engine. No page errors.
- **Throttled DOM result:** CLS 0.0007 over the whole session, one entry, sources `layer-row` elements while shapes were being drawn (rows inserted into the Layers list after a commit; input driven but outside the 500 ms window under 4x CPU). Not from this turn's code; the Tweak dialog, its preview and both commits produced no shift.

## Rectangle tool freeze · v0.8.9 (2026-10-16)

Edit in `vector/editor.ts` (`down`), one line added.

- **Root cause:** `down` returned early while a commit was in flight (`busy`) before it reached `event.preventDefault()`. A press on the overlay capture rect during that window was therefore not cancelled: the browser fired `mousedown` and started native selection or drag handling (`selectstart`, and `dragstart` when text was selected) on the overlay. The following pointer moves then ran against a native drag session that no script was driving, so the page was idle (not at 100 percent CPU) while Playwright's `mouse.move` never returned. A second press right after drawing the first shape lands inside that busy window, which is why it needed one shape just drawn and selected. Escape or a Selection tool click first avoids it only because the second press then no longer reaches this path in time. There is no loop in snapping, geometry or the overlay.
- **Fix:** `down` now cancels the press for the active tool (`tool !== null`, primary button) before the busy and gesture guards, so a press during a commit is swallowed without starting native selection or drag. Confirmed with event logging: before, the second press produced `mousedown` and `selectstart` or `dragstart` on `.fw-vo-capture`; after, only `pointerdown`.
- **Limit:** a hang with no pending commit was not reproduced in 100+ drags, so this is the one confirmed path to a native drag from the Rectangle tool.
- **Driven (real engine, latency 600 ms):** Rectangle, Ellipse, Polygon, Star and Pen each drew a first shape, a second away from it pressed immediately, and one overlapping it; every drag completed, page script evaluation stayed responsive, and `GET /api/state` path count rose by one each time.
- **Checked:** `tsc --noEmit`, `vite build` into a private outDir.

## Translator placeholders and live retranslation · v0.8.9 (2026-10-16)

Edits in `i18n/index.ts`, `i18n/keys.ts`, the 23 locale files (7 strings appended, all translated), `vector/panel.ts`, `vector/panel2.ts`, `features/wishlist/cms.ts` and `setup/i18n_check.py`. No version file was changed.
- **API:** `t(english, values?)` fills `{0}`, `{1}` tokens; a value may be a string, a number or `{ t: 'English' }` (translated when filled, for example `t('{0} ({1})', [{ t: tool.label }, tool.keys])`). `tn(n, one, other, values?)` picks one of two keys with `Intl.PluralRules` for the current language (category `one` takes the first key, everything else the second; values default to `[n]`). `t(english)` without values behaves as before. A format-only template such as `{0} ({1})` needs no key.
- **Live switching:** every string `t()` returns in a non-English language, and every string built with values in any language, is remembered in a bounded map (3000 entries, least recently used) as shown text to an English form plus a `redo` function that renders it in the current language. The page translator checks that map before its exact English match: a text node or a title, aria-label or placeholder attribute whose current value is a remembered string is adopted with its `redo`, and on each language switch (or later change) it is rewritten in place with `node.data` or `setAttribute`. No container is rebuilt and no node is replaced. Built in English or in German, switched to German, Japanese and back: same nodes, numbers kept, plural chosen for the new language.
- **Strings converted:** `All {0} corners of the selection`, `{0} anchor selected` and `{0} anchors selected` (panel.ts), `Variable width: {0} point` and `points` (panel2.ts, replacing the three-part concatenation), `{0} item in {1}.` and `{0} items in {1}.` (the CMS order note, which carries the collection name), and the tool bar titles `Pen (P)` through the `{0} ({1})` template. Static neighbours `No rounded-corner anchors...`, `Uniform width` and `Create a collection to order its items.` are now wrapped in `t()`. Other template strings found by grep (stock library, team library, brand kit, agent connect, AI writer, Step N, Studio summary) were left; they are not in the tables and mix user data.
- **Keys and locales:** 7 keys at the end of `keys.ts` (1525 in all) and 7 translations at the end of each of the 23 locales. In Czech, Polish, Russian and Ukrainian the one and other forms are the same neutral wording (a colon form such as `Выбрано опорных точек: {0}`), because only the `one` category is told apart. `es-MX` and `fr-CA` inherit.
- **Check script:** `setup/i18n_check.py` compares the sorted `{n}` tokens of every key with its translation in the 23 locales and in the regional override tables and reports each mismatch as a problem (first 20 per locale). It passes with 1525 keys.
- **Verified:** `tsc --noEmit`, `vite build`, headless Chromium against the real API: Direct Selection marquee gave `2 anchors selected`, `Alle 6 Ecken der Auswahl`, `2 Ankerpunkte ausgewählt`, the Japanese forms and back; the tool tips `Pen (P)`, `Direct Selection (A)` and `Curvature` followed de, ja and en with no reload, also when the panel was first built in German. The numbered label node stayed in the document.
- **Known limits:** plain `t()` strings whose translation is shared by two English keys retranslate from whichever was built last; the English form in `tn` uses English plural rules, so a count such as 0 or 21 may use the other key after a switch in a language with more forms. The Pathfinder titles and `Width profile:` still use their older whole-sentence keys and now follow a live switch through the same map.

Edits in `vector/panel.ts`, `vector/panel2.ts`, `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (22 strings appended, all translated). Method as in v0.8.4 and v0.8.6 (CDP CPU 4x, latency 400 ms, 50000 B/s down, 20000 B/s up, cache off, fresh context, layout-shift and removed-node observers, private static server in front of the real API). The Task A script drives each control and reads `GET /api/state` after every step to prove the action took effect.

CLS all and spec was 0.0000 on every step except: Alt-Unite 0.0035 all (input driven, layer rows arriving, spec 0), Make with Warp 0.0023 and Make with Top Object 0.0028 (same, input driven, spec 0), Make clip 0.0002 (input driven), Stroke Align mode 0.0001 all and spec (the breadcrumb current crumb moving as a crumb is added, the known 0.0001 from v0.8.6). Removed nodes were only keyed layer rows and breadcrumb crumbs that no longer exist (Expand, Release, arrowhead regeneration), no container was cleared and refilled (0 records with removals and additions together), dialogs stayed mounted and toggled by `hidden`. Flashes: none. No source change was needed for Task A.

The page was not at 100 percent CPU when paused; cause not found. Needs a look by whoever owns `editor.ts` gestures.

Visible text, titles, aria-labels, placeholders and select option text were collected in English and in each language and compared. Pathfinder Options, Expand and Release, Edit Contents, Edit Clipping Path, Stroke options and the warp fields were all translated.

Pathfinder button titles `X on the selected paths` (10 strings, built by concatenation): now `t()` of the full sentence, 10 new keys. Width profile titles `Width profile: X`: `t('Width profile:')` plus `t(label)`, key `Width profile`. The width note `Variable width: N point(s)`: keys `Variable width`, `point`, `points`. Result for German and Japanese: 63 and 65 fewer untranslated strings in the vector states.

The page translator does not retranslate a title that was built with `t()` after a live language switch, only strings that are whole keys.

## Envelope mesh, live Divide and Outline, Pathfinder effects · v0.8.8 (2026-10-16)

Edits in `vector/warp.ts`, `vector/live.ts`, `vector/ops.ts`, `vector/extras.ts`, `vector/panel.ts`, `vector/panel2.ts`, `vector/editor.ts`, new `vector/meshdialog.ts`, `ui/canvas.ts`, `native/crates/formweb-model/src/vector.rs`, `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (16 strings appended), the parity JSON. Each item was driven in headless Chromium against the real engine and read back from `GET /api/state`, with screenshots read.

- **Source basis, stated plainly:** Make with Mesh follows two secondary sources (Dummies and Creative Bloq, item 3 of `research/illustrator_verification_2.md` as given in the task brief; that file was not in this checkout, so it was not re-read). Live Divide and Outline and Pathfinder effects are UNVERIFIED against Adobe; they extend the v0.8.7 compound machinery by our own design. Nothing here is from Adobe documentation.
- **Make with Mesh:** Envelope and distort section gets Make with Mesh and Reset with Mesh. Both open `meshdialog.ts` (built once, toggled by `hidden`) with Rows and Columns, 1 to 50, default 4 by 4. The envelope is a `data-fw-warp` group in mode `mesh` with three more keys, `rows`, `cols` and `mesh` (flat x,y pairs of the (rows+1)*(cols+1) grid points, normalized to the bounding box of the sources, so moving the sources moves the mesh). The result samples each source segment, maps points by bilinear interpolation inside the cell they fall in, and refits with `fit.ts` (`meshMap`, `warpOutputs`). With the Direct Selection tool on a selected mesh envelope the grid and its points show as overlay handles (`x-mesh` role in `extras.ts`); dragging a point commits the new array and `liveFixes` regenerates the result (the shapes redraw on release, not during the drag). Reset with Mesh converts a live warp envelope (not Top Object) by sampling the warp function at the grid points (`meshFromWarp`), or re-grids an existing mesh. Expand and Release are the existing envelope commands.
- **Validation (vector.rs):** `data-fw-warp` allows mode `mesh` with 14 keys; rows and cols 1 to 50, `mesh` length (rows+1)*(cols+1)*2, every number within -10 and 10, mesh keys refused on warp and top; attribute length limit raised to the data limit for `data-fw-warp` (a 50 by 50 mesh is about 45 KB). Driven: 50 by 50 accepted; 51 rows, 0 rows, a short array, an out of range point, an extra key and mesh keys on a warp envelope refused with 400.
- **Live Divide and Outline:** Alt-click on Divide or Outline in the Pathfinder section makes a `data-fw-compound` with op `divide` or `outline` (validator and `readCompound` accept the two ops; the four older ops are unchanged). All results become generated paths (several pieces), painted by the Pathfinder rules (Outline gives fill none and a stroke from the object fill), respecting Pathfinder Options (precision, redundant points, unpainted removal). `liveFixes` regenerates when an operand changes; Expand and Release are the compound ones. Driven: Alt-Divide on three overlapping rectangles gave 7 pieces with the unpainted option off and 2 with it on (the fill-none rectangle owned the overlap), dragging an operand anchor with Direct Selection regenerated the pieces, Expand left plain paths, Release restored the three operands; Alt-Outline gave four stroked outline paths, Expand left them as plain paths.
- **Pathfinder effects:** new `data-fw-effect` on a group (`{v, fx: "pathfinder", op, sig}`, op any of the ten operations) and `data-fw-live` value `effect`. The group keeps its contents exactly as they are (the operand paths get no attribute changes); the group itself carries `visibility: hidden` and the generated result paths carry `visibility: visible`, so only the result renders and the markup stays pure SVG. Operands are all path children of the group, so adding or editing one regenerates the result (`effectSig`, `effectOutputs`, `liveFixes`). Pathfinder section: a Pathfinder effect select plus Apply effect (groups the selected paths, or uses the plain group of a single selected path, or changes the operation of a selected effect group), Remove effect (drops the effect and live result, keeps the contents) and Expand appearance (the compound Expand: result paths replace the group). Driven: Unite gave one result with operands untouched and the group hidden; Divide on the same group gave 3 results; Minus Back gave the front shape minus the back one; dragging an operand anchor regenerated; Remove effect left two visible paths and no live node; Expand appearance left plain result paths. The engine refused a bad op, an extra key and an effect group with one path with 400.
- **Fix found on the way:** the canvas attribute allowlist in `ui/canvas.ts` lacked `visibility`, so the v0.8.7 live compound shapes kept their hidden operands visible on the canvas; `visibility` is now rendered (confirmed in the iframe DOM, computed style hidden).
- **Strings:** 16 new strings (Make with Mesh, Reset with Mesh, two tooltips, Envelope Mesh, Rows, Columns, a dialog note, Select a live warp envelope first., Pathfinder effect, Apply effect, Remove effect, Expand appearance, the longer Alt-click note, two Select first notices) in all 23 locales; the older Alt-click note key stays in `keys.ts` unused. `python3 setup/i18n_check.py` passes.
- **Checked:** `tsc --noEmit`, `vite build`, `cargo build --release`, i18n check, parity JSON parsed. FW-094 evidence and gap updated (still Partial); there is no Pathfinder row, so none was added.
- **Open limits:** mesh cells are bilinear (straight cell edges), not Illustrator's mesh patches; no adding or removing mesh lines, no handles on mesh points; the mesh result does not preview during the drag; Reset with Mesh does not take a Top Object envelope; effects are Pathfinder only (no other Effect menu items) and apply to every path in the group; the effect and compound results ignore the Pathfinder Options in their signature, so a change of options shows only on the next regeneration.

## Pathfinder Options and live compound shapes · v0.8.7 (2026-10-16)

Edits in `vector/boolean.ts`, `vector/live.ts`, `vector/ops.ts`, `vector/editor.ts`, `vector/panel.ts`, new `vector/pfdialog.ts`, `styles/vector.css`, `native/crates/formweb-model/src/vector.rs`, `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 locale files (11 strings appended). Each item was driven in headless Chromium against the real engine and read back from `GET /api/state`, with screenshots read.

- **Source basis, stated plainly:** everything in this section comes from one secondary source, the Envato Tuts+ 2009 tutorial A Comprehensive Guide to the Pathfinder Panel (research/illustrator_verification_2.md item 1), not from Adobe documentation. The Options names, the Alt/Option-click behaviour of the Shape Mode buttons and the Expand button are taken from that tutorial. The default values (Precision 0.028 pt, Remove Redundant Points on, Divide and Outline Remove Unpainted off) and the accepted Precision range (0.001 to 100) were from memory or chosen here and are unverified. The tutorial describes a CS4-era panel; whether current Illustrator behaves the same was not checked.
- **Pathfinder Options dialog:** the panel gets an Options button (Pathfinder section) that opens a dialog built once and toggled by `hidden` (`pfdialog.ts`): Precision in points, Remove redundant points, Divide and Outline will remove unpainted artwork. Precision replaces the old fixed flattening and curve-fit tolerances in `boolean.ts` (flatten tolerance is the value, curve-fit tolerance twice it; the old fixed values were 0.04 and 0.08). Remove redundant points drops collinear points in results; stacked (zero length) points are always dropped. Unpainted pieces are filtered only for Divide and Outline, by the owning object having fill none and stroke none. Persistence is per viewer in `localStorage` key `fw.vector.pathfinder`, every read and write in try/catch (the options work without storage); it is not saved with the site. Driven: two overlapping rectangles with aligned top and bottom edges united to 4 nodes with the option on and 8 with it off; two overlapping ellipses united to 18 nodes at 0.028 and 8 at precision 5 (with redundant off); a fill-none stroke-none third rectangle with Divide gave 5 pieces (2 unpainted) off and 3 on; the values survived a page reload and the dialog reopened with them.
- **Live compound shapes:** Alt-click on Unite, Minus Front, Intersect or Exclude (plain click is unchanged and expands at once) groups the operands into a `g` carrying `data-fw-compound` (`{v, op, src, sig}`) with the operands kept in place and set to `visibility: hidden`, plus one locked `g data-fw-live="compound"` holding the generated result path, following the `data-fw-blend` pattern (`liveFixes` regenerates when an operand changes, by signature; `liveOf`, `liveSources`, `liveCopyOwner`, Expand and Release know the new kind). Result paint follows the Pathfinder rules (Unite, Intersect and Exclude take the front object, Minus Front the rear; Exclude is evenodd). Expand deletes the operands and the wrapper and leaves the result as a plain path; Release removes the result, restores operand visibility and ungroups. The Pathfinder section gets Expand and Release buttons that reuse the live Expand and Release commands. Driven: Alt-Unite gave the compound group, one result of 8 nodes and two hidden operands; rotating one operand regenerated the result; Expand left a single plain path; Alt-Exclude gave two rings with evenodd; Alt-Intersect gave a 4 node result; Release returned two visible paths. Operands must sit in the same group and not already belong to a live blend, repeat, brush, stroke or compound (sixteen operands at most).
- **Validation (vector.rs):** `data-fw-compound` is strict (exactly four keys, `v` 1, `op` one of the four, two to sixteen distinct ids that name path children of the group, 8 hex `sig`), only on a `g`, exclusive with the other live kinds; `data-fw-live` accepts `compound`; `visibility` accepts `hidden` and `visible`. Driven against `POST /api/batch`: bad op, extra key, duplicate source, bad sig, one source and a visibility of `nope` were all refused with 400, and a valid group with two operands was accepted.
- **Not done:** Pathfinder Effects (Effect menu, live on any object) are not built; Divide and Outline stay expanded; the options are not stored with the site; the live compound is not offered from the command palette (the commands stay plain).

## Warp styles, envelopes and clip edit modes · v0.8.7 (2026-10-16)

Edits in `vector/distort.ts`, new `vector/warp.ts`, `vector/live.ts`, `vector/ops.ts`, `vector/panel2.ts`, `vector/editor.ts`, `vector/arrange.ts` and `native/crates/formweb-model/src/vector.rs`.
- **Warp styles:** Arc, Arc Lower, Arc Upper, Arch, Bulge, Shell Lower, Shell Upper, Flag, Wave, Fish, Rise, Fisheye, Inflate, Squeeze, Twist, each Horizontal or Vertical, with Bend, Horizontal Distortion and Vertical Distortion from -100 to 100 percent. The list and ranges come from a single weak source (a third-party script vendor page) and are UNVERIFIED against Adobe; the per-style formulas are our own approximations in normalized box coordinates. Shapes are sampled per segment, mapped, and refit with `fitCurve` from `fit.ts`.
- **Live envelope:** Make with Warp wraps the selected paths in a g carrying `data-fw-warp` (mode, style, orient, bend, hd, vd, src, env, op, sig), keeps the originals hidden with opacity 0 (the original opacities are stored in `op` and restored by Release), and writes one generated path per source into a locked `data-fw-live="warp"` group. `liveFixes` regenerates it when the model or a source changes. Selecting an envelope loads its settings into the panel and changing them updates it. Expand removes the originals and keeps plain paths; Release restores the originals.
- **Make with Top Object:** the topmost selected closed path is the envelope; the bounding box of the others is mapped into it with a Coons patch built from the four outline arcs between the corners nearest to its bounding box corners. Live, with Expand and Release.
- **Validation:** `data-fw-warp` is checked in `vector.rs` (eleven keys, style list, orient, ranges, op list, signature, sources inside the group, env present only for top mode); the engine returned 400 for an unknown style and for bend 500.
- **Clipping masks:** Release keeps returning the clip as an unpainted path. Edit Contents selects the clipped artwork and enters isolation (the same mode as double-click on a clipped path); Edit Clipping Path selects the clipPath child and reshapes it with Direct Selection (`clipEdit` in `editor.ts`, Done editing or Escape leaves). Both edits persisted across a reload. Clipping behavior is UNVERIFIED against Adobe (no source could be read).
- **Checked:** `tsc --noEmit`, `vite build`, `cargo build --release`, `python3 setup/i18n_check.py` (1469 keys), parity JSON parsed. Driven in headless Chromium against the real engine: 20 warp renders, live edit, source edit, Expand, Release, Top Object, clip contents and clip path edits with reload.
- **Open limits:** no Make with Mesh; one generated path per source rather than one merged result; the envelope hides originals with opacity 0 so Release restores only a single stored opacity per path.

## Throttled DOM stability, newer panels · v0.8.6 (2026-10-16)

Edits in `vector/brushdialog.ts`, `vector/editor.ts`, `vector/panel2.ts`, `vector/brushpanel.ts`, `vector/tracepanel.ts`, `features/chat-pane.ts`, `styles/chat.css` and `styles/app.css`; no new strings. Same method and instruments as v0.8.4 (Chromium over CDP, `Emulation.setCPUThrottlingRate` 4, `Network.emulateNetworkConditions` latency 400 ms, 50000 B/s down, 20000 B/s up, cache disabled, fresh context, gzip from a private static server proxying `/api` to the real API, layout-shift and removed-node observers). The chat reply was streamed from a timed Server-Sent Events stand-in on the same server (11 events, 350 ms apart: bold, inline code, list, code fence, a tool row) so the real `claude` CLI was not needed. Every step reports all shifts and spec CLS (shifts without recent input), and each removed node.
- **Stroke options (dashes, arrowheads, align and preserve buttons), Free Transform mode strip, Transform fields:** CLS 0 and no re-render on every control, before and after. No change.
- **Blend and repeat (live settings, mode, steps, grid, rows, mirror, expand, release):** 0 re-renders (only keyed layer rows that no longer exist are removed); CLS 0.0001 per Blend or Repeat from the breadcrumb moving as a crumb is added, no change. Opening a section moves the sections below by design.
- **Symbols:** define 0.0077 and place 0.0003 to 0.0002 are the list row and layer rows arriving after the click (input driven, spec 0); Size and Rotation fields 0. Break link flashed: for about 450 ms the Symbols and Brushes lists collapsed (1 and 1 rows, then 0 and 0, then 1 and 1, CLS 0.0020, spec 0.0010) because `activeSvg()` is empty while the selected instance has just been removed. Fix: `Editor.panelSvg()` keeps the last active svg while the selection is empty or points at a removed node, used by the Symbols and Brushes lists; after: rows stay 1 and 1, CLS 0.
- **Brushes and Brush Options:** kind switch shifts (0.0022 to 0.0080) and Define brush (0.0017 to 0.0100) are fields and rows of the chosen kind appearing, input driven, spec 0. The dialog is built once and toggled by `hidden`; Preview, field edits, Cancel and OK moved nothing (CLS 0, no removed node). Before: the dialog could freeze the page. `schedule` assigned `this.running = this.pump()` but `pump` cleared `running` in its `finally` when it finished without awaiting (an edit that equals the applied settings), leaving a settled promise in `running`, so `settle()` looped forever on the next Preview off, Cancel or OK (renderer at 100 percent CPU, never returned, reproduced with and without throttling). Fix: `pump` no longer clears the flag; `schedule` clears it from `.finally` on the promise it stored. After: Preview off and on, Cancel, a second open and OK all complete.
- **Image Trace (progress bar, presets, worker run, Stop):** CLS 0 and the worker run placed the shapes with one keyed layer insertion; the progress bar and status line have reserved height. Before: the Source image select was emptied and refilled when the asset list arrived after focus (about 600 ms later under throttling, one re-render). Now the assets load when the section opens, and `group()` replaces only an option group whose content changed, so the first option and untouched groups stay; after: 0 removed nodes.
- **Average dialog, width dialog:** built once, `hidden` toggled, fixed position; open, OK and Cancel: CLS 0 and no removed node, before and after (no change needed). Opening the width dialog needed the Width tool double-click on a width point; Average needed two anchors from a marquee with the Direct tool.
- **CMS dialog New item flow:** New item, slug, values, save (three times), pick, New item again, pick, delete: CLS 0, 0 re-renders of the item select (only the deleted option is removed), before and after. Creating a collection moves the form below its new row (0.0007, input driven).
- **Steps titles:** typing and clearing titles, grouping and removing: titles section appears below the rest and the inputs keep their nodes (keyed); CLS 0. Group into steps 0.0036 (input driven, element rows leave the list). Known, not changed: Remove steps lets the element rows return above the buttons (0.0013 spec, 131 px), data that the list shows again.
- **Chat pane streaming:** CLS 0 and the log keeps its nodes. Before: every delta cleared and rebuilt all inline nodes of the open paragraph (`code` and `strong` recreated, selection lost), the code fence text node was replaced, and the send button icon was recreated at the start and end of every reply (2 per reply). After: `inline()` patches children by position and replaces a node only when its kind changes, code fences update their text node, and the send and stop icons both stay in the button and toggle `hidden`; removed nodes during the stream: 0 (before 3 per reply).
- **Status bar:** the revision label grew from 156 to 163 px when digits were added (shift 0.00004 on every commit); `min-width` raised from 150 to 240 px, after: no shift.
- **Known, not changed:** list rows arriving after a response (layer rows when a brush, symbol, form or repeat adds nodes, 0.0002 to 0.0056, a new symbol row 0.0056) and the breadcrumb moving as a crumb is added (0.0001); these are the content the person asked for, not reserved space.
