## Stroke options: align, variable width, corner dashes · v0.8.6 (2026-10-16)

Edits in `vector/strokefx.ts` (geometry), `vector/strokecmd.ts`, `vector/live.ts`, `vector/stroke.ts` (`alignFixes`) and parity row FW-159; no new strings (the refusal string `Remove the variable width before adding dashes or arrowheads.` is no longer used and stays in the catalogue), no engine change (`vector.rs` already accepts these specs). Driven in headless Chromium against the real engine (own API on port 8264), read from `GET /api/state` and zoomed screenshots.
- **Align inside and outside:** generated parts now copy the source's `clip-path` and use the doubled `stroke-width` (before they were drawn centred at the plain weight and without the clip). Driven: a rectangle at weight 1 with Inside gave parts at `stroke-width` 2 with `url(#fwa-...)`; Center removed both on the next regeneration. Outside on a filled shape has no clip (plain strokes use `paint-order: stroke`), so the generated group is placed before the source and the fill covers the inner half; `liveFixes` moves the group when the align changes (`strokeUnder`). Arrowheads on an open path with Inside carried the same clip and stroked heads the doubled width. `alignFixes` no longer removes a clipPath another node still references, so Expand keeps the parts clipped.
- **Variable width with dashes and arrowheads:** the refusal is gone. `widthSpec` reads `data-fw-width`; `widePart` cuts the profile along the path length into filled ribbon pieces (profile sampled by arc length on the bezier track, miter, round or bevel join and cap from the path, miter limit), one `path` per sub-path with every dash as a sub-path, colour from the width. Preserve cuts the dash pattern from the start; Align uses the same corner aligned spans as plain strokes. Arrowheads are sized by the width at each end (minimum 1) and Place arrow tip trims by that head length. The Width tool's outline sibling is hidden with `opacity` 0 while the group exists (`liveFixes` keeps it hidden, Remove restores it, Expand removes it). Driven: Bulge zigzag gave 24 pieces, Align 25; a rectangle with Width tool points gave 41 and 40; anchor drag, Width tool drag, undo and Remove regenerated or restored.
- **Aligned dashes at corners:** `dashSpans` places dashes by arc length per run (scaled by L / (n * period), centred on corners and ends), `joinSpans` merges the two half dashes at a corner (and at the seam of a closed path) and `extract` cuts the bezier track, so each corner dash is one open sub-path and the path's line join applies; the generated path holds all dashes (no dasharray). Driven: `M119.16 7.68 L122.91 3 L126.66 7.68` on a zigzag at weight 12.
- **Known limits:** in a variable width path the join is built on the offset ribbon, not by the browser; align is not applied to variable width paths; on open paths Inside and Outside clip to the implicitly closed region as plain strokes do; Preserve with a variable width and no arrowhead still writes filled pieces.
- **Checked:** `tsc --noEmit`, `vite build` into a private outDir, `python3 setup/i18n_check.py`, parity JSON parse.

## Stroke options and Average · v0.8.5 (2026-10-16)

New `vector/strokefx.ts` (model, validation, geometry), `vector/strokecmd.ts` (commands), `vector/strokepanel.ts` (panel section), `vector/averagedialog.ts`; edits in `vector/live.ts`, `vector/ops.ts`, `vector/panel.ts`, `vector/panel2.ts`, `vector/index.ts`, `vector/edit.ts`, `styles/vector.css`, native `formweb-model/src/vector.rs`, i18n (32 keys, all 23 locales translated), parity rows FW-083 (evidence) and new FW-159. `canvas.ts` and `assets.py` needed no change: `data-fw-*` attributes are skipped by the canvas and are not in the SVG import allowlist. Sources: `research/illustrator_verification_2.md` items 2 and 4, secondary sources only (CreativePro, a Wiley For Dummies excerpt, Envato Tuts+, Astute Graphics), not Adobe documentation. Every item was driven in headless Chromium against the real engine (own API on port 8263) and read from `GET /api/state` and screenshots.
- **Caps, joins:** the panel already had Butt, Round and Square caps, Miter, Round and Bevel joins and Miter limit; the cap button is now labelled Projecting (still `stroke-linecap` square). Driven: Projecting gave `square`, Bevel gave `bevel`, Miter limit 8 gave `stroke-miterlimit` 8.
- **Dashes:** a Stroke options section has Dashed line and three Dash and Gap pairs; a Gap left empty takes the dash value (10 and empty gave `10 10`; three pairs gave `10 10 4 8 2 3`). With Preserve exact dash and gap lengths and no arrowhead the pairs are plain `stroke-dasharray` on the path. Align dashes to corners and path ends, adjusting lengths to fit, wraps the path in a `g` with `data-fw-stroke` (JSON: v, src, dash, mode, a0, a1, s0, s1, tip, sig) and one `data-fw-live=stroke` group, following the brush pattern: the source path stays editable with `stroke-opacity` 0 (colour, weight, cap and join still come from it), and `liveFixes` regenerates the parts when the path or the spec changes (the signature ignores `stroke-opacity`). Align splits each sub-path at path ends and at anchors where the tangent turns by more than 2 degrees, then gives each run `stroke-dasharray` scaled by L / (n * period) with n = round(L / period) and `stroke-dashoffset` of half the first dash, so a dash is centred on every corner and end. Driven: a three segment zigzag gave three parts with scale 0.954 and offset 4.774; a rectangle gave four parts. The Preserve button on a wrapped path returns the unscaled array.
- **Arrowheads:** none, simple arrow, filled triangle, open triangle, circle, square, bar, per end with Scale %, and Extend arrow tip beyond end of path (base on the end point) or Place arrow tip at end of path (tip on the end point, body trimmed by the head length with a bezier split). Heads are generated `path` elements (no markers), oriented by the end tangent from the end segment, filled or stroked in the stroke colour, size 4 times the weight at 100 percent. Driven: all seven shapes rendered; tip at end moved the body ends to (14.31 141.57) and (441.34 14.31) with the heads on the real end points; dragging an end anchor regenerated body and heads; Remove dashes and arrowheads (the existing release path, extended in `live.ts` for the new kind) restored one plain path, Expand turns the parts into ordinary paths. A Width tool button in the section opens the Width tool; the width profile presets stay in Stroke align and width.
- **Model:** `vector.rs` allows `data-fw-stroke` (10 keys, dash at most six non-negative numbers in pairs, mode, arrow and tip enums, scale 1 to 1000, `src` a non-generated path child of the group) and `data-fw-live=stroke`; one of blend, repeat, brush or stroke per group. Eight malformed specs were refused with 400; wrapping is one revision and one undo removes it.
- **Average dialog:** Average (panel button and `Mod+Alt+Shift+A`) opens a dialog with Horizontal, Vertical and Both axes (default Both). Horizontal moves the anchors' y to their mean, Vertical their x, Both to one point; no join. Driven on a joined path: Horizontal gave y 107.92 on three anchors, Vertical x 117.92, Both one point. Join and average end anchors (`Mod+Alt+Shift+K`) is unchanged.
- **Join:** two open end points that are apart already gave a straight segment and moved nothing (driven: ends at 520,300 and 560,350 gave `L167.9 77.94` between them); `joinEndpoints` now also clears a stray end handle so the segment is always straight.
- **Rapid edits:** `Ops.strokeFx` used to build its commands from the site at call time, so a second change fired before the first commit returned was built from the old state and replaced the first (wrapping twice, or writing the old spec back). It now chains each call on a promise queue and builds its commands when its turn comes, from the state the previous commit left; no DOM is touched. Driven: dash 9, End arrowhead Filled triangle and Scale end 150 dispatched in one synchronous script produced one `data-fw-stroke` with dash [9,9], a1 filled, s1 150, and two generated parts, in `GET /api/state`.
- **Known limits:** stroke align inside and outside is not applied to the generated parts; arrowheads are not drawn on closed paths; a path with a variable width cannot also take dashes or arrowheads.

## Throttled DOM stability · v0.8.4 (2026-10-16)

Edits in `styles/terminal.css`, `styles/app.css`, `styles/shell.css`, `styles/stock-library.css`, `styles/ai-writer.css`, `styles/features.css`, `features/terminal.ts` and `main.ts`; no new strings. Verified under throttling: Chromium over CDP with `Emulation.setCPUThrottlingRate` 4 (cold loads also at 6) and `Network.emulateNetworkConditions` latency 400 ms, download 50000 B/s, upload 20000 B/s, cache disabled, a fresh context per run (hard load), gzip served from a private static server that proxies `/api` to the real API. Instruments: `layout-shift` observer (CLS with and without input-excluded shifts, shifted nodes with rects), MutationObserver on the document in the editor and every iframe (removed and re-added subtrees), per frame sampling of html and body background, `data-theme`, `lang`, font faces and visible text, and a CDP screencast during loads. Pointer and keyboard actions are excluded from the spec CLS by Chrome, so each step also reports the all-shifts value.
- **Cold load, English, Japanese, light and sand themes, 4x and 6x:** CLS 0, no theme flash (background correct in the first sample, `data-theme` set before body), the app stays hidden until ready so no English appears before Japanese, no re-render. Before: a tiny shift of the dock status text when restored sessions added tabs after first paint (CLS 0.0005 to 0.0006). After: 0.
- **Dock (Mod+J), Claude and Chat panes:** before the status label shifted (CLS 0.0001) when a session reported; after, no shift besides the dock panel itself growing when the person opens it (0.028 all-shifts, input driven, nothing else moves). The terminal pane is xterm.js: its own row and cursor nodes are replaced by xterm and are not ours.
- **Inspector tabs and layer selection:** no panel rebuild; tab switches 0 mutations, selection changes patch keyed breadcrumbs and layer rows only; CLS 0. Inserting a grid or deleting a node shifted the inspector fields (CLS 0.0007) because a field label row grew when the Reset chip appeared; the label row now reserves the chip height, after: 0.
- **Dialogs, Studio tabs, Creative tools, command search (Mod+K):** panes are persistent and toggled by `hidden`; CLS 0 and no rebuild, except two found: Stock library (0.0025, the count line above the icon grid was empty until the catalog arrived) and the site generator (0.0148, the status note grew the dialog from 369 to 417 px). Both now reserve their line space; after: 0. Search results patch keyed rows.
- **Agent Connect pane:** before CLS 0.0200 (client cards, approvals and activity arrived after the pane opened). The pane data is now fetched once after the system call at startup, so the first open shows filled sections and polling patches in place; after: 0.
- **Vector tools and panel:** drawing with rect, ellipse, pen, star and line, tool changes and path selection: 0 re-renders and CLS 0 (one new layer row insertion 0.0004). Opening Appearance, Gradient, Stroke and Corners sections moves the sections below by design (the accordion, 0.005 to 0.034 when toggled by script, excluded when clicked); nothing is rebuilt.
- **Breakpoints and language:** breakpoint buttons and the select re-render nothing; the toolbar readout shifted with its text (0.0078); it now has a fixed width, after: only the centred canvas frame resizes (0.005 to 0.07, the canvas width changes by design). Live language switch from Settings translates text nodes in place with 0 re-renders in one paint; text length changes still reflow neighbours (0.011 to 0.039, same before and after) because widths follow translated text.
- **Undo, redo, edit, preview:** undo and redo (menu command and keyboard) and style edits produced 0 removed nodes in the canvas iframe and CLS 0. The compiled preview iframe laid out at a collapsed width while display none, then jumped (CLS 0.77 to 0.83 inside the frame); the preview wrapper and its frames now stay laid out with `visibility: hidden` instead of `display: none`, after: 0 on first, repeated and refreshed previews.
- **Fixes:** terminal status label moved after the spacer with a fixed basis and right alignment, JetBrains Mono requested in the dock constructor; toolbar readout `min-width` and right alignment; `.field-label` `min-height` 18px; stock and generator reserved lines; preview kept laid out; Agent Connect prefetch (one call in `main.ts`).
- **Known, not changed:** panel collapse and dock growth animate the stage so the centred canvas frame moves in more than one frame (0.11 and 0.15 all-shifts, user driven); xterm.js internals.
- **Checked:** `tsc --noEmit`, `vite build` into a private outDir, `python3 setup/i18n_check.py`.

## Bristle brush, Brush Options dialog · v0.8.4 (2026-10-16)

New `vector/brushdialog.ts`; edits in `vector/brush.ts`, `vector/brushcmd.ts`, `vector/brushpanel.ts`, `vector/live.ts`, `vector/icons.ts` (sliders icon), `styles/vector.css`, native `formweb-model/src/vector.rs`, backend `mcp_catalog.py`, i18n (17 keys, all 23 locales translated; es-MX and fr-CA inherit), parity row FW-158. Driven in headless Chromium against the real engine (own API on port 8262, port 8252 was held by a process outside my view) and read from `GET /api/state` and screenshots.
- **Bristle:** kind `bristle` with `bshape` (round, flat, fan, angle), `size` (brush width), `blen` 5 to 100 (bristle length %, how much of the path each bristle may cover; the rest is trimmed at random ends), `bdens` 1 to 100 (coverage %), `bthick` 0.1 to 50 (stroke width of one bristle), `bopac` 5 to 100 (stroke opacity), `bstiff` 0 to 100 (100 keeps bristles parallel to the path, lower adds a seeded sine wobble), plus the existing `seed`. Bristle count is `size * density / 100 / thickness`, 2 to 200 per sub-path; each bristle is a round-capped polyline `d` only (no `data-fw-vector`, to keep the batch small) offset along the path normal with a per-bristle jitter; round shape shortens outer bristles at both ends, angle trims one side diagonally, fan widens along the path. All randomness comes from mulberry32 on the seed. Output stops at `BRUSH_LIMIT` 3000; `needsArt(kind)` replaces the old calligraphic checks in `live.ts` and `brushcmd.ts`.
- **Model:** `data-fw-brush` and `data-fw-brushdef` gain 6 keys (27 and 23). `vector.rs` accepts the old 21 and 17 key forms and the extended forms (all six present and in range, `kind` bristle only with them, bristle groups need no art); `settingsOf` fills defaults for old brushes, so an old brush regenerates once on the next commit because the signature now covers the new keys.
- **Brush Options:** each row in the Brushes section has an Options button (sliders icon) after the name; double-clicking the name opens the same dialog (the first click still applies the brush). `BrushDialog` is one persistent popover built on first use (`fw-v-dialog fw-v-brushdlg`, `z-index` from `--z-popover`) that shows only the rows of the brush kind (`SHOWN`), toggled by `hidden` when it opens. The paths using a brush are the `data-fw-brush` groups whose stored settings equal the definition. Preview (on by default) commits the change to those groups as you edit, serialised so only one commit is in flight; Cancel and turning Preview off undo exactly the preview commits made (only if the revision is still the last preview's, and no-change edits are not committed); OK undoes the previews and then commits the definition and every group in one batch, so a single undo restores all. The panel is not rebuilt and no row moves.
- **Driven:** Paintbrush with a Bristle brush made 14 bristle paths per stroke (size 24, density 60); seed 8 changed the first path and seed 7 gave it back exactly; 20 sub-paths at size 400, density 100 gave 3000 paths in one batch and 200 for a short path; the dialog showed the 8 bristle fields; with two brushed paths, a preview of size 40, density 90, opacity 30 gave 36 bristles on each, Cancel restored both groups and the definition, OK applied size 40, length 50, Fan to both and the definition, and one undo restored all; panel row elements were the same nodes and dx, width and height of every section were 0 while the dialog was open; Remove brush restored stroke and weight, Expand left plain paths.
- **Known limits:** if a source path is edited outside the editor, undoing the preview steps leaves a stale brush that the editor regenerates with one extra commit; two brushes with equal settings both match the same groups.
- **Strings:** 17 keys appended (Bristle, Round point, Flat, Fan, Angled, Shape, Bristle length %, Bristle density %, Bristle thickness, Paint opacity %, Stiffness %, Brush Options, Open the options of this brush, Changes apply to every path that uses this brush., Preview, OK, Cancel).
- **Checked:** `tsc --noEmit`, `vite build` into a private outDir, `cargo build --release`, `compileall` (pycache removed), `python3 setup/i18n_check.py`, parity JSON loads (115 rows).

## Uncapped brushes and blends, Paintbrush, blend spine ends · v0.8.3 (2026-10-16)

Edits in `vector/brush.ts`, `vector/brushcmd.ts`, `vector/brushpanel.ts`, `vector/live.ts`, `vector/editor.ts`, `vector/panel.ts`, `vector/panel2.ts`, `vector/ops.ts`, `vector/icons.ts`, `vector/index.ts`, native `formweb-engine/src/lib.rs` and `formweb-model/src/vector.rs`, backend `app/main.py`, i18n (4 keys, all 23 locales translated), parity rows FW-093 and FW-158. Driven in headless Chromium against the real engine and read from `GET /api/state`.
- **Truncation:** the 150 path cap existed because `POST /api/batch` took 200 commands (`Batch.commands` max_length 200 in `main.py`; the engine allowed 500). Nested children are not allowed in one `node.add` (the engine requires no children on a new node) and a compound path would lose per-step colours, so the limits were raised instead: engine `batch` to 4000 commands, `Batch` to 4000, and `vector.rs` blend `steps` to 500. The size guard is unchanged and already matches the engine: the request middleware rejects bodies over `MAX_DOCUMENT_BYTES` (8 MB) and `validate` caps a site at 10000 nodes. The MCP `apply_commands` tool keeps its own 200 limit for agent payloads. Frontend caps are now `BRUSH_LIMIT`, `BLEND_LIMIT` and `COPY_LIMIT` at 3000 (a safety bound under the batch limit); blend steps per pair go to 500 in the panel, `readBlend`, `blendCount` and `ops.blend`. Driven: a Scatter brush on a long zigzag gave 958 copies in one revision and one undo removed the group; a 300 step blend gave 300 children in one revision and one undo removed it.
- **Paintbrush:** tool `paintbrush` (key B, command `vector.paintbrush`, free in the registry and in `TOOL_KEYS`) collects the drag, fits it with `simplifyPolyline` and `fitCurve` from `fit.ts` at a tolerance of Fidelity (Brushes section, default 2.5) times the pixel size, and calls `commitNew(..., brush)`. With a brush (`editor.activeBrush`, set by Apply and Define), `brushedPathCommands` writes the group, the source path with stroke none, and the generated strokes in the same batch, so the path and the live brush are one undo step (revision +1). Without a brush it creates a plain stroked path. Driven: plain path (5 curve pieces from 61 pointer moves), brushed path (group with 52 scatter copies, one revision), undo.
- **Blend spine:** Replace spine moves the first and last source by the offset that puts their bounding centres on the spine start and end, through `moveCommands`, and stores the applied offsets as `home` (4 numbers) in `data-fw-blend`; `readBlend` carries it and Rust accepts the 11 key form only with a valid `home`. Replacing the spine again computes from the restored centres; Remove spine (`removeSpineCommands`) moves both sources back by `home` and drops it. Middle sources of a three or more path blend are not moved. Driven: sources moved to the ends of a four point spine in one revision and Remove spine restored both source bounds exactly. If the spine path is deleted by hand the sources stay where they were moved.
- **Checked:** `tsc --noEmit`, `vite build` into a private outDir, `cargo build --release`, `compileall` (pycache removed), `python3 setup/i18n_check.py`, parity JSON loads.

## Brushes, symbol transforms, multi-blend · v0.8.2 (2026-10-16)

New `vector/brush.ts` (specs, validation, geometry), `vector/brushcmd.ts` (define, apply, spine commands), `vector/brushpanel.ts` (Brushes, Blend along a spine and Symbol instance sections); edits in `vector/live.ts`, `vector/symbols.ts`, `vector/extras.ts`, `vector/editor.ts` (instance move follows the instance transform), `vector/ops.ts`, `vector/panel2.ts`, `vector/index.ts`, `styles/vector.css`, native `formweb-model/src/vector.rs`, i18n (50 keys), parity rows FW-093, FW-156 and new FW-158. `canvas.ts` and `assets.py` needed no change: `data-fw-*` attributes are skipped by the canvas and are not in the SVG import allowlist. Every item was driven in headless Chromium against the real engine and read from `GET /api/state` and screenshots.
- **Brush model:** a brush definition is a `symbol` in the svg's `defs` with `data-fw-brushdef` (17 keys) and, for Scatter, Art and Pattern, the artwork paths inside it; it is hidden from the Symbols list. Applying wraps the source path in a `g` with `data-fw-brush` (21 keys: the definition plus `src`, `color`, `weight`, `sig`) and one generated group `data-fw-live="brush"`. The source stays selectable and editable, its stroke is set to none and its colour and weight are remembered; `liveFixes` regenerates the output when the path, the artwork or the settings change (sig is an FNV hash). Rust rejects bad kinds, ranges, a `src` outside the group, `art` that is not a symbol in the same svg, a brush on a non-group and a group that is more than one of blend, repeat or brush.
- **Kinds:** Calligraphic sweeps an elliptical nib along the flattened path and emits one filled outline, so width follows direction against the nib angle (angle 0: 3 units on a horizontal line, 10 on a vertical one). Scatter uses size, spacing, scatter (perpendicular offset), rotation fixed or random (mulberry32 from the seed, so deterministic) relative to page or path. Art warps the artwork along the spine with Stretch to fit and two flips. Pattern repeats the tile with Stretch (whole tiles) or Add space. Output is capped at 150 generated paths because the API takes 200 commands per batch (Smooth Color blends and tight Scatter hit this).
- **Remove and expand:** Remove brush restores the stroke colour and weight; Expand appearance keeps the generated copies as plain paths and drops the source.
- **Symbol instances:** with the Selection tool a selected `use` shows four corner handles and a round rotate handle (`x-use` role); corner drag scales about the opposite corner (Alt: centre), rotate drag turns about the centre (Shift: 15 degrees), both write a `matrix(...)` transform on the `use`; Size % and Rotation fields do the same; dragging an instance divides the pointer delta by its matrix so it follows the pointer. The master is untouched, so recoloring or editing it still updates every instance.
- **Multi-blend:** `data-fw-blend` gained `mid` (up to 14 ids) and `spine`; 2 to 16 paths blend in stacking order, pair by pair, with the same mode, steps and distance. Replace spine moves a selected extra path into the group and displaces each generated step by how far the spine point sits from the straight chord; Align to Path also turns steps to the spine tangent. The sources do not move onto the spine ends (a difference from Illustrator). Release and Expand work on the new form (Release of three paths returned three).
- **Strings:** 50 keys appended to `i18n/keys.ts` and all 23 locales; the 31 short labels are translated and the 19 long sentences and tooltips stay in English, as in v0.8.1.
- **Checked:** `tsc --noEmit`, `vite build` into a private outDir, `cargo build --release`, `python3 setup/i18n_check.py` (1390 keys, 23 languages), parity JSON loads (115 rows, ids unique).

## Image Trace · v0.8.2 (2026-10-16)

`vector/trace.ts` (tracer), `vector/traceworker.ts` (module worker), `vector/tracepanel.ts` (panel section and `runTrace`), `vector/panel2.ts` and `vector/index.ts` (registration, command `vector.trace`, key Mod+Alt+Shift+T), `vector/fit.ts` (`fitCurve` takes optional end tangents), `styles/vector.css`, `i18n` (41 keys and 23 locales, plus 27 untranslated vector entries per locale translated); parity row FW-157.
- **Pipeline:** the image is read through a canvas and downscaled so its longer side is at most 1024 px; a cross-origin image that taints the canvas gives a message asking for an upload as an asset. Pixels are quantized (Black and White threshold, Grayscale levels, Color by median cut refined with k-means), small regions are merged into their neighbour by Noise, then each color layer is traced along pixel edges, split at corners by turn angle and fitted with `fit.ts`. Holes are separate loops, so paths use fill-rule evenodd.
- **Options:** Paths, Corners, Noise, Ignore White, Method (Abutting masks a label, Overlapping stacks cumulative masks) and output per color (one compound path) or per region. Caps keep each path under the engine data limit and the shape count bounded; a capped result says so.
- **Panel:** progress bar and status line are always present and the buttons are disabled rather than hidden, so nothing shifts while a trace runs. The worker reports progress and Stop terminates it.
- **Driven:** headless Chromium against the real engine and the built editor; Black and White, Grayscale and Color traces of a test image each placed an svg that was read back from `GET /api/state` and screenshotted.
- **Fix found while driving:** straight edges came out as bulges because two-point runs were fitted with zero tangents; `fitCurve` now accepts end tangents and collinear runs become lines.
- **Checked:** `tsc --noEmit`, `vite build` into a private outDir, `python3 setup/i18n_check.py`, parity JSON parse.

## CMS item saves and step titles · v0.8.2 (2026-10-16)

`main.ts` (CMS dialog: `cmsNew`, `fillCmsItem`, `saveCmsItem`), `features/wishlist/quality.ts` (Steps tab), `i18n` (5 keys and 23 locales); native `formweb-model/src/advanced.rs`, `formweb-engine/src/wishlist.rs`, `formweb-export/src/wishlist.rs` and `lib.rs` (one call). Driven in headless Chromium against the real engine and read back from `GET /api/state`.
- **CMS dialog overwrote one item:** after Save the dialog kept the saved item selected and kept its id in the Item ID field, so the next Save sent the same id and `cms.upsert` replaced that record. The only way to start a new record was to pick "New item" in the select, which nothing suggested. Fix: a New item button clears the selection and fields and focuses Slug; Save uses the selected item's id (the Item ID field is read-only while an item is selected), a new item gets a fresh id, and a custom id that is already taken is refused with a message instead of replacing the record. After a save the select follows the saved record (options are updated in place by `keyed`; the select node and existing options were not rebuilt). Driven: three saves with New item between each gave three records, editing the second changed only it, picking the first and saving changed only it.
- **Step titles:** the Steps tab has a Step titles section once a form is grouped (one input per step, placeholder "Step n"); a change sends `form.steps` with `titles` (one per step, in step order; empty clears). Grouping no longer stores "Step n" as a title, so an untitled step stays untitled and falls back to "Step n". The engine and the model share `advanced::step_title`: 1 to 60 characters, no leading, trailing or doubled spaces, no control characters and none of `< > & "`; the model also rejects a title on a node without `data-fw-step`, so `node.update` cannot bypass it. Export writes each step's heading as `h3.fw-step-heading` (title or "Step n"), and the progress label reads "Step 1 of N: title" in the page and at runtime. Driven: titles set in the editor, an invalid title reverted in the input and refused by the API, a cleared title falling back to "Step 2", published page showed the headings and label and Next moved to step 2.
- **Source note:** `quality.ts` line 51 builds the title check with `new RegExp(...)` whose string holds the raw characters U+2028 and U+2029 (an editing tool wrote them); they are valid there and duplicate the `\p{Zl}\p{Zp}` classes.
- **Checked:** `tsc --noEmit`, `vite build` into a private outDir, `cargo build --release`, `python3 setup/i18n_check.py`.

## Wishlist QA · v0.8.1 (2026-10-16)

`features/wishlist/cms.ts`, `structure.ts`, `quality.ts`, `styles/wishlist.css`, `features/authoring.ts` (one description); native `formweb-export` (`cms.rs`, `lib.rs`, `advanced.rs`) and `formweb-model` (`advanced.rs`).
- **Driven in the real editor** (Chromium against the built editor and the running service, no mocked context): a Catalog collection made in the CMS dialog, a bound collection list and a form on the home page; every tab was then used through its `wishlist.*` command and checked in `GET /api/state` and screenshots, then the site was published with `POST /api/publish` and the published pages were opened and exercised.
- **Collections:** Duplicate page sent the typed path as the new page slug without a leading slash, so the duplicate got a slug the other pages never have and its template route became `new/catalog/{item.slug}` with no leading slash. The panel now sends `/path`, rejects characters outside letters, numbers, dash, underscore and slash, and refuses a path another page already uses.
- **Type:** Superscript and Subscript dropped a baseline shift set earlier; they now pass the existing numeric shift back with the mode. New section "Mark words in a text element": type words that occur in the selected paragraph or heading and Mark superscript or Mark subscript splits the element into a before span, a sup or sub for the words and an after span in one batch (one undo step), then selects the marked element. Before this a span could only be made by changing a whole element's tag, so a word inside a sentence could not be raised or lowered.
- **Fields:** the panel had presets and file rules but no way to give a field a custom pattern or mark it required. Each non-file row now has a Pattern input (no groups or alternation, the same limit the server enforces; a rejected value is reset) and a Required checkbox, both saved in the form constraints. CSS lets a row wrap in the 400 px panel.
- **Export:** the exporter wrote every constraint key as an HTML attribute, so `preset="phone"` and `maxSize="512000"` appeared on inputs. They are now kept in the page config only (the runtime and the server already read them there); `pattern`, `required`, `accept`, `min`, `max`, `minlength` and `maxlength` still become attributes.
- **Nested lists:** a `cms.query` filter value of `{parent.id}` or `{parent.field}` (operators eq, ne, contains) inside a list that sits in another list's item is replaced by that field of the enclosing item at export time; outside a list it matches nothing. The model validates the token, so the Studio "Collection list query" form accepts it. Driven: Makers with a `maker` reference on Catalog items, a products list inside each maker card, the published page showed each maker's own products only, and the filter was re-saved from the Studio form in the editor.
- **Checked:** `tsc --noEmit`, Vite build into a private outDir, `cargo build --release`, i18n check (14 strings added to keys and 23 locales). Published output run in Chromium: all four filter kinds, sort, pagination, URL restore, slider next, previous, dots, End, Home and no autoplay under reduced motion, sup and flex computed styles, table markup, duplicate page routes, a two-step form (required, email and phone blocked on step 1, back, pattern, file errors shown) and a live POST through the service: a PDF was stored under `form-uploads` and listed in the inbox, while an exe, an svg, a 600 KB file against a 500 KB rule, a wrong-content PDF, a bad phone and a bad code were rejected with field errors.
- **Not done:** step titles stay "Step n"; Collections folders do not change exported routes; Table row and column editing needs a cell or row selected (a selected table only reports its size).

## Vector live objects and symbols · v0.8.1 (2026-10-16)

New `vector/live.ts`, `vector/freetransform.ts`, `vector/symbols.ts`; edits in `vector/editor.ts`, `vector/extras.ts`, `vector/ops.ts`, `vector/panel2.ts`, `vector/index.ts`, `vector/store.ts`, `vector/arrange.ts`, `vector/distort.ts`, `styles/vector.css`, `ui/canvas.ts` (the tag and `vectorTags` lines), `native/crates/formweb-model/src/vector.rs` and `lib.rs` (tag list), `backend/app/assets.py` (allowlist lines and the `href` rule), i18n (48 keys). Sources: `research/illustrator_parity.md` and `research/illustrator_verification.md` items 7 and 8. Every item was driven in headless Chromium against the real engine (panel buttons, pointer drags, held modifiers) and read back from `GET /api/state` and screenshots.
- **Live blend:** Blend now wraps the two source paths in a `g` carrying `data-fw-blend` (JSON: v, a, b, mode color | steps | distance, steps, distance, orient, sig) and one generated child group `data-fw-live="blend"` holding the intermediate paths. Rust validates both: exactly 8 keys, ids that are `ref_id` and name two non-generated path children of the same group, steps 1 to 100, distance 0.5 to 10000, one generated group per live group, generated groups hold only paths. `sig` is an FNV hash of the spec and both sources' attributes; `liveFixes` runs inside `Editor.commit` (and `reconcileLive` after any outside change, so an MCP edit of a source also regenerates) and rewrites the copies in place when the sig no longer matches, so a drag, a fill change or a panel setting never fights the generated paths. Smooth Color picks the step count from the largest RGB channel difference (capped at 100), Specified Distance from the distance between the source centres divided by the spacing. Clicking a generated copy selects its sources; double-click enters the group (isolation) to edit one source. Expand moves the copies out as plain paths and drops the attributes; Release removes the copies and returns the sources to the parent; Ungroup on a live group releases it. Driven: create, steps 5 to 3 to 4, Smooth Color, Specified Distance 40 (15 steps), fill change, source move (isolation), external API fill change regenerated 7 copies, Expand, Release, undo of each.
- **Align to Path not built:** `orient` accepts only `page`. A spine path (Replace Spine) does not exist, so there is nothing to orient to; Align to Page is the only behavior and it is what a straight blend does.
- **Live repeat:** `data-fw-repeat` (JSON: v, kind radial | grid | mirror, src list of up to 16 path ids, count 2 to 120, radius, cols, rows, gx, gy, angle -360 to 360, brick none | row | column, offset, sig; exactly 13 keys) and the same generated child group. Radial: count, radius and angle are the pivot distance and the direction of the pivot from the source centre (90 is below), so the ring stays complete. Grid: rows, columns, gaps, and Offset rows or Offset columns shift alternate rows by half a cell width or alternate columns by half a cell height. Mirror: axis angle (90 is a vertical axis, 0 horizontal) and an offset from the box edge along the axis normal. The generated copy limit is 800 paths. Panel controls update the selected live repeat; Expand and Release as for blends. Driven: radial 6 then 8 copies, radius 120 and angle 0, grid 3 by 3 with Offset rows (8 copies), mirror at 45 degrees with offset 10, source fill change followed by all copies, Expand, undo, Release. The older Blend and Repeat expanded-copy functions in `arrange.ts` are no longer called from the panel.
- **Free Transform:** Transform tool (E) gained a mode strip (Scale and rotate, Shear, Perspective, Free Distort) and held-key shortcuts: Ctrl on a side handle shears, Ctrl on a corner is Free Distort, Ctrl+Alt+Shift on a corner is Perspective, Alt on Free Distort mirrors the opposite corner. Shear is a skew matrix about the opposite edge (about the centre with Alt). Perspective moves a corner and its mate together along one axis; Free Distort moves one corner. Both map the path through a homography fitted to the dragged quad, falling back to bilinear when the quad is concave or the denominator goes non-positive; curves are split into up to 48 pieces so the result stays smooth, and Live Corners are baked first. Constrain: Shift snaps a shear to 15 degree steps and a Free Distort corner to one axis. The v0.7.1 double-matrix defect stays fixed: quad results go through `Host.clearDrafts` and `updateGeometry` only; a plain scale drag of 30 pixels grew a 91.93 unit rect to exactly 136.92 (one application). Driven on a rectangle (shear by button and by Ctrl, perspective narrowing the top edge, distort) and an ellipse (perspective and distort keep four smooth curve segments).
- **Symbols:** `symbol` inside a `defs` and `use` with `href="#id"`, `x`, `y`, `transform` (and `width`, `height`) are in the Rust, `canvas.ts` and `assets.py` allowlists. Rust: a symbol id must be a `ref_id`, `href` on `use` must be `#` plus a `ref_id`, a `use` must point at a symbol defined in the same svg, a symbol cannot hold a `use` (no recursion), and a `use` without `href` is rejected. `assets.py` keeps `href` only when it is `#id` for a definition in the same document and drops `xlink:href`. Driven against the engine: external URL, javascript, `#nope`, a path target, a cross-svg use, `use` inside a symbol, a missing href, `xlink:href`, `onclick` and a bad `transform` were each refused with 400; a valid `transform`, `x` and `y` were accepted; export carried `<symbol id>` and `<use href>`. Panel: Define symbol (moves the selected paths into a new symbol in the svg's `defs` and leaves one instance in place), place another instance, Edit this symbol (Direct Selection on the master paths, Escape or Done editing leaves; every instance updates because it is the same node tree), Replace the selected instance with a symbol, Break link (expands the instance to a group of path copies with the `x`, `y` and transform baked in) and a list of the svg's symbols. Dragging an instance with the Select tool writes `x` and `y`. Driven: define, place, move an instance (x 105.93 to 135.91, y 0 to 59.96), recolor the master (both instances followed in the canvas), drag a master anchor, define a second symbol, replace an instance, break a link.
- **Limits:** symbols are per svg, not per page; instances cannot be scaled or rotated on canvas (their `transform` can be set through the engine and Break link honours it); Smooth Color is capped at 100 steps (Illustrator allows 255); live blend needs both sources in the same group; the panel's Blend and Repeat creation uses the current selection order of paths in the svg.
- **Strings:** 48 keys appended to `i18n/keys.ts` and all 23 locales; about 21 short labels are translated per language, the 27 long sentences and tooltips stay in English.
- **Checked:** `tsc --noEmit` (only an existing error in `features/wishlist/quality.ts`, not this change), `vite build` into a private outDir, `cargo build --release`, `python3 setup/i18n_check.py` (1280 keys, 23 languages), `compileall` on `assets.py` (pycache removed), parity JSON loads.

## Vector Phase 9 A · v0.8.0 (2026-10-16)

`vector/corners.ts`, `vector/boolean.ts`, `vector/cut.ts`, `vector/extras.ts`, `vector/editor.ts` (Pathfinder runner, corner drag), `vector/panel.ts`, `vector/panel2.ts`, `vector/icons.ts`. Sources: `research/illustrator_verification.md` items 1 to 4. Each item was driven in headless Chromium against the real engine and read back from `GET /api/state`.
- **Relative corner rounding is by angle:** `corners.ts` now stores, in Relative mode, the radius a 90 degree corner would get. The effective radius is `value * tan(theta / 2)` with theta the interior angle between the two adjacent tangents, so a 90 degree corner gets exactly the value, sharper corners get smaller radii and wider corners larger ones, still clamped to the geometric maximum. Absolute is unchanged. For a round or inverted corner the tangent distance from the vertex is therefore `value` at every angle. Driven on a 66, 45 and 69 degree triangle: Absolute 20 gave tangent distances of about 30 and 48 (20 / tan(theta / 2)), Relative 20 gave 20.0 at each corner. The old meaning (percent of half the shorter adjacent segment) is gone: an existing document with a Relative corner keeps its stored number but now reads it as the 90 degree radius, so a stored 50 is a radius of 50 at a right angle instead of 50 percent of half a segment. The panel no longer forces 50 when switching to Relative; it keeps the current number. The only single source for the definition is CreativePro (angle dependent); the tan(theta / 2) scale is our reading of it, not an Adobe formula.
- **Corner widget:** Alt-click cycled Round, Inverted Round, Chamfer, Round, Inverted Round on four clicks. The widget turned red (`data-limit="true"`, `--danger`) when dragged past the clamp; that already worked and was left as is.
- **Pathfinder styles:** Unite, Intersect and Exclude take the front object's paint (fill and stroke), Minus Front the rear object's, Minus Back the front object's, Divide keeps each piece's own fill, Trim and Merge and Crop drop strokes, Merge joins same-fill pieces (two red shapes plus one blue gave one red path and one blue path; Trim kept two red paths), Crop keeps the parts of the underlying objects inside the front one. Most of this was already right; the missing tenth button was added: Outline (`boolean.ts` `outlineSegments`) splits each object's boundary at the intersections with the other objects and emits every segment as an unfilled path whose stroke is the original fill (the stroke weight is the original weight, or 1 when the original had no stroke). Verified on three overlapping shapes with different fills and strokes (rect, ellipse, rect: 12 segments). The Pathfinder Options dialog is still not built (its contents were not verified).
- **Shape Builder:** dragging merges the touched regions and the result takes the style of the topmost object under the region where the drag started (z order, not selection order). Alt-click on a region deletes it and splits the overlapped objects; Alt-drag now deletes every region it crosses (it only deleted the first before). Shift+M selects the tool. Gap detection (`Shape Builder` panel section, `cut.ts` `gapInputs`): Off, 3, 6, 12 or Custom points; open paths whose end points chain together within the gap, even across objects, become one closed region. In the test the gap was 4.2 units: 3 left two triangles, 6 gave one square. Option "Click stroke splits the path" (`cut.ts` `strokeSplit`): clicking a stroke splits it at its crossings with the other selected paths into separate paths (one line across a rectangle gave left stub, middle and right stub). Split pieces are refitted with the curve fitter only when the source had handles, so a split curve is an approximation within 0.15 units. Pick Color From Artwork or swatches and Consider Open Filled Path as Closed are not built.
- **Strings:** five appended to the end of `i18n/keys.ts` and the 23 full locales: Outline, Gap detection (pt), Off, Custom, Click stroke splits the path.

## Vector Phase 9 B · v0.8.0 (2026-10-16)

`vector/editor.ts`, `vector/index.ts`, `vector/edit.ts`, `vector/extras.ts`, `vector/ops.ts`, `vector/panel2.ts`, `vector/arrange.ts`, `vector/store.ts`, `vector/stroke.ts`, `vector/widthprofile.ts`, new `vector/widthdialog.ts` and `vector/typepath.ts`, `styles/vector.css`, `ui/canvas.ts` (one allowlist line), `native/crates/formweb-model/src/vector.rs`, `backend/app/assets.py` (one allowlist line), i18n (28 keys). Every item below was driven in headless Chromium against the real engine (pointer drags, panel controls, key chords) and checked in `GET /api/state` and screenshots.
- **Join and Average:** Join `Mod+Alt+Shift+J` and Average `Mod+Alt+Shift+A` already existed; new `vector.path.join-average` (Join and average end anchors) is `Mod+Alt+Shift+K`, the next free chord (no registered key, terminal key or vector key uses it). Average now takes the mean over every selected anchor across paths (it needed two anchors on one path before); Join plus Average moves the two end anchors to their midpoint and merges them into one corner anchor, across two paths or on one path. Smooth join is not offered: the merged anchor is always a corner.
- **Pen modifiers:** Shift on a new point already snapped to 45 degrees from the last point; Shift while dragging out handles now snaps the handle to 45 degree steps around the anchor. Alt while dragging a just-placed anchor's handle already moved only the outgoing handle; confirmed (the incoming handle keeps its mirrored position and the anchor becomes a corner). Alt-click on an anchor of the selected path with the Pen tool retracts both handles (converts to corner); Alt-drag from a corner pulls out two equal handles (the Convert gesture).
- **Width tool:** Shift+W was already bound. A width point is now `[t, side1, side2]` in `data-fw-width` (older `[t, total]` points read as two equal halves, so saved sites keep working). Alt-drag changes only the side the pointer is on; plain drag sets both sides; Alt-click without dragging still removes a point. Double-click a point opens a dialog (Side 1, Side 2, Total width, Adjust adjoining width points; Total scales both sides, the checkbox scales the neighbouring points by the same ratios). Saved profiles live in `localStorage` key `formweb_width_profiles` per viewer (wrapped in try/catch, 12 slots, names up to 40 characters), stored as side widths divided by the stroke weight so they rescale on reuse; they are not site data because a profile is a reusable tool setting, like Illustrator's. Flip profile reverses along the path and keeps the sides.
- **Type on a path:** effects Rainbow (default), Skew, 3D Ribbon, Stair Step, Gravity; align Ascender, Descender, Center, Baseline (default); Spacing; Flip. A type run is a `g` with `data-fw-type` (JSON: v, path id, text, size, offset, effect, flip, align, spacing). Rainbow is a real `text` with one `textPath` (`startOffset`, `letter-spacing`, align through `dy`). Chromium and the allowlist have no `side="right"`, so Flip adds a hidden reversed copy of the path ("Type guide") and points the textPath at it. Skew, 3D Ribbon, Stair Step and Gravity lay out one `text` per glyph with a `matrix(...)` transform and a `textPath` on a shared horizontal carrier path, because the engine allows text only inside `textPath` and `canvas.ts` only renders text through `tspan`; glyph widths are measured with a canvas 2D context (no kerning in glyph modes, 80 glyph limit). Skew keeps verticals vertical and slopes the baseline with the tangent, 3D Ribbon rotates with the tangent and foreshortens and shears with the tangent change (baseline locked), Stair Step stays upright, Gravity points each glyph's up axis away from the path's centroid (Flip points it toward it). The round bracket at the text centre (Select and Direct tools) drags along the path to move the text and across the path to flip it. `typeFixes` rebuilds glyph and flipped runs when the path's geometry is edited and removes the run when the path is deleted. Group `g` with `data-fw-type` is skipped by `pathIds`, so guides never appear as selectable shapes. New attributes: `dy` (text, textPath; canvas.ts, vector.rs, assets.py), `data-fw-type` and `data-fw-mask` (vector.rs only, like the other `data-fw-*` model attributes; canvas.ts skips `data-fw-*` on render). Validation in `vector.rs`: `data-fw-type` must be a nine-key object with enum, range and id checks; `data-fw-mask` is one of four words; `dy` is a number within 2000; `data-fw-width` must be an array of two strings then points of two or three finite numbers.
- **Opacity masks:** Make mask now writes a "Mask background" path first in the `mask` (black when Clip is on, white when off) and `data-fw-mask` on the masked group (`clip`, `plain`, `invert`, `clip-invert`; a mask without it reads as `clip`). Clip and Invert Mask checkboxes in Group, clip and mask apply to the selected masked group after creation, or set the defaults for the next mask when none is selected. Invert complements the mask shape's solid fill and stroke and flips the background; non-solid colors refuse with a message. Release restores the shape (un-inverted) and skips the background. Rendered check at 20 percent luminosity: clip on hides the outside, clip off shows it, invert flips the inside to 80 percent.
- **Strings:** 28 keys appended to `i18n/keys.ts` and every full locale (ten more strings reuse the formvis tables: OK, Cancel, Spacing, Center, Size, Text, Flip, Effect, Clip, Skew).
- **Checked:** `tsc --noEmit`, `vite build`, `python3 setup/i18n_check.py` (1232 keys, 23 languages), `cargo build --release`, `compileall` on `assets.py` (pycache removed).

## Vector QA · v0.7.1 (2026-10-16)

`vector/stroke.ts`, `vector/widthprofile.ts`, `vector/extras.ts`, `vector/editor.ts`, `vector/host.ts`, `vector/index.ts`. Every tool below was driven in headless Chromium against the real engine (pointer drags on the overlay, panel buttons, registered commands and key chords), checked in `GET /api/state` and in a screenshot.
- **Key bindings:** `vector.path.join` is `Mod+Alt+Shift+J` (`Mod+Alt+J` is `chat.open`, `Mod+Shift+J` is `claude.open`, `Mod+J` is `terminal.toggle`); `vector.path.average` is `Mod+Alt+Shift+A`. No other registered key uses either. Both fired from the keyboard and ran.
- **Blocking bug outside this scope:** `ui/canvas.ts` lowercases the tag (`allowedTags.has(data.tag.toLowerCase())`), so `clipPath`, `linearGradient`, `radialGradient` and `textPath` are not in the sets and render as HTML `div`s. Stroke inside, clipping masks, opacity masks, gradients and type on a path therefore showed nothing in the real canvas. With the tag kept in its own case (and `node.localName !== tag` in the replace test) all of them render. Verified in a scratch build only; the fix belongs to the `canvas.ts` owner.
- **Driven, works:** opacity mask (luminosity, half grey gave half red), clip make and release, group and ungroup, isolation (double-click enters, dashed frame, Escape leaves), knife (drag and Alt straight line), eraser (filled and open paths), Shape Builder (hover, drag merge), stroke align inside, center and outside (filled and unfilled), align and distribute for objects (Selection, Key object, Artboard) and for anchors, distribute with a set spacing, smart guides and snapping (x guide snap, Mod+Shift+; toggle), transform panel (rotate, scale, reflect, shear, move, Copy), Transform Each, blend steps, repeat (radial, grid, mirror both axes), warp presets (arc, bulge, flag, wave), distort (pucker and bloat, roughen, zig zag, twist), type on a path, gradient create, stop drag, click to add a stop, Alt-click to remove, end handle drag, join and average.
- **Fixed:** (1) Width profiles: a second preset never applied, because `widthFixes` re-wrote `data-fw-width` from the old points on every command that also set stroke; it now skips patches that carry `data-fw-width`. (2) Width outlines were sampled only at the flattened anchors, so a straight line ignored every interior point (bulge, taper both and wide ends looked uniform or vanished); `variableOutline` now densifies by arc length first. (3) Free Transform applied its matrix twice (the drag preview wrote a draft and `matrixCommands` then read the draft); `Host.clearDrafts` is called before the commands are built. (4) Join now merges the end anchors of two different paths in the same svg (before, any selection spanning two paths refused with the one-path message); a shape with no picked anchors is skipped.
- **Notes, not bugs:** dragging the first or last gradient stop moves the end handle that sits on it (use a middle stop for offsets); releasing a clip returns the shape as a grey outline, since the clip stores no paint; "artboard" for align is the svg viewBox; the first rect drawn leaves a viewBox whose corner is also a snap point.
- **Checked:** `tsc --noEmit` and `vite build` pass; no page errors in any run.

## Canvas SVG tag case · v0.7.1 (2026-10-16)

`ui/canvas.ts` render: the tag is kept as written when the allowlist has it, and lowercased only as a fallback; the replace test compares `localName`. Before this, `clipPath`, `linearGradient`, `radialGradient` and `textPath` were lowercased, missed the camelCase allowlist and rendered as divs, so gradients, clip and opacity masks, inside strokes and type on a path showed nothing in the editor (export was unaffected). Checked in headless Chromium against the real build: a gradient with stop edits and a text on a curved path render.

## Canvas keyboard forwarding · v0.7.1 (2026-10-16)

`ui/canvas.ts` only (one keydown listener in `bindEvents`).
- **Forwarding:** a keydown inside the canvas iframe document is re-dispatched as a synthetic `KeyboardEvent` (key, code, location, repeat and the four modifiers; bubbles, cancelable, composed) on the iframe element in the top document. Both the vector editor's capture listener and the keymap router on `document` therefore see it exactly once, with the iframe as target (not a typing target). If either calls `preventDefault`, `dispatchEvent` returns false and the original event is prevented too (no browser default such as find or save).
- **Not forwarded:** events whose target is an input, textarea, select or `contenteditable` element, and composing events, so inline text editing keeps its own keys.
- **No keymap change:** the target is the iframe element rather than the document so `target.closest` in the vector handler stays valid.
- **Checked:** headless Chromium against the real engine (port 8241): after clicking an element in the canvas (focus on the iframe) Delete removed it (29 to 28 nodes), Mod+Z restored it (29), Mod+K opened command search; during inline text editing, typing, Delete, Backspace and Mod+Z changed only the text and the node count stayed 29.

## Interface languages · v0.7.0 (2026-10-16)

`i18n/index.ts`, `i18n/langs.ts`, `i18n/keys.ts`, `i18n/locales/*`, `i18n/family/*` (new); `main.ts`; `setup/i18n_check.py` and `./formweb i18n check`.
- **Languages:** 26, English the source and default: en, es, es-MX, pt-BR, fr, fr-CA, de, it, nl, da, fi, nb, sv, ru, pl, cs, hu, uk, tr, zh-CN, zh-TW, ja, ko, id, th, vi (the formvis set and codes). es-MX and fr-CA keep only differing words and fall back to es and fr, then English.
- **Tables, three layers per language, later wins:** `family/extra`, `family/more` and `family/<code>.ts` are formvis's tables copied unchanged (same owner, Apache-2.0; objects keyed by the English), then formweb's own `locales/<code>.ts`. A formweb locale is an ordered array, one entry per `keys.ts` entry (1199); `asTable()` zips it with `KEYS`. Regional locales are objects keyed by the English. New strings are appended to the end of `keys.ts`; a locale shorter than the keys shows English for the tail, and `./formweb i18n check` reports the gap. Each language loads lazily through dynamic import; English loads nothing.
- **Translator:** the formvis page translator: text nodes and title, aria-label and placeholder translated by exact English match from a MutationObserver (before paint, so English never flashes), each node remembering its English so switching back or to another language restores the right text. Trailing "..." and ":" share the bare entry. Never touched: inputs' values, iframes (canvas, preview), code, `[data-no-i18n]`, the terminal, the chat log, layer and page lists, crumbs, the inspector name, the project name, the frame label and the stage page name.
- **Start and switch:** `main.ts` awaits `startLanguage()` (top-level await) before building the UI, starts the translator after `menubar.build()`, and Settings has an Editor language select (native names, `data-no-i18n`). `setLanguage` stores `formweb_language` in localStorage and relabels live with no reload.
- **Checked:** `./formweb i18n check` passes: 23 full languages with 1199 entries each (11 to 55 left identical, mostly product names, key names and code tokens) and 33 es-MX and 24 fr-CA overrides. In headless Chromium the menus switched live to German and Japanese without a reload, Japanese persisted across reload, and layer names stayed as written. Known weak spots: a few terms are debatable (for example ja "Row rev." rendered as revision, nl "Beide taps", fr "Onglet" for Miter); the zh, ja, ko, id, th, vi, tr tables were written without the family glossary.

## Vector parity · v0.7.0 (2026-10-16)

`vector/` gains `host.ts`, `gradient.ts`, `widthprofile.ts`, `stroke.ts`, `distort.ts`, `arrange.ts`, `cut.ts`, `snap.ts`, `ops.ts`, `extras.ts`, `panel2.ts`; edits in `editor.ts`, `store.ts`, `overlay.ts`, `panel.ts`, `index.ts`, `icons.ts`, `boolean.ts`, `svg.ts`, `styles/vector.css`, `ui/canvas.ts`, `native/crates/formweb-model/src/vector.rs`, `lib.rs` (tag whitelist and `linked_attrs`), `backend/app/assets.py`; new `backend/app/vector_routes.py`. Parity rows FW-080 to FW-096.
- **Allowlists:** svg children may now be defs-type nodes (linearGradient, radialGradient, stop, clipPath, mask) plus text and textPath. `url(#id)` is accepted only on fill, stroke, clip-path and mask, only for ids defined in the same svg, and a textPath href must name a path in the same svg. Rust, `canvas.ts` and `assets.py` enforce this independently and must stay in step. The export crate was not edited; the generic node renderer emitted the gradient defs.
- **Storage:** gradients are userSpaceOnUse defs that follow moves and transforms. Stroke align stores `data-fw-align` and `data-fw-weight` (logical weight); inside and outside use an owned `fwa-` clipPath kept in sync by `alignFixes`. Width profiles store `data-fw-width` and a generated filled outline path kept in sync by `widthFixes`.
- **Tools and commands:** Knife K, Eraser Shift+E, Shape Builder Shift+M, Width Shift+W, Gradient G, Transform E, handled in the vector module with `preventDefault`. Group Mod+G, ungroup Mod+Shift+G, clip Mod+7 and Mod+Alt+7, opacity mask Mod+Alt+M, blend Mod+Alt+B, snap Mod+Shift+;.
- **Backend route:** register `backend/app/vector_routes.py` in `main.py` with `from .vector_routes import router as vector_router` and `app.include_router(vector_router)` beside the other include_router lines. Not registered and not called yet.
- **Checked:** `cargo build --release`, `tsc --noEmit`, `compileall` pass. Headless Chromium against the real engine: group and ungroup, clip make and release, align refusal on one object, gradient create, export carrying linearGradient, and a hostile `url(#nope)` fill rejected by the engine.
- **Unverified [K]:** the Relative corners definition; Knife, Eraser, Shape Builder, Width, opacity mask, isolation, Free Transform, snapping guides, Blend, Repeat, warp and distort and type on a path were compiled but not driven in a browser; stroke align was not compared pixel for pixel.
- **Open limits:** Free Transform has no shear or perspective; closed paths in Width give two rings; Blend and Repeat are expanded copies; four warp presets; opacity masks are luminosity only; type on a path has no on-canvas editing.

## Command registry · v0.7.0 (2026-10-16)

`commands/registry.ts`, `commands/keymap.ts`, `commands/catalogue.ts`, `styles/commands.css` (new); edits in `ui/shell.ts`, `main.ts`, `features/terminal.ts`.
- **Registry:** one `Commands` singleton (`commands`) holds every command: id, label (string or thunk), `keys` in `Mod+Shift+S` form, `enabled`, `checked`, `menu`, `sep` (menu separator bucket), `group`, `description`, `params`, `mutates`, `risky`. `add` throws on a duplicate id; a key combo already taken is dropped from the later command (first registration wins). Combos are canonical (`Alt+Shift+Mod+Key`, read from `event.code`, so layout independent); Mod is Cmd on macOS and Ctrl elsewhere, and `formatKeys` draws glyphs on macOS and `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+K` elsewhere. User rebinding (`setKeys`, `resetKeys`) is saved in `localStorage` `formweb_shortcuts` (try/catch). `run` goes through the runner set by `setRunner` (main's `run`, so errors reach the status bar); `call` awaits the command and returns its result for the agent bridge.
- **Shell:** `CommandDef`, `registerCommands`, `commandList`, `runCommand`, `setRunner`, `Menubar`, `CommandSearch`, `exposeToAgents` and `BRAND_MARK` keep their names. `registerCommands` converts legacy key text (`'Ctrl S'`) to bindings, maps a numeric `group` to `sep`, and marks the vector module's bare-letter keys `passive` (the vector editor handles those itself, because its tool keys depend on the selection). Menus are found by `data-menu` instead of `textContent`; rows and search results read label and keys from the registry and rewrite their text in place when a menu opens.
- **Keys:** one capture listener on `document` (`installKeymap`, installed after the vector editor so its handler runs first and a key it consumed is skipped through `defaultPrevented`). It ignores input, textarea, select, contenteditable, `.xterm` and `.fw-term` targets, so single keys never fire while typing; a command with `global: true` (save, command search, panel toggles, dock keys) still fires on a Mod combo there. Auto-repeat is allowed for undo and redo only. The three Ctrl+Z paths, Ctrl+S, Delete, Ctrl+K, the bracket and backtick listener and the dock's own Mod+J listener are gone; Escape and the dialog Tab trap stay in main (dialog focus code). `edit.redo` also answers Mod+Y.
- **Buttons:** toolbar, title-bar and top-bar buttons (undo, redo, preview, refresh preview, publish, theme, settings, the three breakpoints, Export, Audit) carry `data-cmd` and one delegated click handler runs `runCommand`.
- **Catalogue and bridge:** `catalogue.ts` serializes the registry (id, label, keys, group, menu, description, when, params, mutates, risky, enabled, checked; words for most ids in `META`) and posts it to `/api/commands/catalogue` 400 ms after a change, a state update or a focus. The window long-polls `/api/commands/bridge`, runs each request with `commands.call` and answers on `/api/commands/bridge/result`. A risky command shows a small fixed confirm (`.fw-agent-confirm`, `--z-toast`, built once, toggled with `hidden`) with Allow and Deny; no answer in 45 s denies.
- **Inspector:** the HTML tag select now lists svg, path and the other SVG tags for those nodes, as a single disabled option, so a div cannot be turned into a path and the field is no longer blank; an HTML tag outside the list (for example aside) is prepended so it shows.
- **Dock after reload:** `TerminalDock.remount()` runs once the engine answers, so the tabs and status line come back even when the dock is collapsed (the list was fetched only on first open, leaving "No session"). The list request is one shared promise, so a quick open cannot start a second shell while the first fetch is in flight.
- **Checked:** Chromium against the built editor: Mod+K and Enter in the search switched to Mobile; a View menu row ran; Mod+Z, Mod+Shift+Z, Delete and Mod+S ran from the body; typing "vapmlc", Delete and Backspace in the Name field changed nothing else; the same keys inside the xterm did nothing, while Mod+J, Mod+` and Mod+[ worked there; the tag select showed `path` (disabled) for a path; after reload with the dock collapsed one tab and its status were back; REST and MCP `run_ui_command` ran view.tablet and view.desktop in the open page, and a risky one (agents.toggle) was denied and then allowed from the confirm. Clicking the canvas moves focus into its iframe, where no editor shortcut (old or new) is heard; the iframe was outside this change.

## Wishlist · v0.7.0 (2026-10-16)

`features/wishlist/` (new: `index.ts`, `kit.ts`, `cms.ts`, `structure.ts`, `quality.ts`), `styles/wishlist.css` (new); `ui/canvas.ts` allowlists gain `sup`, `sub`, `tfoot` and `accept`. Not yet wired into `main.ts` (owned by another engineer this wave); the exact integration is in the hand-back.
- **Install:** `installWishlist(ctx)` builds one fixed panel (`wl-panel`, z-index token `--z-popover`) once, with nine tabs (Filters, Slider, Collections, Table, Type, Layout, Steps, Fields, Checks) as persistent `tabpanel` nodes toggled with `hidden`. It returns `{commands, refresh, open}`; the nine `CommandDef`s sit in the Insert, CMS, Page, Edit and Arrange menus.
- **Context:** the module never imports `main.ts`. It takes `state()`, `commands(batch)`, `selected()`, `select(id)`, `notify()` and `audit()` from the caller, so every edit is an ordinary engine batch (one undo step, MCP-equal).
- **DOM stability:** dynamic lists (facets, sort options, folders, item order, step assignment, field rules, check results) use `keyed()`; selects use `sync()` that only rewrites options when they changed; inputs use `setValue()` that skips the focused control. `refresh()` returns at once while the panel is closed.
- **Item order:** rows are `draggable` and also carry Up and Down buttons, both ending in `cms.move`.
- **Checked:** `tsc --noEmit` shows nothing in `features/wishlist`; a scratch Vite bundle with a mocked context was driven in headless Chromium (open, tab keys, add filter emits `cms.list`, move emits `cms.move`, Insert table targets the selected container, Checks lists findings, Escape closes) with no console errors.

## Live agent workspace · v0.6.0 (2026-10-16)

`features/terminal.ts`, `features/chat-pane.ts`, `styles/chat.css`; small edits in `api.ts`, `agent-connect.ts` and `main.ts`.
- **Dock:** the terminal dock is now an agent dock with session tabs (`fw-term-tab`, keyed in a Map, nodes created once). Each shell or Claude Code session has its own xterm in its own pane, switched with `hidden`, so no pane is rebuilt. Fit runs on the active pane through addon-fit and a ResizeObserver; resize frames go to the server only when xterm reports a new size.
- **Sessions:** buttons call `POST /api/terminal/open` (kind shell or claude, optional prompt, resume, preset). On first open the dock re-mounts live sessions from `GET /api/terminal/sessions`, so a reload keeps every tab and the server replays recent output (the terminal is reset first, so a reconnect never doubles the text). A claude request reuses the live Claude tab. Failures (for example `claude` missing) show the server's install text in the note area, which swaps with the stack at the same fixed body height.
- **Agents switch:** the dock no longer waits for it. Agent Connect's Open button is always enabled; the switch now means outside MCP clients only, and the card text says so.
- **Keys:** Mod+J toggles the dock, Shift+Mod+J opens Claude Code, Alt+Mod+J opens Chat (document capture listener on `event.code === 'KeyJ'`, so Alt works on macOS). Keydown inside the dock stops at the dock, so editor shortcuts (undo, delete, save) do not fire while typing; Mod+J and Ctrl+backtick are let through, and xterm is told to ignore them.
- **Chat:** `ChatPane` posts to `/api/chat/send` through the new `stream()` helper in `api.ts` (fetch plus Server-Sent Events, same auth and errors as `request()`). Markdown is parsed to blocks and reconciled by index, so a streaming delta touches only the block that changed. Tool calls are pills with running, done or failed state. Enter sends, Stop calls `/api/chat/cancel`, New chat calls `/api/chat/reset`, and Continue in Claude Code opens the terminal with `--resume` on the same session. State returns from `/api/chat/state` when the tab is reopened. The page and selected element id are sent as context with each message.
- **Title bar and commands:** three `fw-tt-btn` buttons (Terminal, Claude Code, Chat) built by `dockTitleButtons()` and prepended to the title tools; commands `terminal.toggle`, `claude.open`, `chat.open` in `registerCommands`. The buttons show `is-on` for the open dock or the active tab kind.
- **Icons:** `glyph()` in `chat-pane.ts` draws claude, chat, terminal, send and stop as inline SVG with currentColor, in the same wrapper as `icon()`. `ui/icons.ts` was outside this change.

## Vector editing · v0.6.0 (2026-10-16)

`vector/` (new), `styles/vector.css`, small edits in `ui/canvas.ts`; model, export and backend allowlists in the same change.
- **Element model:** an inline vector is an `svg` node (viewBox, width, height; styles position absolute, left, top, overflow visible) holding `path` nodes. Each path has `d` plus `data-fw-vector`, a JSON anchor model `{"v":1,"subs":[{"closed","nodes":[{x,y,in,out,kind,corner}]}]}`. `d` is always the rendered result, so Live Corners are non-destructive: the model keeps the sharp anchors and a per-anchor `corner {type round|inverted|chamfer, radius, mode absolute|relative}`. If an agent edits `d` alone, `readShape()` sees the two disagree and re-reads `d`. The allowlist lives in three places that must stay in step: `formweb-model/src/vector.rs` (tags, per-tag attributes, value grammar, d parser, parent and child rules), `ui/canvas.ts` (`vectorTags`, `vectorAttributes`, svg created with `createElementNS`) and `backend/app/assets.py` (`SVG_ATTRS`, `sanitize_svg`, `import_vector_svg`). Export drops `data-fw-vector` and marks an svg without role or label `aria-hidden`.
- **Geometry:** `geometry.ts` (anchors, d parse and format, split, kind conversion, shape generators, model encode and decode), `corners.ts` (tangent distance d = r / tan(theta / 2), clamped per segment by arc length and by neighbouring corners; cubic arc with k = 4/3 tan(phi / 4); inverted reflects the arc across the chord; chamfer is a line; curved neighbours are split at arc length d and use their own tangents), `edit.ts` (delete, add, scissors, join, average, simplify, offset, outline stroke with caps, joins and dashes), `fit.ts` (curve fitting, from formdeck), `boolean.ts` (flatten, arrange, link loops, from formdeck; the nine Pathfinder operations).
- **Gestures:** one batch per gesture. During a drag only the iframe `d` attribute and the overlay change; pointer-up sends `node.update` or `node.add` commands through `api.commands`, so undo, revision and MCP behave as for any edit.
- **Overlay:** `Overlay` appends one svg to `canvas.shell` (so it scales with the canvas) and keeps pooled elements per kind, updated by attribute only; hidden items use `data-off`. Handle sizes use `--fw-vo-px` so they stay constant on screen. The capture rect is only hit-testable while a tool is active.
- **Panel:** `VectorPanel` builds the tool strip and the Appearance, Corners, Shapes, Pathfinder, Path and SVG sections once; `update()` writes values in place and never rebuilds.
- **Open items:** gradients, clipping masks, stroke align inside and outside, knife, width tool, group nodes in the UI (imported groups are flattened), a backend route for `import_vector_svg` (the import currently parses in the browser with the same allowlist).

## References and Revise in Generate pages · v0.5.7

`features/ai-writer.ts`.
- **References:** Add reference (`ai.site-reference`) opens a hidden file input (PDF and the four raster types). `api.readReference()` posts the bytes to `/api/ai/site/reference`; the result is kept in the dialog only and listed with `keyed()` keyed by a per-reference id from a WeakMap, so the same file added twice gives two removable rows (name, page count or colour swatches, Remove as `ai.site-reference-remove`). At most six. Building clears them.
